/* ══ 性能：毛玻璃半径收敛 ══════════════════════════════════════════════
   背景图层一直在动，而 backdrop-filter 每帧都要按「背后的内容」重算模糊，
   开销 ≈ 模糊面积 × 半径。下面把「数量多」和「面积大」的两类压下来：
    · 卡片系：一层 10+ 张卡，每张卡上还有徽标/心愿按钮/订阅条各一层模糊
     · 全屏浮层：detail / 设置视图 / 各种弹窗
    半径减半后肉眼几乎看不出差别，但每帧要重算的像素少了一大截。
   想要更彻底：设置里关掉「背景动效」即可（那时连图层动画都没有）。 */
.card,.card-panel,.meta-grid,.inject-panel,.sub-bar{
  backdrop-filter:blur(6px);-webkit-backdrop-filter:blur(6px);
}
.card-badge,.card-wish,.card-sublen,.card-panel .btn-secondary{
  backdrop-filter:blur(4px);-webkit-backdrop-filter:blur(4px);
}
.sidebar,.topbar,.detail-top,.genre-pop,.toast,.ui-card,.panel,.renew-card{
  backdrop-filter:blur(12px);-webkit-backdrop-filter:blur(12px);
}
.detail,.ui-view,.ui-pop,.modal,.iosp{
  backdrop-filter:blur(16px);-webkit-backdrop-filter:blur(16px);
}
.gate-ovl,.success-ovl{
  backdrop-filter:blur(18px) saturate(160%);-webkit-backdrop-filter:blur(18px) saturate(160%);
}
.tb-btn,.hero-ctrl,.hero-badge,.btn-secondary,.btn-ghost,.sec-filter,.genre-btn,
.topbar-search input{
  backdrop-filter:blur(10px);-webkit-backdrop-filter:blur(10px);
}

function uiFlagSvg(code){
  var inner = UI_FLAGS[code];
  if (!inner) { return ''; }
  return '<svg class="lang-flag-svg" viewBox="0 0 24 18" aria-hidden="true" ' +
         'preserveAspectRatio="none">' + inner + '</svg>';
}

/* ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   共享 UI 主逻辑
   ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
var uiState = {
  theme: (UI_CFG && UI_CFG.theme) || 'light',
  lang:  (UI_CFG && UI_CFG.lang)  || 'ru',
  systemDark: !!(UI_CFG && UI_CFG.systemDark),
  custom: (UI_CFG && UI_CFG.customThemes) ? UI_CFG.customThemes.slice() : [],
  editorBase: 'light',
  editorPreset: 'l-blue',
  editorColors: null,          /* 完全自定义配色的当前值 */
  editorFx: 'none',
  editorDeco: 'none',
  editorLayout: 'rail-l',
  renewPlan: '90D'
};
/* 旧版本只存了 accent，这里留一个兼容字段 */
Object.defineProperty(uiState, 'editorAccent', {
  get: function(){
    return (this.editorColors && this.editorColors.accent) || '#3b82f6';
  },
  set: function(v){
    if (!this.editorColors) { this.editorColors = {}; }
    this.editorColors.accent = v;
  },
  configurable: true
});



function uiSend(message){
  try { if (window.chrome && window.chrome.webview) window.chrome.webview.postMessage(message); }
  catch (e) { /* host bridge unavailable */ }
}
function uiEsc(value){
  return String(value == null ? '' : value)
    .replace(/&/g,'&amp;').replace(/</g,'&lt;').replace(/>/g,'&gt;')
    .replace(/"/g,'&quot;').replace(/'/g,'&#39;');
}
function uiT(key){
  var table = UI_T[uiState.lang] || UI_T.en;
  return (table && table[key]) || UI_T.en[key] || key;
}
function uiRgba(hex, alpha){
  var h = String(hex || '').replace('#','').trim();
  if (h.length === 3) { h = h.charAt(0)+h.charAt(0)+h.charAt(1)+h.charAt(1)+h.charAt(2)+h.charAt(2); }
  if (h.length !== 6) { return hex; }
  var r = parseInt(h.slice(0,2),16), g = parseInt(h.slice(2,4),16), b = parseInt(h.slice(4,6),16);
  if (!isFinite(r) || !isFinite(g) || !isFinite(b)) { return hex; }
  return 'rgba(' + r + ',' + g + ',' + b + ',' + alpha + ')';
}
function uiRgbTriple(hex){
  var h = String(hex || '').replace('#','').trim();
  if (h.length !== 6) { return '18,20,28'; }
  return parseInt(h.slice(0,2),16) + ',' + parseInt(h.slice(2,4),16) + ',' + parseInt(h.slice(4,6),16);
}


var UI_LOCALES = { en:'en-US', zh:'zh-CN', ru:'ru-RU', de:'de-DE',
                   ja:'ja-JP', es:'es-ES', tr:'tr-TR', pt:'pt-BR' };

function uiPrice(key){
  var prices = (UI_CFG && UI_CFG.prices) || {};
  var value = prices[key];
  return typeof value === 'number' ? value : 0;
}
/* 价格统一美元，不再按界面语言换算币种 */
function uiMoney(amount){
  var value = (typeof amount === 'number' && isFinite(amount)) ? amount : 0;
  return '$' + value.toFixed(2);
}
function uiPaintPrices(){
   /* Renew 页那三个价格槽位不在这里刷：它们跟着“当前点击的游戏”走
     由 uiRenewPaintPrices() 负责（游戏没写价才回退到这里的全局价）。 */
  var slots = [
    ['lP30Price',    uiMoney(uiPrice('d30'))],
    ['lP90Price',    uiMoney(uiPrice('d90'))],
    ['lPltPrice',    uiMoney(uiPrice('life'))],
    ['lP30PPrice',   uiMoney(uiPrice('d30') + uiPrice('pauseAdd'))],
    ['lP90PPrice',   uiMoney(uiPrice('d90') + uiPrice('pauseAdd'))],
    ['lPauseAdd',    '+' + uiMoney(uiPrice('pauseAdd'))]
  ];
  for (var i = 0; i < slots.length; i++) {
    var el = document.getElementById(slots[i][0]);
    if (el) { el.textContent = slots[i][1]; }
  }
}


/* ── 预设主题：light/dark 两组合并成一份，渲染成一个网格 ── */
function uiPresetList(base){ return UI_PRESETS[base] || UI_PRESETS.light; }
function uiAllPresets(){
  var out = [], groups = [uiPresetList('light'), uiPresetList('dark')];
  for (var g = 0; g < groups.length; g++){
    var base = g === 0 ? 'light' : 'dark';
    for (var i = 0; i < groups[g].length; i++){
      var p = groups[g][i];
      var st = UI_THEME_STYLE[p.id] || {};
      out.push({ id:p.id, name:p.name, base:base, tokens:p,
                 fx:st.fx || 'none', deco:st.deco || 'none', layout:st.layout || 'sidebar',
                 builtin:true });
    }
  }
  return out;
}
function uiPresetDef(id){
  var all = uiAllPresets();
  for (var i = 0; i < all.length; i++) { if (all[i].id === id) { return all[i]; } }
  return all[0];
}
function uiPreset(base, id){
  var list = uiPresetList(base);
  for (var i = 0; i < list.length; i++) { if (list[i].id === id) { return list[i]; } }
  return list[0];
}
function uiIsHex(v){ return /^#[0-9a-fA-F]{6}$/.test(String(v || '')); }
function uiFirstHex(){
  for (var i = 0; i < arguments.length; i++) { if (uiIsHex(arguments[i])) { return String(arguments[i]).toLowerCase(); } }
  return '#000000';
}
function uiCustomAt(index){ return uiState.custom[index]; }
function uiFindCustom(name){
  for (var i = 0; i < uiState.custom.length; i++) {
    if (uiState.custom[i] && uiState.custom[i].name === name) { return uiState.custom[i]; }
  }
  return null;
}
/* 已保存主题 → 统一结构（兼容旧版只存 accent 的数据）*/
function uiNormalizeCustom(theme){
  var presetId = theme.preset || 'blue';
  var def = uiPresetDef(presetId);
  var colors = {};
  if (theme.colors) {
    for (var k in def.tokens) {
      if (k === 'id' || k === 'name') { continue; }
      colors[k] = uiFirstHex(theme.colors[k], def.tokens[k]);
    }
  } else {
    for (var k2 in def.tokens) {
      if (k2 === 'id' || k2 === 'name') { continue; }
      colors[k2] = def.tokens[k2];
    }
    if (uiIsHex(theme.accent)) {
      colors.accent = String(theme.accent).toLowerCase();
      colors.accentHi = colors.accent;
    }
  }
  return {
    name: theme.name,
    base: (theme.base === 'dark' || def.base === 'dark') ? 'dark' : 'light',
    preset: presetId,
    colors: colors,
    fx: theme.fx || def.fx || 'none',
    deco: theme.deco || def.deco || 'none',
    layout: theme.layout || def.layout || 'sidebar'
  };
}
/* 当前显示主题的完整 tokens（预设或已保存）*/
function uiActiveSpec(themeName){
  var custom = uiFindCustom(themeName);
  if (custom) { return uiNormalizeCustom(custom); }
  if (themeName === 'light' || themeName === 'dark') {
    return { name:themeName, base:themeName, preset:'l-blue',
             colors:uiPreset(themeName, 'l-blue'), fx:'none', deco:'none', layout:'sidebar' };
  }
  /* 点击预设时主题名是 'preset:<id>'；这里也接受裸 id */
  var bare = String(themeName || '');
  if (bare.indexOf('preset:') === 0) { bare = bare.slice(7); }
  var all = uiAllPresets();
  for (var ai = 0; ai < all.length; ai++) {
    if (all[ai].id === bare) {
      var def = all[ai];
      return { name:themeName, base:def.base, preset:def.id, colors:def.tokens,
               fx:def.fx || 'none', deco:def.deco || 'none', layout:def.layout || 'sidebar' };
    }
  }
  var base = uiState.systemDark ? 'dark' : 'light';
  return { name:themeName, base:base, preset:'l-blue', colors:uiPreset(base, 'l-blue'),
           fx:'none', deco:'none', layout:'sidebar' };
}
function uiSpecTokens(spec){
  var out = {};
  var fields = UI_COLOR_FIELDS;
  for (var i = 0; i < fields.length; i++) {
    var k = fields[i].key;
    out[k] = uiFirstHex(spec.colors && spec.colors[k], uiPreset('dark', 'blue')[k], '#000000');
  }
  return out;
}
function uiResolvedTokens(theme){
  if (typeof theme === 'string') { return uiSpecTokens(uiActiveSpec(theme)); }
  if (theme && theme.colors) { return uiSpecTokens(theme); }
  return uiSpecTokens(uiNormalizeCustom(theme));
}
function uiTokenCss(tokens){
  return [
    '--bg:' + tokens.bg, '--bg-rgb:' + uiRgbTriple(tokens.bg),
    '--surface:' + uiRgba(tokens.surface, 0.7),
    '--surface-2:' + uiRgba(tokens.surface2, 0.84), '--surface2:' + uiRgba(tokens.surface2, 0.84),
    '--surface-3:' + uiRgba(tokens.surface3, 0.93), '--surface-4:' + tokens.surface4,
    '--surface4:' + tokens.surface4, '--surface-solid:' + tokens.surface,
    '--border:' + tokens.border, '--border-strong:' + tokens.borderStrong,
    '--border-hi:' + tokens.borderStrong, '--border-2:' + tokens.borderStrong,
    '--text:' + tokens.text, '--text-dim:' + tokens.textDim, '--text-mute:' + tokens.textMute,
    '--text-2:' + tokens.textDim, '--text-3:' + tokens.textMute,
    '--sub:' + tokens.textDim,
    '--accent:' + tokens.accent, '--accent-hi:' + tokens.accentHi, '--accent2:' + tokens.accentHi,
    '--accent-2:' + tokens.accentHi, '--accent-3:' + tokens.violet,
    '--accent-soft:' + uiRgba(tokens.accent, 0.16), '--glow:' + uiRgba(tokens.accent, 0.22),
    '--accent-glow:' + uiRgba(tokens.accent, 0.38),
    '--violet:' + tokens.violet, '--violet-soft:' + uiRgba(tokens.violet, 0.16),
    '--overlay:' + uiRgba(tokens.bg, 0.84),
    '--ov:' + uiRgbTriple(tokens.surface)
  ].join(';');
}
function uiThemeNode(){
  var node = document.getElementById('uiThemeOverride');
  if (!node) {
    node = document.createElement('style');
    node.id = 'uiThemeOverride';
    document.head.appendChild(node);
  }
  return node;
}
function uiThemeRule(tokens){
  return 'html:root,html:root[data-theme="dark"],html:root[data-theme="light"]{' +
    uiTokenCss(tokens) + '}';
}
function uiBaseOf(theme){
  var custom = uiFindCustom(theme);
  if (custom) { return uiNormalizeCustom(custom).base; }
  if (theme === 'light' || theme === 'dark') { return theme; }
  /* 预设主题（含浅色预设）用主题自己的 base，不能回退到系统深浅色 */
  var name = String(theme || '');
  if (name.indexOf('preset:') === 0) { name = name.slice(7); }
  var all = uiAllPresets();
  for (var i = 0; i < all.length; i++) {
    if (all[i].id === name) { return all[i].base; }
  }
  return uiState.systemDark ? 'dark' : 'light';
}
/* 特效层 / 装饰层 / 布局：没加载对应模块时静默跳过 */
function uiApplyDynamic(spec){
  var root = document.documentElement;
  if (root.setAttribute) { root.setAttribute('data-layout', spec.layout || 'sidebar'); }
  if (typeof fxSet === 'function') { fxSet(spec.fx || 'none'); }
  else { document.body.setAttribute('data-fx', spec.fx || 'none'); }
  if (typeof decoSet === 'function') { decoSet(spec.deco || 'none'); }
  else { document.body.setAttribute('data-deco', spec.deco || 'none'); }
  /* banner 角饰跟着装饰类型走（万圣节/圣诞/四季）*/
  var hero = document.getElementById('hero');
  if (hero) { hero.setAttribute('data-deco', spec.deco || 'none'); }
}
function uiApplyTheme(theme, persist){
  uiState.theme = theme;
  var spec = uiActiveSpec(theme);
  document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-theme', spec.base);
  var custom = uiFindCustom(theme);
  uiThemeNode().textContent = (custom || (theme !== 'light' && theme !== 'dark'))
    ? uiThemeRule(uiSpecTokens(spec)) : '';
  uiApplyDynamic(spec);
  uiPaintThemeSeg();
  uiRenderPresets();      /* 让预设卡片的高亮跟着当前主题走 */
  uiRenderThemeGrid();
  if (persist !== false) { uiSend('UI_THEME:' + theme); }
}
/* 编辑器实时预览（换色、换特效都立刻可见）*/
function uiPreviewTheme(){
  var spec = {
    base: uiState.editorBase, colors: uiState.editorColors,
    fx: uiState.editorFx, deco: uiState.editorDeco, layout: uiState.editorLayout
  };
  document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-theme', spec.base);
  uiThemeNode().textContent = uiThemeRule(uiSpecTokens(spec));
  uiApplyDynamic(spec);
}
function uiPaintThemeSeg(){
  var seg = document.getElementById('uiThemeSeg');
  if (!seg) { return; }
  var active = uiState.theme;
  if (active !== 'system' && active !== 'light' && active !== 'dark') { active = uiBaseOf(active); }
  for (var i = 0; i < seg.children.length; i++) {
    var btn = seg.children[i];
    btn.classList.toggle('active', btn.getAttribute('data-theme-value') === active);
  }
}
function uiSetTheme(name){ uiSyncEditorBase(name); uiApplyTheme(name); }
/* 把编辑器状态对齐到某个主题（预设或已保存）*/
/* 把编辑器状态对齐到某个主题。name 可能是：
   'light' / 'dark' / 'system' / 'preset:<id>' / '<已保存主题名>' / 裸预设 id */
function uiSyncEditorBase(name){
  var custom = uiFindCustom(name);
  var spec = null;
  var presetId = null;
  if (custom) {
    spec = uiNormalizeCustom(custom);


    presetId = spec.preset || null;
  } else {
    var n = String(name || '');
    if (n.indexOf('preset:') === 0) { n = n.slice(7); }
    /* 裸 id 也要能认出来（切换主题后再打开设置就是这种情况）*/
    var all = uiAllPresets();
    for (var i = 0; i < all.length; i++) {
      if (all[i].id === n) { presetId = all[i].id; break; }
    }
    if (presetId) {
      spec = uiPresetDef(presetId);
    } else if (n === 'light' || n === 'dark') {
      presetId = (n === 'dark') ? 'd-blue' : 'l-blue';
      var p = uiPresetDef(presetId);
      spec = { base:n, colors:p.tokens, fx:'none', deco:'none', layout:'sidebar' };
    } else {
      var base = uiBaseOf(name);
      presetId = (base === 'dark') ? 'd-blue' : 'l-blue';
      var p2 = uiPresetDef(presetId);
      spec = { base:base, colors:p2.tokens, fx:'none', deco:'none', layout:'sidebar' };
    }
  }
  uiState.editorBase = spec.base;
  uiState.editorPreset = presetId || 'l-blue';
  uiState.editorColors = spec.colors || spec.tokens;
  uiState.editorFx = spec.fx || 'none';
  uiState.editorDeco = spec.deco || 'none';
  uiState.editorLayout = spec.layout || 'sidebar';
  uiRenderPresets();
  uiRenderColourGrid();
  uiRenderStylePickers();
  uiSyncEditorInputs();
}
/* 点击预设：直接应用（不再需要再点 Apply）*/
function uiPickPreset(id){
  var def = uiPresetDef(id);
  uiState.editorPreset = id;
  uiState.editorBase = def.base;
  uiState.editorColors = def.tokens;
  uiState.editorFx = def.fx || 'none';
  uiState.editorDeco = def.deco || 'none';
  uiState.editorLayout = def.layout || 'sidebar';
  uiRenderPresets();
  uiRenderColourGrid();
  uiRenderStylePickers();
  uiSyncEditorInputs();
  uiApplyTheme('preset:' + id, true);
}
function uiEditorIsPreset(){ return uiState.theme === ('preset:' + uiState.editorPreset); }
/* 任意一个颜色被改动 */
/* 取色器改成了自绘的 iOS 风面板，所以颜色值一律以 uiState 为准 */
function uiColorValue(key){ return uiFirstHex(uiState.editorColors && uiState.editorColors[key], '#000000'); }
function uiPaintColorTriggers(){
  var fields = UI_COLOR_FIELDS;
  for (var i = 0; i < fields.length; i++) {
    var k = fields[i].key;
    var btn = document.querySelector('[data-colour="' + k + '"]');
    if (!btn) { continue; }
    var v = uiColorValue(k);
    btn.style.background = v;
    btn.setAttribute('title', v.toUpperCase());
  }
}
/* 由一个 key 推出一组联动值（accent 同时带动 accentHi）*/
function uiColorLinked(key){ return (key === 'accent') ? ['accent', 'accentHi'] : [key]; }
function uiApplyColorValue(key, value, fromTrigger){
  if (!uiIsHex(value)) { return; }
  value = String(value).toLowerCase();
  var keys = uiColorLinked(key);
  for (var i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) { uiState.editorColors[keys[i]] = value; }
  uiPaintColorTriggers();
  uiSyncEditorInputs(true);
  if (!fromTrigger) { uiSyncColorPane(); }
  uiState.theme = '';
  uiRenderPresets();
  uiPreviewTheme();
}
function uiOnColorHex(key){
  var hex = document.getElementById('uiHex_' + key);
  if (!hex) { return; }
  var value = String(hex.value || '').trim();
  if (!/^#?[0-9a-fA-F]{6}$/.test(value)) { return; }
  if (value.charAt(0) !== '#') { value = '#' + value; }
  uiApplyColorValue(key, value, false);
}
function uiOnStylePick(what){
  var el = document.getElementById(what === 'fx' ? 'uiFxPick' : (what === 'deco' ? 'uiDecoPick' : 'uiLayoutPick'));
  if (!el) { return; }
  if (what === 'fx') { uiState.editorFx = el.value; }
  else if (what === 'deco') { uiState.editorDeco = el.value; }
  else { uiState.editorLayout = el.value; }
  uiStyleLabelRefresh(el);
  uiState.theme = '';
  uiPreviewTheme();
}
/* 浮层选完后刷新按钮上的文字 */
function uiStyleLabelRefresh(sel){
  if (!sel) { return; }
  var label = sel.getAttribute('data-ui-label') || '';
  var shown = uiStyleLabel(sel.value);
  sel.setAttribute('data-ui-label', label);
  sel.title = shown;
  if (sel.__popLabel) { sel.__popLabel.textContent = shown; }
}
/* 旧版本存的是 sidebar，等价于现在的左侧栏 */
function uiLayoutNormal(v){
  if (!v || v === 'sidebar' || v === 'topbar' || v === 'compact') { return 'rail-l'; }
  return v;
}
/* NVIDIA App 主题的布局是固定的，不允许手改 */
function uiLayoutLocked(){
  var t = String(uiState.theme || '');
  if (t.indexOf('preset:') === 0) { t = t.slice(7); }
  var id = t || String(uiState.editorPreset || '');
  if (id === 'd-nvidia') { return true; }

}
function uiOnLayoutPick(lv){
  if (uiLayoutLocked()) { return; }
  uiState.editorLayout = uiLayoutNormal(lv);
  /* 单独改布局时，把当前预设的配色作为自定义基底保留 */
  uiState.theme = '';
  uiRenderStylePickers();
  uiPreviewTheme();
}
/* ── Windows 11 弹出菜单：给每个下拉套一个自绘浮层 ───────────────────── */
function uiInitPopups(){
  document.addEventListener('mousedown', function(e){
    var sel = e.target && e.target.closest ? e.target.closest('select.ui-select') : null;
    if (sel) {
      e.preventDefault();
      uiOpenSelectPopup(sel);
      return;
    }
    if (e.target && e.target.closest && e.target.closest('.ui-pop')) { return; }
    uiCloseAllPopups();
  });
  document.addEventListener('keydown', function(e){ if (e.key === 'Escape') { uiCloseAllPopups(); } });
  window.addEventListener('resize', uiCloseAllPopups);
}
function uiCloseAllPopups(){
  var all = document.querySelectorAll('.ui-pop');
  for (var i = 0; i < all.length; i++) { all[i].hidden = true; }
}
function uiOpenSelectPopup(sel){
  var pop = document.getElementById('uiSelectPop');
  if (!pop) {
    pop = document.createElement('div');
    pop.className = 'ui-pop';
    pop.id = 'uiSelectPop';
    document.body.appendChild(pop);
  }
  uiCloseAllPopups();
  var html = '';
  for (var i = 0; i < sel.options.length; i++) {
    var o = sel.options[i];
    var on = (o.value === sel.value);
    html += '<button type="button" class="ui-pop-item' + (on ? ' on' : '') + '" data-value="' + uiEsc(o.value) + '">' +
      '<span class="ui-pop-tick">' + (on ? '\u2713' : '') + '</span>' +
      '<span>' + uiEsc(uiStyleLabel(o.value)) + '</span></button>';
  }
  pop.innerHTML = html;
  pop.hidden = false;
  pop.onclick = function(e){
    var btn = e.target && e.target.closest ? e.target.closest('.ui-pop-item') : null;
    if (!btn) { return; }
    sel.value = btn.getAttribute('data-value');
    uiStyleLabelRefresh(sel);
    uiCloseAllPopups();
    uiOnStylePick(sel.id === 'uiFxPick' ? 'fx' : (sel.id === 'uiDecoPick' ? 'deco' : 'layout'));
  };
  var r = sel.getBoundingClientRect();
  pop.style.left = Math.round(r.left) + 'px';
  pop.style.top = Math.round(r.bottom + 4) + 'px';
  pop.style.minWidth = Math.round(r.width) + 'px';
  var pr = pop.getBoundingClientRect();
  if (pr.bottom > window.innerHeight - 8) { pop.style.top = Math.round(r.top - pr.height - 4) + 'px'; }
  if (pr.right > window.innerWidth - 8) { pop.style.left = Math.round(window.innerWidth - pr.width - 8) + 'px'; }
}
function uiSyncEditorInputs(skipColorInputs){
  var fields = UI_COLOR_FIELDS;
  for (var i = 0; i < fields.length; i++) {
    var k = fields[i].key;
    var v = uiColorValue(k);
    var hex = document.getElementById('uiHex_' + k);
    if (hex && document.activeElement !== hex) { hex.value = v.toUpperCase(); }
  }
  uiPaintColorTriggers();
  if (skipColorInputs !== true) { uiSyncColorPane(); }
}
function uiSaveTheme(){
  var nameEl = document.getElementById('uiThemeName');
  var name = nameEl ? String(nameEl.value || '').trim() : '';
  if (!name) { name = uiT('themeNamePh'); }
  if (name.length > 24) { name = name.slice(0, 24); }
  var theme = {
    name: name, base: uiState.editorBase, preset: uiState.editorPreset,
    colors: uiState.editorColors,
    fx: uiState.editorFx, deco: uiState.editorDeco, layout: uiState.editorLayout
  };
  var index = -1;
  for (var i = 0; i < uiState.custom.length; i++) {
    if (uiState.custom[i] && uiState.custom[i].name === name) { index = i; }
  }
  if (index >= 0) { uiState.custom[index] = theme; } else { uiState.custom.push(theme); }
  if (nameEl) { nameEl.value = ''; }
  uiPersistCustom();
  uiApplyTheme(name);
}
function uiRemoveTheme(index){
  var removed = uiState.custom[index];
  uiState.custom.splice(index, 1);
  uiPersistCustom();
  if (removed && uiState.theme === removed.name) { uiApplyTheme('system'); }
  else { uiRenderThemeGrid(); }
}
function uiPersistCustom(){
  try { uiSend('UI_CUSTOM:' + JSON.stringify(uiState.custom)); }
  catch (e) { /* ignore */ }
}


/* 预设主题网格：swatch 卡片，点一下直接应用 */
function uiRenderPresets(){
  var box = document.getElementById('uiPresetRow');
  if (!box) { return; }
  var all = uiAllPresets();
  var html = '';
  for (var i = 0; i < all.length; i++) {
    var d = all[i];
    var active = (uiState.theme === ('preset:' + d.id));
    html += '<button type="button" class="ui-swatch' + (active ? ' active' : '') + '"' +
      ' data-tip="' + uiEsc(uiSwatchTip(d.base, d.fx, d.deco)) + '"' +
      ' onclick="uiPickPreset(\'' + uiEsc(d.id) + '\')">' +
      '<span class="ui-swatch-chip" style="--chip-s:' + uiEsc(d.tokens.surface) +
        ';--chip-bg:' + uiEsc(d.tokens.bg) + ';--chip-a:' + uiEsc(d.tokens.accent) +
        ';--chip-v:' + uiEsc(d.tokens.violet) + ';--chip-h:' + uiEsc(d.tokens.accentHi) + '">' +
        '<span class="sw-top"></span>' +
        '<span class="sw-row r1"></span><span class="sw-row r2"></span>' +
        '<span class="sw-row r3"></span><span class="sw-row r4"></span>' +
      '</span>' +
      '<span class="ui-swatch-text">' +
        '<span class="ui-swatch-name">' + uiEsc(d.name) + '</span>' +
        '<span class="ui-swatch-sub">' + uiEsc(d.base === 'dark' ? uiT('darkTheme') : uiT('lightTheme')) +
          (d.fx !== 'none' ? ' \u00B7 ' + uiEsc(d.fx) : '') + '</span>' +
      '</span>' +
      '</button>';
  }
  box.innerHTML = html;
}
/* 已保存主题：和预设完全一样的卡片布局 */
function uiRenderThemeGrid(){
  var box = document.getElementById('uiThemeList');
  if (!box) { return; }
  if (!uiState.custom.length) {
    box.innerHTML = '<div class="ui-empty">' + uiEsc(uiT('noSaved')) + '</div>';
    return;
  }
  var html = '';
  for (var i = 0; i < uiState.custom.length; i++) {
    var theme = uiState.custom[i];
    if (!theme) { continue; }
    var spec = uiNormalizeCustom(theme);
    var tokens = uiSpecTokens(spec);
    var active = (uiState.theme === theme.name);
    html += '<div class="ui-swatch' + (active ? ' active' : '') + '" role="button" tabindex="0"' +
      ' data-tip="' + uiEsc(uiSwatchTip(spec.base, spec.fx, spec.deco)) + '"' +
      ' onclick="uiApplyTheme(uiCustomAt(' + i + ').name)">' +
      '<span class="ui-swatch-chip" style="--chip-s:' + uiEsc(tokens.surface) +
        ';--chip-bg:' + uiEsc(tokens.bg) + ';--chip-a:' + uiEsc(tokens.accent) +
        ';--chip-v:' + uiEsc(tokens.violet) + ';--chip-h:' + uiEsc(tokens.accentHi) + '">' +
        '<span class="sw-top"></span>' +
        '<span class="sw-row r1"></span><span class="sw-row r2"></span>' +
        '<span class="sw-row r3"></span><span class="sw-row r4"></span>' +
      '</span>' +
      '<span class="ui-swatch-text">' +
        '<span class="ui-swatch-name">' + uiEsc(theme.name) + '</span>' +
        '<span class="ui-swatch-sub">' + uiEsc(spec.base === 'dark' ? uiT('darkTheme') : uiT('lightTheme')) +
          (spec.fx && spec.fx !== 'none' ? ' \u00B7 ' + uiEsc(spec.fx) : '') + '</span>' +
      '</span>' +
      '<button class="ui-swatch-del" type="button" title="' + uiEsc(uiT('remove')) + '"' +
        ' onclick="event.stopPropagation();uiRemoveTheme(' + i + ')">\u00D7</button>' +
      '</div>';
  }
  box.innerHTML = html;
}
/* 完全自定义：每个颜色一个取色器 + 十六进制输入 */
function uiRenderColourGrid(){
  var box = document.getElementById('uiColourGrid');
  if (!box) { return; }
  if (!uiState.editorColors) { uiState.editorColors = uiPresetDef(uiState.editorPreset).tokens; }
  var html = '';
  for (var i = 0; i < UI_COLOR_FIELDS.length; i++) {
    var f = UI_COLOR_FIELDS[i];
    var v = uiColorValue(f.key);
    html += '<div class="ui-colour-row">' +
      '<span class="ui-colour-name" title="' + uiEsc(f.label) + '">' + uiEsc(uiT(UI_COLOR_LABEL_KEY[f.label] || f.label)) + '</span>' +
      '<button type="button" class="ui-iosdot" data-colour="' + f.key + '"' +
        ' style="background:' + v + '"' +
        ' onclick="uiOpenColorPane(\'' + f.key + '\', this, event)"></button>' +
      '<input type="text" class="ui-hex" id="uiHex_' + f.key + '" maxlength="7" spellcheck="false"' +
        ' value="' + v.toUpperCase() + '" oninput="uiOnColorHex(\'' + f.key + '\')">' +
      '</div>';
  }
  box.innerHTML = html;
}
/* 背景特效 / 装饰 / 整体布局 */
/* Windows 11 风格下拉：原生 select 只保留语义，交互走自绘浮层 */
function uiRenderStylePickers(){
  var defs = [
    { id:'uiFxPick',   list:UI_FX_LIST,   cur:uiState.editorFx,   label:uiT('fxLabel') },
    { id:'uiDecoPick', list:UI_DECO_LIST, cur:uiState.editorDeco, label:uiT('decoLabel') }
  ];
  for (var d = 0; d < defs.length; d++) {
    var def = defs[d];
    var sel = document.getElementById(def.id);
    if (!sel) { continue; }
    var html = '';
    for (var i = 0; i < def.list.length; i++) {
      var val = def.list[i];
      html += '<option value="' + uiEsc(val) + '"' + (val === def.cur ? ' selected' : '') + '>' +
              uiEsc(uiStyleLabel(val)) + '</option>';
    }
    sel.innerHTML = html;
    sel.setAttribute('data-ui-label', def.label || '');
  }
  /* 布局：左侧栏 / 右侧栏 / 图标栏，可点击切换 */
  var seg = document.getElementById('uiLayoutSeg');
  if (seg) {
    var cur = uiLayoutNormal(uiState.editorLayout);
    var locked = uiLayoutLocked();
    var html2 = '';
    for (var k = 0; k < UI_LAYOUT_LIST.length; k++) {
      var lv = UI_LAYOUT_LIST[k];
      var on = (lv === cur) ? ' on' : '';
      html2 += '<button type="button" class="' + on.trim() + '"' +
        (locked ? ' disabled aria-disabled="true"' : ' onclick="uiOnLayoutPick(\'' + lv + '\')"') + '>' +
        uiEsc(uiStyleLabel(lv)) + '</button>';
    }
    seg.innerHTML = html2;
  }
}
/* fx / deco / layout 的取值都翻译成当前语言 */
function uiStyleLabel(v){
  if (v === 'none') { return uiT('fxNone'); }
  if (UI_LAYOUT_KEY[v]) { return uiT(UI_LAYOUT_KEY[v]); }
  var k = 'fx_' + v;
  var t = uiT(k);
  return (t && t !== k) ? t : v;
}
/* 悬停主题色板时的提示文字（跟随语言）*/
function uiSwatchTip(base, fx, deco){
  var parts = [base === 'dark' ? uiT('darkTheme') : uiT('lightTheme')];
  if (fx && fx !== 'none') { parts.push(uiStyleLabel(fx)); }
  if (deco && deco !== 'none') {
    var d = uiStyleLabel(deco);
    var dup = false;
    for (var i = 0; i < parts.length; i++) { if (parts[i] === d) { dup = true; } }
    if (!dup) { parts.push(d); }
  }
  return parts.join(' · ');
}



/* ── iOS 风格取色面板 ──────────────────────────────────────────────── */
var iosPane = { key:null, el:null, h:0, s:1, v:1, drag:null, esc:false };
function uiHsvToHex(h, s, v){
  var c = v * s, x = c * (1 - Math.abs(((h / 60) % 2) - 1)), m = v - c, r = 0, g = 0, b = 0;
  if (h < 60)       { r = c; g = x; }
  else if (h < 120) { r = x; g = c; }
  else if (h < 180) { g = c; b = x; }
  else if (h < 240) { g = x; b = c; }
  else if (h < 300) { r = x; b = c; }
  else              { r = c; b = x; }
  var f = function(n){ var q = Math.round((n + m) * 255); return (q < 16 ? '0' : '') + q.toString(16); };
  return '#' + f(r) + f(g) + f(b);
}
function uiHexToHsv(hex){
  var h = String(hex || '').replace('#', '');
  if (h.length !== 6) { return { h:0, s:0, v:0 }; }
  var r = parseInt(h.slice(0, 2), 16) / 255;
  var g = parseInt(h.slice(2, 4), 16) / 255;
  var b = parseInt(h.slice(4, 6), 16) / 255;
  var mx = Math.max(r, g, b), mn = Math.min(r, g, b), d = mx - mn, hh = 0;
  if (d > 0) {
    if (mx === r)      { hh = 60 * (((g - b) / d) % 6); }
    else if (mx === g) { hh = 60 * (((b - r) / d) + 2); }
    else               { hh = 60 * (((r - g) / d) + 4); }
  }
  if (hh < 0) { hh += 360; }
  return { h:hh, s:mx > 0 ? d / mx : 0, v:mx };
}
function uiColorPaneEl(){ return document.getElementById('uiColorPane'); }
function uiSyncColorPane(){
  var pane = uiColorPaneEl();
  if (!pane || pane.hidden || !iosPane.key) { return; }
  var hex = uiColorValue(iosPane.key);
  var sv = pane.querySelector('.iosp-sv');
  var kn = pane.querySelector('.iosp-knob');
  var hk = pane.querySelector('.iosp-hueknob');
  var sw = pane.querySelector('.iosp-sw');
  var hx = pane.querySelector('.iosp-hex');
  var hue = pane.querySelector('.iosp-sv');
  if (hue) { hue.style.background = uiHsvToHex(iosPane.h, 1, 1); }
  if (kn && sv) { kn.style.left = (iosPane.s * 100) + '%'; kn.style.top = ((1 - iosPane.v) * 100) + '%'; }
  if (hk) { hk.style.left = ((iosPane.h / 360) * 100) + '%'; }
  if (sw) { sw.style.background = hex; }
  if (hx && document.activeElement !== hx) { hx.value = hex.toUpperCase(); }
}
function uiColorPaneFromPoint(){
  uiApplyColorValue(iosPane.key, uiHsvToHex(iosPane.h, iosPane.s, iosPane.v), true);
}
function uiOpenColorPane(key, anchor, ev){
  if (ev) { ev.stopPropagation(); }
  var pane = uiColorPaneEl();
  if (!pane) { return; }
  if (!pane.hidden && iosPane.key === key) { uiCloseColorPane(); return; }
  iosPane.key = key;
  iosPane.el = anchor || null;
  var hsv = uiHexToHsv(uiColorValue(key));
  iosPane.h = hsv.h; iosPane.s = hsv.s; iosPane.v = hsv.v;
  pane.hidden = false;
  pane.setAttribute('data-for', key);
  uiSyncColorPane();
  /* 贴着取色点弹出，超出视口就翻到另一侧 */
  var r = anchor ? anchor.getBoundingClientRect() : { left:20, bottom:20 };
  var pw = pane.offsetWidth || 220, ph = pane.offsetHeight || 210;
  var hasSV = !!(pane.querySelector('.iosp-sv') && pane.querySelector('.iosp-sv').clientWidth);
  if (!hasSV) { ph = 216; }
  var x = Math.min(Math.max(10, r.left - pw + 34), window.innerWidth - pw - 10);
  var y = r.bottom + 8;
  if (y + ph > window.innerHeight - 10) { y = Math.max(10, r.top - ph - 8); }
  pane.style.left = x + 'px';
  pane.style.top = y + 'px';
}
function uiCloseColorPane(){
  var pane = uiColorPaneEl();
  if (!pane) { return; }
  pane.hidden = true;
  iosPane.key = null;
  iosPane.drag = null;
}
function uiColorPaneDown(ev){
  var sv = ev.currentTarget;
  var move = function(e){
    var r = sv.getBoundingClientRect();
    iosPane.s = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, (e.clientX - r.left) / r.width));
    iosPane.v = 1 - Math.min(1, Math.max(0, (e.clientY - r.top) / r.height));
    uiSyncColorPane();
    uiColorPaneFromPoint();
  };
  iosPane.drag = 'sv';
  move(ev);
  var up = function(){ iosPane.drag = null; window.removeEventListener('pointermove', move); window.removeEventListener('pointerup', up); };
  window.addEventListener('pointermove', move);
  window.addEventListener('pointerup', up);
  if (ev.preventDefault) { ev.preventDefault(); }
}
function uiColorHueDown(ev){
  var hue = ev.currentTarget;
  var move = function(e){
    var r = hue.getBoundingClientRect();
    iosPane.h = Math.min(359.9, Math.max(0, ((e.clientX - r.left) / r.width) * 360));
    uiSyncColorPane();
    uiColorPaneFromPoint();
  };
  iosPane.drag = 'hue';
  move(ev);
  var up = function(){ iosPane.drag = null; window.removeEventListener('pointermove', move); window.removeEventListener('pointerup', up); };
  window.addEventListener('pointermove', move);
  window.addEventListener('pointerup', up);
  if (ev.preventDefault) { ev.preventDefault(); }
}
function uiColorPaneHex(){
  var pane = uiColorPaneEl();
  if (!pane) { return; }
  var hx = pane.querySelector('.iosp-hex');
  if (!hx) { return; }
  var v = String(hx.value || '').trim();
  if (!/^#?[0-9a-fA-F]{6}$/.test(v)) { return; }
  if (v.charAt(0) !== '#') { v = '#' + v; }
  var hsv = uiHexToHsv(v);
  iosPane.h = hsv.h; iosPane.s = hsv.s; iosPane.v = hsv.v;
  uiApplyColorValue(iosPane.key, v, true);
}
function uiInitColorPane(){
  var pane = uiColorPaneEl();
  if (!pane) { return; }
  var sv = pane.querySelector('.iosp-sv');
  var hue = pane.querySelector('.iosp-hue');
  if (sv) { sv.addEventListener('pointerdown', uiColorPaneDown); }
  if (hue) { hue.addEventListener('pointerdown', uiColorHueDown); }
  window.addEventListener('resize', function(){ if (!pane.hidden) { uiCloseColorPane(); } });
  document.addEventListener('pointerdown', function(e){
    if (pane.hidden) { return; }
    if (pane.contains(e.target)) { return; }
    if (e.target && e.target.closest && e.target.closest('.ui-iosdot')) { return; }
    uiCloseColorPane();
  }, true);
  document.addEventListener('keydown', function(e){ if (e.key === 'Escape') { uiCloseColorPane(); } });
}
function uiRenderLangGrid(){
  var grid = document.getElementById('uiLangGrid');
  if (!grid) { return; }
  var html = '';
  for (var i = 0; i < UI_LANGS.length; i++) {
    var lang = UI_LANGS[i];
    html += '<button type="button" class="lang-option' + (lang.code === uiState.lang ? ' active' : '') +
      '" lang="' + lang.code + '" onclick="uiSetLang(\'' + lang.code + '\')">' +
      '<span class="lang-flag">' + uiFlagSvg(lang.flag) + '</span>' +
      '<span class="lang-text">' +
        '<span class="lang-native">' + uiEsc(lang.native) + '</span>' +
        '<span class="lang-latin">' + uiEsc(lang.latin) + '</span>' +
      '</span></button>';
  }
  grid.innerHTML = html;
}
function uiLogId(v){
  return 'uiLogEntry-' + String(v).replace(/[^0-9A-Za-z_-]/g, '-');
}

function uiRenderChangelog(){
  var box = document.getElementById('uiLogList');
  var railBox = document.getElementById('uiLogRail');
  if (!box) { return; }

  var html = '';
  var rail = '';

  for (var i = 0; i < UI_CHANGELOG.length; i++) {
    var entry = UI_CHANGELOG[i];
    var items = entry[uiState.lang] || entry.en || [];
    var tag = entry.tag ? '<span class="ui-log-tag">' + uiEsc(uiT(entry.tag)) + '</span>' : '';
    var id = uiLogId(entry.v);

    html += '<div class="ui-log-entry" id="' + id + '" data-log-v="' + uiEsc(entry.v) + '">' +
      '<div class="ui-log-head"><span class="ui-log-ver">v' + uiEsc(entry.v) + '</span>' + tag +
      '<span class="ui-log-date">' + uiEsc(entry.date) + '</span></div>' +
      '<div class="ui-log-list">';

    for (var j = 0; j < items.length; j++) {
      html += '<div class="ui-log-item"><span>' + uiEsc(items[j]) + '</span></div>';
    }

    html += '</div></div>';

    /* 右侧导航项：正常只显示小横线，悬停展开显示版本 + 第一条更新摘要 */
    var first = items.length ? items[0] : '';
    var summary = 'v' + entry.v + (first ? ' · ' + first : '');

    rail += '<button type="button" class="ui-log-rail-item' + (i === 0 ? ' active' : '') + '"' +
      ' data-log-v="' + uiEsc(entry.v) + '"' +
      ' onclick="uiLogGo(\'' + uiEsc(entry.v) + '\')">' +
      '<span class="ui-log-rail-label">' + uiEsc(summary) + '</span>' +
      '<span class="ui-log-rail-dash"></span>' +
      '</button>';
  }

  box.innerHTML = html;

  if (railBox) {
    railBox.innerHTML = rail;
  }
}

function uiLogGo(v){
  var id = uiLogId(v);
  var el = document.getElementById(id);
  if (!el) { return; }

  el.scrollIntoView({
    behavior: 'smooth',
    block: 'start'
  });

  var items = document.querySelectorAll('#uiLogRail .ui-log-rail-item');
  for (var i = 0; i < items.length; i++) {
    items[i].classList.toggle('active', items[i].getAttribute('data-log-v') === v);
  }
}
function uiRenderI18n(){
  var nodes = document.querySelectorAll('[data-ui]');
  for (var i = 0; i < nodes.length; i++) {
    var key = nodes[i].getAttribute('data-ui');
    if (key) { nodes[i].textContent = uiT(key); }
  }
  var titles = document.querySelectorAll('[data-ui-title]');
  for (var j = 0; j < titles.length; j++) {
    var tkey = titles[j].getAttribute('data-ui-title');
    if (tkey) { titles[j].setAttribute('title', uiT(tkey)); }
  }
  var holders = document.querySelectorAll('[data-ui-ph]');
  for (var k = 0; k < holders.length; k++) {
    var pkey = holders[k].getAttribute('data-ui-ph');
    if (pkey) { holders[k].setAttribute('placeholder', uiT(pkey)); }
  }
}
function uiSetLang(code, persist){
  if (!UI_T[code]) { code = 'en'; }


  uiState.lang = code;
  document.documentElement.lang = code;
  uiRenderLangGrid(); uiRenderI18n(); uiRenderChangelog();
  if (typeof onUiLangChanged === 'function') { onUiLangChanged(code); }
  uiPaintPrices();
  if (persist !== false) { uiSend('UI_LANG:' + code); }
}
function uiSetSystemDark(dark){
  uiState.systemDark = !!dark;
  if (uiState.theme === 'system') { uiApplyTheme('system', false); }
}
function uiLockBackground(){
  try { document.body.classList.add('ui-blocked'); } catch (e) {}
}
function uiUnlockBackground(){
  try { document.body.classList.remove('ui-blocked'); } catch (e) {}
}
function uiOpenSettings(){
  var log = document.getElementById('uiChangelogView');
  var renew = document.getElementById('uiRenewView');
  if (log) { log.classList.remove('open'); }
  if (renew) { renew.classList.remove('open'); }
  uiSyncEditorBase(uiState.theme);
  var view = document.getElementById('uiSettingsView');
  uiShow(view);
  uiLockBackground();
  if (typeof onUiViewOpened === 'function') { onUiViewOpened('settings'); }
}
function uiOpenChangelog(){
  var settings = document.getElementById('uiSettingsView');
  var renew = document.getElementById('uiRenewView');
  if (settings) { settings.classList.remove('open'); }
  if (renew) { renew.classList.remove('open'); }
  var view = document.getElementById('uiChangelogView');
  uiShow(view);
  uiLockBackground();
  if (typeof onUiViewOpened === 'function') { onUiViewOpened('changelog'); }
}
/* 打开视图时由各页面自己的 onUiViewOpened/onUiViewClosed 记住并恢复上一级 */
function uiCloseView(){
  var settings = document.getElementById('uiSettingsView');
  var log = document.getElementById('uiChangelogView');
  var renew = document.getElementById('uiRenewView');
  uiHide(settings); uiHide(log); uiHide(renew);
  uiApplyTheme(uiState.theme, false);
  uiUnlockBackground();
  if (typeof onUiViewClosed === 'function') { onUiViewClosed(); }
}
/* 统一的 ESC 处理：先收浮层，再退回上一级 */
function uiEscBack(){
  var pane = document.getElementById('uiColorPane');
  if (pane && !pane.hidden) { uiCloseColorPane(); return true; }
  var pop = document.getElementById('uiSelectPop');
  if (pop && !pop.hidden) { uiCloseAllPopups(); return true; }
  var settings = document.getElementById('uiSettingsView');
  var log = document.getElementById('uiChangelogView');
  var renew = document.getElementById('uiRenewView');
  if (settings && settings.classList.contains('open')) { uiCloseView(); return true; }
  if (log && log.classList.contains('open')) { uiCloseView(); return true; }
  if (renew && renew.classList.contains('open')) { uiCloseView(); return true; }
  /* 主界面自己的层级（游戏详情页 / 个人资料） */
  if (typeof onUiEscBack === 'function' && onUiEscBack()) { return true; }
  if (typeof closeDetail === 'function') {
    var d = document.getElementById('detail');
    if (d && d.classList.contains('open')) { closeDetail(); return true; }
  }
  return false;
}
(function(){
  document.addEventListener('keydown', function(e){
    if (e.key !== 'Escape' && e.key !== 'Esc') { return; }
    if (e.altKey || e.ctrlKey) { return; }
    var t = e.target;
    if (t && t.tagName === 'INPUT' && t.classList && t.classList.contains('iosp-hex')) { return; }
    uiEscBack();
  });
})();
function uiShow(el){
  if (!el) { return; }
  el.__tk = (el.__tk || 0) + 1;
  el.classList.remove('closing');
  el.classList.add('open');
}
function uiHide(el){
  if (!el || !el.classList.contains('open')) { return; }
  var tk = (el.__tk = (el.__tk || 0) + 1);
  el.classList.add('closing');
  setTimeout(function(){
    if (el.__tk === tk) { el.classList.remove('open'); el.classList.remove('closing'); }
  }, 240);
}
function uiDrag(event){
  if (event && typeof event.button === 'number' && event.button !== 0) { return; }
  var t = event && event.target;
  if (t && t.closest && t.closest('button,input,a,select,textarea,label,[data-nodrag],.tb-btn,.tb-avatar,.tb-actions,.topbar-search,.ui-icon-btn,.detail-back')) { return; }
  uiSend('DRAG');
}
function uiErrText(reason){
  if (reason === 'ERR_NOT_FOUND')    { return uiT('errNotFound'); }
  if (reason === 'ERR_DLL_MISSING')  { return uiT('errDllMissing'); }
  if (reason === 'ERR_INJECT')       { return uiT('errInject'); }
  if (reason === 'DOWNLOAD_FAILED')  { return uiT('errDownload'); }
  if (reason === 'EXTRACT_FAILED')   { return uiT('errExtract'); }
  if (reason === 'RUN_FAILED')       { return uiT('errRun'); }
  if (reason === 'FILE_NOT_FOUND')   { return uiT('errDllMissing'); }
  if (reason === 'ERR_NOT_OWNED')    { return uiT('notSubscribed'); }
  return reason || uiT('unknownError');
}


/* ── Renew 视图逻辑 ──────────────────────────────────────────── */
function uiRenderRenewChips(){
  var box = document.getElementById('renewChips');
  var games = (UI_CFG && UI_CFG.gameCodes) || [];
  if (box) {
    var html = '';
    games.forEach(function(g){
      html += '<span class="renew-chip"><b>' + uiEsc(g) + '</b></span>';
    });
    box.innerHTML = html;
    box.classList.remove('dragging');
    box.scrollLeft = 0;
  }
  /* 同一份前缀列表也喂给 license key 右边问号的弹层 */
  var list = document.getElementById('renewKeyHelpList');
  if (list) {
    var lh = '';
    games.forEach(function(g){ lh += '<span>' + uiEsc(g) + '</span>'; });
    list.innerHTML = lh;
  }
}
/* 前缀太多时：按住鼠标左右拖动横向滚动（不再换行/撑高面板） */
function uiRenewChipsDrag(){
  var box = document.getElementById('renewChips');
  if (!box || box.getAttribute('data-drag') === '1') return;
  box.setAttribute('data-drag', '1');
  var down = false, startX = 0, startLeft = 0, moved = false;
  box.addEventListener('pointerdown', function(e){
    if (e.button) return;
    down = true; moved = false;
    startX = e.clientX; startLeft = box.scrollLeft;
    box.classList.add('dragging');
    try { box.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId); } catch (err) {}
  });
  box.addEventListener('pointermove', function(e){
    if (!down) return;
    var dx = e.clientX - startX;
    if (Math.abs(dx) > 3) moved = true;
    box.scrollLeft = startLeft - dx;
  });
  function release(){
    if (!down) return;
    down = false;
    box.classList.remove('dragging');
  }
  box.addEventListener('pointerup', release);
  box.addEventListener('pointercancel', release);
  box.addEventListener('click', function(e){
    if (moved) { e.stopPropagation(); e.preventDefault(); moved = false; }
  }, true);
  /* 顺带支持一下滚轮（没有横向滚轮时用竖直滚轮推） */
  box.addEventListener('wheel', function(e){
    if (Math.abs(e.deltaY) <= Math.abs(e.deltaX)) return;
    box.scrollLeft += e.deltaY;
    e.preventDefault();
  }, { passive: false });
}
function uiRenewGameCode(){
  return String(uiState.renewGame || '').trim();
}
/* 当前游戏的 games.txt 条目（登录页没有 GAMES，返回 null） */
function uiRenewMeta(){
  var code = uiRenewGameCode();
  if (!code || typeof GAMES === 'undefined' || !GAMES) return null;
  return GAMES[code] || null;
}
/* 某个方案的价钱：显式 0 = 免费；显式 >0 = 用游戏自己的价（和商店卡片一致，按 USD 显示）；
   没写 = 回退到全局默认价（按用户币种换算，保持原来的样子） */
function uiRenewPlanPrice(plan){
  var m = uiRenewMeta();
  var k = uiRenewPlanKeys(plan);
  if (m && m[k.free]) return { free: true, text: uiT('free') };
  if (m && typeof m[k.price] === 'number' && m[k.price] > 0) {
    return { free: false, text: '$' + m[k.price].toFixed(2) };
  }
  return { free: false, text: uiMoney(uiPrice(k.global)) };
}
/* 档位 → games.txt 里的价格字段 / 免费标记 / 全局默认价 */
function uiRenewPlanKeys(plan){
  if (plan === '7D')       return { free: 'free7',    price: 'price7',    global: 'd7'  };
  if (plan === '15D')      return { free: 'free15',   price: 'price15',   global: 'd15' };
  if (plan === '30D')      return { free: 'free30',   price: 'price30',   global: 'd30' };
  if (plan === 'LIFETIME') return { free: 'freeLife', price: 'priceLife', global: 'life' };
  return { free: 'free90', price: 'price90', global: 'd90' };
}
/* 把价钱刷到五张套餐卡上（跟着当前游戏走），并同步按钮文案/免费提示/输入框前缀 */
function uiRenewPaintPrices(){
  var slots = [['renewPrice7', '7D'], ['renewPrice15', '15D'], ['renewPrice30', '30D'],
               ['renewPrice90', '90D'], ['renewPriceLt', 'LIFETIME']];
  for (var i = 0; i < slots.length; i++) {
    var el = document.getElementById(slots[i][0]);
    if (!el) continue;
    var p = uiRenewPlanPrice(slots[i][1]);
    el.textContent = p.text;
    el.classList.toggle('free', !!p.free);
    var card = el.closest ? el.closest('.renew-plan') : null;
    if (card) card.classList.toggle('is-free', !!p.free);
  }
  var plan = uiState.renewPlan || '90D';
  var cur = uiRenewPlanPrice(plan);
  var hasGame = !!uiRenewGameCode();
  var btn = document.getElementById('renewSubmitBtn');
  var label = btn ? btn.querySelector('.label') : null;
  if (label) label.textContent = (cur.free && hasGame) ? uiT('renewSubscribe') : uiT('renewActivate');
  if (btn) btn.classList.toggle('is-free', !!(cur.free && hasGame));
  var hint = document.getElementById('renewFreeHint');
  if (hint) hint.hidden = !(cur.free && hasGame);
  var inp = document.getElementById('renewKeyInput');
  if (inp) {
    var code = uiRenewGameCode();
    inp.placeholder = (code || 'KEY') + '-' + plan + '-XXXX-XXXX-XXXX-XXXX';
  }
}
function uiRenewFmtLeft(sec){
  if (typeof sec !== 'number' || !isFinite(sec)) return '';
  if (sec < 0) return 'LIFETIME';
  var d = Math.floor(sec / 86400);
  var h = Math.floor((sec % 86400) / 3600);
  var mi = Math.floor((sec % 3600) / 60);
  if (d > 0) return d + 'D ' + h + 'h ' + mi + 'm';
  if (h > 0) return h + 'h ' + mi + 'm';
  return mi + 'm';
}
/* 当前游戏卡片：封面 + 名称 + 代号 + 订阅剩余时间（没订阅就写“未订阅”） */
function uiRenewRenderGame(){
  var code = uiRenewGameCode();
  var m = uiRenewMeta();
  var cover = document.getElementById('renewGameCover');
  var name  = document.getElementById('renewGameName');
  var sub   = document.getElementById('renewGameCode');
  var left  = document.getElementById('renewGameLeft');
  var img = m ? String(m.poster || m.img || '') : '';
  if (cover) {
    if (img) { cover.style.backgroundImage = "url('" + img + "')"; cover.classList.remove('empty'); }
    else { cover.style.backgroundImage = ''; cover.classList.add('empty'); }
  }
  if (name) name.textContent = m ? (m.title || code) : (code || '—');
  if (sub)  sub.textContent = code || '';
  var lic = null;
  try { if (code && typeof licOf === 'function') lic = licOf(code); } catch (e) { lic = null; }
  if (left) {
    if (lic && lic.type) {
      var txt = uiRenewFmtLeft(lic.seconds);
      if (lic.paused) txt += ' · ' + uiT('paused');
      left.textContent = txt;
      left.className = 'renew-game-left-val on';
    } else {
      left.textContent = uiT('unsubscribed');
      left.className = 'renew-game-left-val off';
    }
  }
}
function uiOpenRenew(gameCode){
  var s = document.getElementById('uiSettingsView');
  if (s) s.classList.remove('open');
  var l = document.getElementById('uiChangelogView');
  if (l) l.classList.remove('open');
  if (typeof gameCode === 'string' && gameCode) uiState.renewGame = gameCode;
  var r = document.getElementById('uiRenewView');
  uiShow(r);
  uiRenewReset();
  uiRenewPaintPrices();
  uiRenewRenderGame();
  uiPaintPrices();
  uiRenderRenewChips();
  uiLockBackground();
  if (typeof onUiViewOpened === 'function') { onUiViewOpened('renew'); }
}
function uiRenewReset(){
  var wrap = document.getElementById('renewKeyWrap');
  var inp  = document.getElementById('renewKeyInput');
  var clr  = document.getElementById('renewKeyClear');
  var btn  = document.getElementById('renewSubmitBtn');
  if (wrap) wrap.classList.remove('is-err','is-ok');
  if (inp)  { inp.value = ''; inp.disabled = false; }
  if (clr)  clr.classList.remove('show');
  if (btn)  { btn.disabled = false; btn.classList.remove('loading'); }
  uiRenewClearErr();
  uiRenewPickPlan(uiState.renewPlan || '90D');
  setTimeout(function(){ try { if (inp) inp.focus(); } catch (e) {} }, 60);
}
function uiRenewClear(){
  var inp = document.getElementById('renewKeyInput');
  if (inp) { inp.value = ''; inp.focus(); }
  var clr = document.getElementById('renewKeyClear');
  if (clr) clr.classList.remove('show');
  uiRenewClearErr();
}
function uiRenewPaste(){
  try {
    if (navigator.clipboard && navigator.clipboard.readText) {
      navigator.clipboard.readText().then(function(t){
        var inp = document.getElementById('renewKeyInput');
        if (inp) { inp.value = String(t || '').trim().toUpperCase(); }
        var clr = document.getElementById('renewKeyClear');
        if (clr && inp && inp.value.length > 0) clr.classList.add('show');
        uiRenewClearErr();
      }).catch(function(){});
    }
  } catch (e) {}
}
function uiRenewPickPlan(plan){
  uiState.renewPlan = plan;
  var nodes = document.querySelectorAll('.renew-plan');
  for (var i = 0; i < nodes.length; i++) {
    var p = nodes[i].getAttribute('data-plan');
    if (p === plan) nodes[i].classList.add('active');
    else nodes[i].classList.remove('active');
  }
  uiRenewPaintPrices();       /* 价钱/按钮文案/输入框前缀都跟着方案走 */
}
function uiRenewClearErr(){
  var st = document.getElementById('renewStatus');
  if (st) st.className = 'renew-status';
  var wrap = document.getElementById('renewKeyWrap');
  if (wrap) wrap.classList.remove('is-err','is-ok');
}
function uiRenewStatus(type, msg){
  var st = document.getElementById('renewStatus');
  if (!st) { return; }
  st.className = 'renew-status ' + type + ' show';
  st.textContent = msg;
}
/* 密钥格式由远端游戏列表动态生成：<code>-(7D|15D|30D|90D|LIFETIME)-(XXXX){4,5} */
function uiRenewKeyRegex(){
  var codes = (UI_CFG && UI_CFG.gameCodes) || [];
  if (!codes.length) codes = ['CS2'];
  var escaped = codes.map(function(c){
    return c.replace(/[-/\\^$*+?.()|[\]{}]/g, '\\$&');
  }).join('|');
  return new RegExp('^(' + escaped + ')-(7D|15D|30D|90D|LIFETIME)(-[A-Z0-9]{4}){4,5}$', 'i');
}
function uiRenewSubmit(){
  var inp = document.getElementById('renewKeyInput');
  var wrap = document.getElementById('renewKeyWrap');
  var btn = document.getElementById('renewSubmitBtn');
  if (!inp || !btn) { return; }
  var key = String(inp.value || '').trim().toUpperCase();
  var plan = uiState.renewPlan || '90D';
  var code = uiRenewGameCode();
  /* 免费方案 + 没填卡密 → 直接订阅，不用输入 renew key */
  if (!key && code && uiRenewPlanPrice(plan).free) {
    btn.disabled = true;
    btn.classList.add('loading');
    uiRenewStatus('ld', uiT('validating'));
    uiSend('SUBSCRIBE:' + code + ':' + plan);
    return;
  }
  if (!key) {
    uiRenewStatus('err', uiT('renewEmpty'));
    if (wrap) wrap.classList.add('is-err');
    return;
  }
  if (!uiRenewKeyRegex().test(key)) {
    uiRenewStatus('err', uiT('renewFmt'));
    if (wrap) wrap.classList.add('is-err');
    return;
  }
  btn.disabled = true;
  btn.classList.add('loading');
  uiRenewStatus('ld', uiT('validating'));
  uiSend('RENEW:' + key);
}
function uiRenewResult(ok, payload){
  var wrap = document.getElementById('renewKeyWrap');
  var btn = document.getElementById('renewSubmitBtn');
  if (btn) { btn.disabled = false; btn.classList.remove('loading'); }
  if (ok) {
    if (wrap) wrap.classList.add('is-ok');
    uiRenewStatus('ok', uiT('renewOk'));
    setTimeout(function(){
      uiCloseView();
      if (typeof onRenewSuccess === 'function') { onRenewSuccess(payload); }
    }, 1100);
  } else {
    if (wrap) wrap.classList.add('is-err');
    var msg = payload;
    if (payload === 'ERR_INVALID') { msg = uiT('errInvalid'); }
    else if (payload === 'ERR_NOT_FREE') { msg = uiT('renewNotFree'); }
    else if (payload === 'ERR_USED') { msg = uiT('errKeyUsed'); }
    else if (payload === 'ERR_AUTH') { msg = uiT('errAuth'); }
    else if (payload.indexOf('ERR_MSG:') === 0) { msg = payload.slice(8) || uiT('errInvalid'); }
    else if (payload.indexOf('ERR_NETWORK') === 0) {
      var d = payload.indexOf(':') > 0
        ? payload.slice(payload.indexOf(':') + 1) : '';
      msg = d ? uiT('errNet') + ' [' + d + ']' : uiT('errNet');
    }
    uiRenewStatus('err', msg);
  }
}
function uiRenewInputChanged(){
  var inp = document.getElementById('renewKeyInput');
  var clr = document.getElementById('renewKeyClear');
  if (!inp || !clr) { return; }
  if (String(inp.value || '').length > 0) clr.classList.add('show');
  else clr.classList.remove('show');
}


function uiInit(){
  uiState.systemDark = !!(UI_CFG && UI_CFG.systemDark);
  uiRenderLangGrid(); uiRenderI18n(); uiRenderChangelog();
  if (typeof fxInit === 'function') { fxInit(); }
  if (typeof decoInit === 'function') { decoInit(); }
  if (typeof uiInitColorPane === 'function') { uiInitColorPane(); }
  if (typeof uiInitPopups === 'function') { uiInitPopups(); }
  uiSyncEditorBase(uiState.theme);      /* 里面会渲染配色网格与风格下拉 */
  uiApplyTheme(uiState.theme, false);   /* 里面会渲染预设/已存主题网格并应用特效装饰 */
  uiSyncEditorInputs();
  uiApplyLangUi();
  uiPaintPrices();
  uiRenderRenewChips();
  uiRenewChipsDrag();
  uiListenHost();

  var inp = document.getElementById('renewKeyInput');
  if (inp) { inp.addEventListener('input', uiRenewInputChanged); }
}
function uiListenHost(){
  if (window.__uiSysListener || !window.chrome || !window.chrome.webview) { return; }
  window.__uiSysListener = true;
  window.chrome.webview.addEventListener('message', function (event) {
    var message = String(event && event.data != null ? event.data : '');
    if (message.indexOf('SYS_THEME:') === 0) {
      uiSetSystemDark(message.slice(10) === 'dark');
    }
  });
}
function uiApplyLangUi(){
  if (typeof onUiLangChanged === 'function') { onUiLangChanged(uiState.lang); }
}

function uiBgUrl(el){
  var m = /url\(["']?([^"')]+)["']?\)/.exec((el && el.style && el.style.backgroundImage) || '');
  return m ? m[1] : '';
}
function uiImgWatch(el, url, fallback){
  if (!el) { return; }
  url = url || uiBgUrl(el);
  if (!url) { return; }
  if (el.getAttribute('data-sl-url') === url && el.querySelector(':scope > .sl-skel.done')) { return; }
  el.setAttribute('data-sl-url', url);
  try {
    if (window.getComputedStyle(el).position === 'static') { el.style.position = 'relative'; }
  } catch (e) {}
  var sk = el.querySelector(':scope > .sl-skel');
  if (!sk) {
    sk = document.createElement('div');
    sk.className = 'sl-skel';
    el.appendChild(sk);
  }
  sk.classList.remove('done');
  var im = new Image();
  var finish = function(){
    if (el.getAttribute('data-sl-url') !== url) { return; }
    sk.classList.add('done');
  };
  im.onload = finish;
  im.onerror = function(){
    /* 图挂了就退回兜底图（通常是 icon），避免留下空白 banner */
    if (el.getAttribute('data-sl-url') !== url) { return; }
    finish();
    if (fallback && fallback !== url) {
      el.style.backgroundImage = "url('" + fallback + "')";
    }
  };
  im.src = url;
  if (im.complete) { finish(); }
}

/* ── 主题背景特效层（纯 CSS 动画，切换即有过渡）────────────────────── */
function fxSet(name){
  var layer = document.getElementById('fxLayer');
  var v = String(name || 'none');
  if (UI_FX_LIST.indexOf(v) < 0) { v = 'none'; }
  document.body.setAttribute('data-fx', v);
  if (layer) { layer.setAttribute('data-fx', v); }
}
function fxInit(){ fxSet('none'); }

/* ── 主题装饰层（飘落粒子：雪 / 落叶 / 花瓣 / 南瓜 / 星 / 彩带）────── */
var deco = { on:false, raf:0, last:0, w:0, h:0, parts:[], kind:'none' };
var DECO_SPECS = {
  snow:     { glyphs:['\u2744','\u2745','\u2746'], size:[10,22], speed:[18,46],  sway:[10,34], alpha:[.45,.9],  spin:0 },
  leaf:     { glyphs:['\u{1F342}','\u{1F341}'],    size:[14,26], speed:[26,60],  sway:[30,70], alpha:[.55,.95], spin:1 },
  petal:    { glyphs:['\u{1F338}','\u{1F337}'],    size:[12,22], speed:[20,50],  sway:[26,64], alpha:[.5,.9],  spin:1 },
  pumpkin:  { glyphs:['\u{1F383}','\u{1F47B}','\u{1F577}'], size:[16,30], speed:[20,50], sway:[16,44], alpha:[.6,1], spin:0 },
  star:     { glyphs:['\u2728','\u2B50'],          size:[12,24], speed:[16,42],  sway:[12,36], alpha:[.5,.95], spin:0 },
  confetti: { glyphs:['\u{1F389}','\u{1F38A}','\u2728'], size:[12,24], speed:[30,70], sway:[24,60], alpha:[.6,1], spin:1 },
  autumn:   { glyphs:['\u{1F342}','\u{1F343}'],    size:[14,26], speed:[26,58],  sway:[30,70], alpha:[.55,.95], spin:1 },
  winter:   { glyphs:['\u2744','\u2745','\u2746'], size:[10,22], speed:[18,48],  sway:[10,32], alpha:[.5,.95], spin:0 },
  sun:      { glyphs:['\u2600\uFE0F','\u{1F30A}'], size:[14,26], speed:[14,36],  sway:[14,38], alpha:[.45,.85], spin:0 }
};
function decoSpec(){ return DECO_SPECS[deco.kind] || null; }
function decoRand(a, b){ return a + Math.random() * (b - a); }
function decoSeed(){
  var spec = decoSpec();
  deco.parts = [];
  if (!spec) { return; }
  var n = Math.max(10, Math.min(46, Math.round(deco.w * deco.h / 34000)));
  for (var i = 0; i < n; i++) {
    deco.parts.push({
      x: Math.random() * deco.w,
      y: Math.random() * deco.h,
      s: decoRand(spec.size[0], spec.size[1]),
      v: decoRand(spec.speed[0], spec.speed[1]),
      sw: decoRand(spec.sway[0], spec.sway[1]),
      ph: Math.random() * 6.283,
      rot: Math.random() * 6.283,
      rs: decoRand(-1.2, 1.2),
      a: decoRand(spec.alpha[0], spec.alpha[1]),
      g: spec.glyphs[i % spec.glyphs.length]
    });
  }
}
function decoResize(){
  var cv = document.getElementById('decoCanvas');
  if (!cv) { return; }
  deco.w = window.innerWidth; deco.h = window.innerHeight;
  cv.width = deco.w; cv.height = deco.h;
  decoSeed();
}
function decoFrame(dt){
  var cv = document.getElementById('decoCanvas');
  if (!cv) { return; }
  var ctx = cv.getContext('2d');
  var spec = decoSpec();
  if (!ctx || !spec) { return; }
  ctx.clearRect(0, 0, deco.w, deco.h);
  ctx.textAlign = 'center';
  ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
  for (var i = 0; i < deco.parts.length; i++) {
    var p = deco.parts[i];
    p.y += p.v * dt;
    p.ph += dt * 1.4;
    if (spec.spin) { p.rot += p.rs * dt; }
    if (p.y - p.s > deco.h) { p.y = -p.s; p.x = Math.random() * deco.w; }
    var x = p.x + Math.sin(p.ph) * p.sw * .35;
    ctx.save();
    ctx.globalAlpha = p.a;
    ctx.translate(x, p.y);
    if (spec.spin) { ctx.rotate(p.rot); }
    ctx.font = p.s + 'px "Segoe UI Emoji","Apple Color Emoji",sans-serif';
    ctx.fillText(p.g, 0, 0);
    ctx.restore();
  }
}
function decoLoop(ts){
  deco.raf = requestAnimationFrame(decoLoop);
  if (deco.last) {
    var dt = Math.min(.05, (ts - deco.last) / 1000);
    decoFrame(dt);
  }
  deco.last = ts;
}
function decoSet(name){
  var v = String(name || 'none');
  if (UI_DECO_LIST.indexOf(v) < 0) { v = 'none'; }
  deco.kind = v;
  document.body.setAttribute('data-deco', v);
  if (v === 'none' || document.hidden) {
    deco.on = false;
    if (deco.raf) { cancelAnimationFrame(deco.raf); deco.raf = 0; }
    var cv = document.getElementById('decoCanvas');
    if (cv) { var ctx = cv.getContext('2d'); if (ctx) { ctx.clearRect(0, 0, cv.width, cv.height); } }
    return;
  }
  if (deco.on) { decoSeed(); return; }
  deco.on = true;
  deco.last = 0;
  decoResize();
  deco.raf = requestAnimationFrame(decoLoop);
}
function decoInit(){
  decoSet(document.body.getAttribute('data-deco') || 'none');
  window.addEventListener('resize', function(){ if (deco.on) { decoResize(); } });
  document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', function(){
    if (document.hidden) { decoSet('none'); }
    else { decoSet(deco.kind); }
  });
}

/* ── 自定义 tooltip ── */
(function(){
  var tip = null, timer = 0, cur = null;
  function ensure(){
    if (tip) { return tip; }
    tip = document.createElement('div');
    tip.className = 'sl-tip';
    tip.setAttribute('role', 'tooltip');
    document.body.appendChild(tip);
    return tip;
  }
  function hide(){
    clearTimeout(timer);
    cur = null;
    if (tip) { tip.classList.remove('show'); }
  }
  function textOf(el){
    var t = el.getAttribute('title');
    if (t !== null) {
      el.removeAttribute('title');
      if (t) { el.setAttribute('data-tip', t); } else { el.removeAttribute('data-tip'); }
    }
    return el.getAttribute('data-tip') || '';
  }
  function show(el){
    var text = textOf(el);
    if (!text || cur !== el) { return; }
    var t = ensure();
    t.textContent = text;
    t.classList.remove('below');
    t.style.left = '0px'; t.style.top = '0px';
    var r = el.getBoundingClientRect();
    var tw = t.offsetWidth, th = t.offsetHeight;
    var vw = document.documentElement.clientWidth, vh = document.documentElement.clientHeight;
    var below = r.top - th - 12 < 6;
    var x = r.left + r.width / 2 - tw / 2;
    x = Math.max(6, Math.min(vw - tw - 6, x));
    var y = below ? r.bottom + 10 : r.top - th - 10;
    if (below && y + th > vh - 6) { y = Math.max(6, r.top - th - 10); below = false; }
    if (below) { t.classList.add('below'); }
    var ax = r.left + r.width / 2 - x;
    t.style.setProperty('--ax', Math.max(14, Math.min(tw - 14, ax)) + 'px');
    t.style.left = Math.round(x) + 'px';
    t.style.top = Math.round(y) + 'px';
    t.classList.add('show');
  }
  document.addEventListener('mouseover', function(e){
    var el = e.target && e.target.closest ? e.target.closest('[title],[data-tip]') : null;
    if (el === cur) { return; }
    hide();
    if (!el) { return; }
    cur = el;
    textOf(el);
    timer = setTimeout(function(){ show(el); }, 280);
  }, true);
  document.addEventListener('mouseout', function(e){
    if (!cur) { return; }
    var to = e.relatedTarget;
    if (to && cur.contains && cur.contains(to)) { return; }
    hide();
  }, true);
  ['mousedown', 'wheel', 'keydown', 'scroll', 'blur'].forEach(function(n){
    window.addEventListener(n, hide, true);
  });
})();

  /* ════════ 背景动效 ════════ */
  var BGFX_ON = !(UI_CFG && UI_CFG.bgFx === false);
  var BGFX_STYLE = (UI_CFG && UI_CFG.bgStyle) || 'aurora';
  var BG_IMAGE = "__BG_IMAGE__";
  var BGFX_STYLES = ['aurora', 'waves', 'grid', 'stars', 'network', 'embers', 'image'];
  var BGFX_NAME_KEY = { aurora:'bgAurora', waves:'bgWaves', grid:'bgGrid', stars:'bgStars',
                        network:'bgNetwork', embers:'bgEmbers', image:'bgImage' };
  var BGFX_CANVAS = { waves:1, stars:1, network:1, embers:1 };
  var BGFX_MAX_CHARS = 600000;
  var bgfx = { raf:0, last:0, w:0, h:0, t:0, parts:[],
               rgb:[[59,130,246],[99,102,241],[56,189,248]] };

  function bgfxReadColors(){
    var cs = getComputedStyle(document.body);
    var cx = document.createElement('canvas').getContext('2d');
    var out = [];
    ['--accent', '--accent-2', '--accent-3'].forEach(function(v, i){
      var rgb = bgfx.rgb[i];
      var raw = (cs.getPropertyValue(v) || '').trim();
      if (raw && cx) {
        try {
          cx.fillStyle = '#000'; cx.fillStyle = raw;
          var f = String(cx.fillStyle), m;
          if ((m = /^#([0-9a-f]{6})$/i.exec(f))) {
            var n = parseInt(m[1], 16); rgb = [(n >> 16) & 255, (n >> 8) & 255, n & 255];
          } else if ((m = /^rgba?\((\d+),\s*(\d+),\s*(\d+)/.exec(f))) {
            rgb = [+m[1], +m[2], +m[3]];
          }
        } catch (e) {}
      }
      out.push(rgb);
    });
    bgfx.rgb = out;
  }
  function bgfxCol(i, a){ var c = bgfx.rgb[i % 3]; return 'rgba(' + c[0] + ',' + c[1] + ',' + c[2] + ',' + a + ')'; }
  function bgfxRand(a, b){ return a + Math.random() * (b - a); }
  function bgfxClamp(v, a, b){ return Math.max(a, Math.min(b, v)); }


  function bgfxSeed(){
    var w = bgfx.w, h = bgfx.h, st = BGFX_STYLE, n, i;
    bgfx.parts = [];
    if (st === 'stars') {
      n = bgfxClamp(Math.round(w * h / 9000), 60, 220);
      for (i = 0; i < n; i++) bgfx.parts.push({ x:Math.random()*w, y:Math.random()*h,
        r:bgfxRand(.4, 1.6), ph:Math.random()*6.28, sp:bgfxRand(.4, 1.6),
        vx:bgfxRand(.1, .5), c:Math.random() < .3 ? 2 : -1 });
    } else if (st === 'network') {
      n = bgfxClamp(Math.round(w * h / 22000), 30, 80);
      for (i = 0; i < n; i++) bgfx.parts.push({ x:Math.random()*w, y:Math.random()*h,
        vx:bgfxRand(-.3, .3), vy:bgfxRand(-.3, .3), r:bgfxRand(1.2, 2.4) });
    } else if (st === 'embers') {
      n = bgfxClamp(Math.round(w * h / 16000), 30, 90);
      for (i = 0; i < n; i++) bgfx.parts.push({ x:Math.random()*w, y:Math.random()*h,
        vy:bgfxRand(.25, .9), r:bgfxRand(.8, 2.6), ph:Math.random()*6.28,
        sw:bgfxRand(.3, 1.1), c:1 + (i % 2) });
    }
  }


  function bgfxResize(){
    var cv = document.getElementById('bgCanvas');
    bgfx.w = window.innerWidth; bgfx.h = window.innerHeight;
    cv.width = bgfx.w; cv.height = bgfx.h;
    bgfxSeed();
  }

  function bgfxFrame(ctx){
    var w = bgfx.w, h = bgfx.h, t = bgfx.t, st = BGFX_STYLE, i, p;
    ctx.clearRect(0, 0, w, h);
    if (st === 'stars') {
      for (i = 0; i < bgfx.parts.length; i++) {
        p = bgfx.parts[i];
        p.x -= p.vx * .35; if (p.x < -2) { p.x = w + 2; p.y = Math.random() * h; }
        var a = .25 + .65 * (.5 + .5 * Math.sin(t * p.sp + p.ph));
        ctx.fillStyle = p.c < 0 ? 'rgba(255,255,255,' + a + ')' : bgfxCol(p.c, a);
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(p.x, p.y, p.r, 0, 6.2832); ctx.fill();
      }
    } else if (st === 'network') {
      var lim = 130, d, dx, dy, j, q;
      for (i = 0; i < bgfx.parts.length; i++) {
        p = bgfx.parts[i];
        p.x += p.vx; p.y += p.vy;
        if (p.x < 0 || p.x > w) p.vx = -p.vx;
        if (p.y < 0 || p.y > h) p.vy = -p.vy;
      }
      ctx.lineWidth = 1;
      for (i = 0; i < bgfx.parts.length; i++) {
        p = bgfx.parts[i];
        for (j = i + 1; j < bgfx.parts.length; j++) {
          q = bgfx.parts[j]; dx = p.x - q.x; dy = p.y - q.y;
          if (dx > lim || dx < -lim || dy > lim || dy < -lim) continue;
          d = Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
          if (d < lim) {
            ctx.strokeStyle = bgfxCol(0, (1 - d / lim) * .38);
            ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(p.x, p.y); ctx.lineTo(q.x, q.y); ctx.stroke();
          }
        }
        ctx.fillStyle = bgfxCol(1, .7);
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(p.x, p.y, p.r, 0, 6.2832); ctx.fill();
      }
    } else if (st === 'embers') {
      ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'lighter';
      for (i = 0; i < bgfx.parts.length; i++) {
        p = bgfx.parts[i];
        p.y -= p.vy; p.x += Math.sin(t * p.sw + p.ph) * .4;
        if (p.y < -10) { p.y = h + 10; p.x = Math.random() * w; }
        var fade = bgfxClamp(p.y / h, 0, 1);
        var g = ctx.createRadialGradient(p.x, p.y, 0, p.x, p.y, p.r * 5);
        g.addColorStop(0, bgfxCol(p.c, .55 * fade + .1));
        g.addColorStop(1, bgfxCol(p.c, 0));
        ctx.fillStyle = g;
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(p.x, p.y, p.r * 5, 0, 6.2832); ctx.fill();
      }
      ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over';
    } else if (st === 'waves') {
      for (var k = 0; k < 3; k++) {
        var base = h * (.70 + .07 * k), amp = 16 + 8 * k, f = .0035 * (1 + .45 * k);
        var sp = .45 + .22 * k, x;
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(0, h);
        for (x = 0; x <= w + 8; x += 8) {
          ctx.lineTo(x, base + Math.sin(x * f + t * sp + k * 2) * amp + Math.sin(x * f * 2.7 - t * .8) * 6);
        }
        ctx.lineTo(w, h); ctx.closePath();
        var gr = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, base - amp, 0, h);
        gr.addColorStop(0, bgfxCol(k, .20 - k * .04)); gr.addColorStop(1, bgfxCol(k, .02));
        ctx.fillStyle = gr; ctx.fill();
      }
    }
  }


  /* 点击 banner 图片 / 空白处进入当前精选游戏的详情页
     按钮、轮播圆点、左右箭头都带 stopPropagation 或匹配 data-nodrag，不会触发这里。 */
  function heroOpenDetail(ev){
    if (!document.getElementById('hero')) return;   /* 登录页没有 banner */
    if (ev && ev.target && ev.target.closest && ev.target.closest('[data-nodrag]')) return;
    if (!HERO_GAME) return;
    if (typeof openDetail === 'function') openDetail(HERO_GAME);
  }

  function bgfxLoop(ts){
    bgfx.raf = requestAnimationFrame(bgfxLoop);
    if (ts - bgfx.last < 33) return;
    var dt = bgfx.last ? Math.min(.1, (ts - bgfx.last) / 1000) : .033;
    bgfx.last = ts; bgfx.t += dt;
    var cv = document.getElementById('bgCanvas');
    var ctx = cv && cv.getContext ? cv.getContext('2d') : null;
    if (ctx) bgfxFrame(ctx);
  }

  function bgfxStop(){
    if (bgfx.raf) { cancelAnimationFrame(bgfx.raf); bgfx.raf = 0; }
    var cv = document.getElementById('bgCanvas');
    var ctx = cv && cv.getContext ? cv.getContext('2d') : null;
    if (ctx) ctx.clearRect(0, 0, cv.width, cv.height);
  }



  function bgfxApply(){
    var body = document.body;
    var fx = document.getElementById('bgFx');
    var layer = document.getElementById('bgLayer');
    var cv = document.getElementById('bgCanvas');
    if (!fx || !layer || !cv) return;
    bgfxStop();
    if (!BGFX_ON) { body.setAttribute('data-bgfx', 'off'); fx.hidden = true; return; }
    var st = BGFX_STYLE;
    if (BGFX_STYLES.indexOf(st) < 0 || (st === 'image' && !BG_IMAGE)) st = 'aurora';
    BGFX_STYLE = st;
    body.setAttribute('data-bgfx', 'on');
    body.setAttribute('data-bgstyle', st);
    fx.hidden = false;
    layer.className = 'bgfx-layer ' + st;
    layer.style.backgroundImage = (st === 'image') ? 'url("' + BG_IMAGE + '")' : '';
    cv.style.display = BGFX_CANVAS[st] ? 'block' : 'none';
    if (BGFX_CANVAS[st] && !document.hidden) {
      bgfxReadColors(); bgfxResize(); bgfx.last = 0;
      bgfx.raf = requestAnimationFrame(bgfxLoop);
    }
  }


  window.addEventListener('resize', function(){
    if (BGFX_ON && BGFX_CANVAS[BGFX_STYLE] && bgfx.raf) bgfxResize();
  });
  document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', function(){ bgfxApply(); });
  try {
    new MutationObserver(function(){
      if (BGFX_ON && BGFX_CANVAS[BGFX_STYLE]) bgfxReadColors();
    }).observe(document.documentElement, { attributes:true, attributeFilter:['style', 'class', 'data-theme'] });
  } catch (e) {}
