    successMeta:function(t,e){return t==='LIFETIME'?'Acesso vitalício':t+' \u2014 '+e}
  }
};


/* 登录页的精选游戏数据（由宿主注入）*/
var LOGIN_DATA = __LOGIN_GAMES_JSON__;
var currentLang = (document.documentElement.getAttribute('lang') || 'en');
function setLang(code) {
  currentLang = code;
  var L = LANGS[code] || LANGS.en;
  document.getElementById('lBrandSub').textContent    = L.brandSub;
  document.getElementById('lHeadline').innerHTML      = L.headline;
  document.getElementById('lBlurb').textContent       = L.blurb;
  document.documentElement.lang = code;
  renderLoginGames();
}
function onUiLangChanged(code) {
  setLang(code);
  accApplyText();
  applyAdminGateText();          /* 提示语里的程序名/文案跟着语言走 */
  document.body.setAttribute('dir', code === 'ar' ? 'rtl' : 'ltr');
}
/* 左边展示的游戏 = games.txt 里标记为精选(featured)的游戏，按列表顺序 */
function loginFeatured(){
  var out = [];
  var d = (typeof LOGIN_DATA !== 'undefined' && LOGIN_DATA) ? LOGIN_DATA : null;
  if (!d || !d.order || !d.games) { return out; }
  d.order.forEach(function(code){
    var g = d.games[code];
    if (g && g.featured) { out.push(code); }
  });
  return out;
}
function loginGame(code){
  var d = (typeof LOGIN_DATA !== 'undefined' && LOGIN_DATA) ? LOGIN_DATA : null;
  return (d && d.games && d.games[code]) ? d.games[code] : {};
}
/* 背景图 = 主界面 banner 用的那张：全部 cover 里第一张非视频图，再退回 icon
   与 dashboard 里的 g.poster 选法完全一致，两个界面的图才不会对不上。 */
function loginIsVideo(u){
  return /\.(mp4|webm|ogv|m4v|mov)(\?.*)?$/i.test(u || '');
}
function loginShot(code){
  var g = loginGame(code);
  var c = g.covers || [];
  var list = c.slice();
  list.push(g.img);
  for (var i = 0; i < list.length; i++) {
    if (list[i] && !loginIsVideo(list[i])) { return list[i]; }
  }
  return g.img || '';
}
function loginTileLabel(code){
  var g = loginGame(code);
  return String(g.short || g.title || code).toUpperCase();
}
/* 预载每张背景图，切换时不会闪白 */
function loginPreload(codes){
  for (var i = 0; i < codes.length; i++) {
    var img = new Image();
    img.src = loginShot(codes[i]);
  }
}
/* ── 背景层：只有 bg0 / bg1 两层，轮流当“当前层” ─────────────────────────
     切页时把目标图铺到隐藏的那层再淡入，另一层淡出。
   （旧写法只给前两张赋图、却按 index 去取 bg2/bg3，所以第 3 个游戏开始就是空白） */
var loginBgCur = -1;      /* 当前可见层：0 / 1；-1 = 还没初始化 */
var loginBgCode = '';     /* 当前层显示的遊戲代号，避免同一张重复淡入 */
function loginBgApply(idx, codes){
  codes = codes || loginFeatured();
  if (!codes.length) { return; }
  if (!(idx >= 0 && idx < codes.length)) { idx = 0; }
  var code = codes[idx];
  if (code === loginBgCode && loginBgCur >= 0) { return; }
  var src = loginShot(code);
  var css = src ? 'url("' + src.replace(/"/g, '%22') + '")' : '';

  if (loginBgCur < 0) {                    /* 首次：直接铺第一层，不做淡入 */
    var first = document.getElementById('bg0');
    var second = document.getElementById('bg1');
    if (first) { first.style.backgroundImage = css; first.classList.add('visible'); }
    if (second) { second.classList.remove('visible'); second.style.backgroundImage = ''; }
    loginBgCur = 0;
  } else {
    var next = (loginBgCur + 1) % 2;
    var el = document.getElementById('bg' + next);
    var prev = document.getElementById('bg' + loginBgCur);
    if (!el) { return; }
    el.style.backgroundImage = css;
    el.classList.remove('visible');
    void el.offsetWidth;                  /* 先让新图落位，再淡入 */
    el.classList.add('visible');
    if (prev) { prev.classList.remove('visible'); }
    loginBgCur = next;
  }
  loginBgCode = code;
}
/* 只预载「当前 + 下一张（+ 上一张）」：精选可能有 7~8 个，
   全部预载会一次性下载+解码 7 张 1920x1080 大图（几十 MB 内存 + 抢带宽，
   反而拖慢首屏）。轮播只会用到下一张，所以这样就够顺滑了。 */
function loginPreloadAround(codes, idx){
  var n = codes.length;
  if (!n) { return; }
  var want = [idx, (idx + 1) % n];
  if (n > 2) { want.push((idx - 1 + n) % n); }
  for (var i = 0; i < want.length; i++) {
    var u = loginShot(codes[want[i]]);
    if (!u) { continue; }
    var img = new Image();
    img.decoding = 'async';
    img.src = u;
  }
}
/* 生成底部圆点指示器 */
function renderLoginDots(codes, active){
  var box = document.getElementById('gameTabs');
  if (!box) { return; }
  var html = "";
  codes.forEach(function(code, i){
    html += '<button type="button" class="gdot' + (i === active ? ' active' : '') + '"' +
      ' title="' + uiEsc(loginTileLabel(code)) + '"' +
      ' aria-label="' + uiEsc(loginTileLabel(code)) + '"' +
      ' onclick="switchGame(' + i + ')"></button>';
  });
  box.innerHTML = html;
}
function renderLoginGames(){
  var codes = loginFeatured();
  var box = document.getElementById('gameTabs');
  if (!box) { return; }
  if (!codes.length) { box.innerHTML = ""; return; }
  var active = Math.min(curGame, codes.length - 1);
  loginBgApply(active, codes);          /* 先把当前那张铺上，再后台预载相邻图 */
  loginPreloadAround(codes, active);
  renderLoginDots(codes, active);
  applyLoginText(codes[active]);
}
/* 自动翻页 */
var loginAuto = { timer: 0, hold: 0 };
var LOGIN_ROTATE_MS = 7000;
function loginAutoStop(){ if (loginAuto.timer) { clearInterval(loginAuto.timer); loginAuto.timer = 0; } }
function loginAutoStart(){
  loginAutoStop();
  if (loginFeatured().length < 2) { return; }
  loginAuto.timer = setInterval(function(){
    if (document.hidden) { return; }
    var n = loginFeatured().length;
    if (n < 2) { return; }
    switchGame((curGame + 1) % n);
  }, LOGIN_ROTATE_MS);
}
/* 登录页初始化：套用语言并生成精选游戏缩略图 */
function loginBoot(){
  setLang(currentLang);
  renderLoginGames();
  loginAutoStart();
  /* 向宿主要已保存的账号：用户名总是保存，密码只在勾选「记住我」后保存 */
  wv('GET_SAVED_PASS');
}
if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
  document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', loginBoot);
} else { loginBoot(); }
var curGame = 0;
function applyLoginText(code){
  var L = LANGS[currentLang] || LANGS.en;
  var g = loginGame(code);
  var em = /<em>.*<\/em>/.exec(L.headline);
  var name = g.short || g.title || code;
  document.getElementById('lHeadline').innerHTML = uiEsc(name) + '<br>' + (em ? em[0] : '<em></em>');
  var tbl = UI_T[currentLang] || UI_T.en;
  document.getElementById('lBlurb').textContent = (g.featured && tbl.dlBlurb) ? tbl.dlBlurb : (L.blurb || '');
  var bs = L.brandSub || "";
  document.getElementById('lBrandSub').textContent = bs;
}
function switchGame(idx) {
  var codes = loginFeatured();
  var n = codes.length;
  if (n < 1) { return; }
  idx = ((idx % n) + n) % n;
  var first = (idx === curGame);
  curGame = idx;
  loginBgApply(idx, codes);
  loginPreloadAround(codes, idx);      /* 提前备好下一张，翻页不会闪白 */
  renderLoginDots(codes, idx);
  applyLoginText(codes[idx]);
  if (!first) {
    ['lHeadline', 'lBlurb'].forEach(function(id){
      var el = document.getElementById(id);
      if (!el) { return; }
      el.classList.remove('swap-in');
      void el.offsetWidth;
      el.classList.add('swap-in');
    });
  }
}
function wv(m) { window.chrome.webview.postMessage(m); }
var accMode_ = 'login', accBusy = false;
var ACC_USER_RE = /^[A-Za-z0-9_.-]{3,20}$/;
var ACC_MAIL_RE = /^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/;
var ACC_FIELDS = ['accUser', 'accPass', 'accEmail'];
/* 加载中还是写在按钮下面；错误/成功一律走提示窗口（见 showErr / sovl） */
function showStatus(type, msg) {
  var el = document.getElementById('status');
  el.className = 'status-msg ' + type + ' show';
  el.textContent = msg;
}
/* 登录/注册失败：弹主题化提示窗口 */
function showErr(msg) {
  var o = document.getElementById('errOvl');
  var b = document.getElementById('errBody');
  var st = document.getElementById('status');
  if (st) { st.className = 'status-msg'; st.textContent = ''; }
  if (!o || !b) { return; }
  /* 别让键盘输入继续落到弹层后面的输入框上 */
  try { if (document.activeElement && document.activeElement.blur) { document.activeElement.blur(); } } catch (e) {}
  b.textContent = msg || uiT('accErrFail');
  o.classList.add('show');
}
function closeErrOvl() {
  var o = document.getElementById('errOvl');
  if (o) { o.classList.remove('show'); }
}
function errOvlShown() {
  var o = document.getElementById('errOvl');
  return !!(o && o.classList.contains('show'));
}
function accApplyText() {
  var reg = accMode_ === 'reg';
  document.getElementById('lFormTitle').textContent = uiT(reg ? 'accRegTitle' : 'accLoginTitle');
  document.getElementById('lFormHint').textContent  = uiT(reg ? 'accRegHint'  : 'accLoginHint');
  document.getElementById('lKeyLabel').textContent  = uiT('accUsername');
  document.getElementById('lActivate').textContent  = uiT(reg ? 'accRegBtn'   : 'accLoginBtn');
}
function accClearErr() {
  ACC_FIELDS.forEach(function(id){
    var e = document.getElementById(id);
    if (e) { e.classList.remove('is-err', 'is-ok'); }
  });
  var st = document.getElementById('status');
  st.className = 'status-msg'; st.textContent = '';
}
function accMark(id) { var e = document.getElementById(id); if (e) { e.classList.add('is-err'); } }
function accSetBusy(b) {
  accBusy = b;
  var btn = document.getElementById('mbtn');
  btn.classList.toggle('loading', b); btn.disabled = b;
  ACC_FIELDS.forEach(function(id){ var e = document.getElementById(id); if (e) { e.disabled = b; } });
}
function accSetMode(m) {
  if (accBusy) { return; }
  accMode_ = (m === 'reg') ? 'reg' : 'login';
  var reg = accMode_ === 'reg';
  document.getElementById('accTabs').classList.toggle('reg', reg);
  document.getElementById('accTabLogin').classList.toggle('active', !reg);
  document.getElementById('accTabReg').classList.toggle('active', reg);
  document.getElementById('accEmailField').hidden = !reg;   /* 邮箱只在注册时出现 */
  document.getElementById('remRow').hidden = reg;      /* 注册页不显示「记住我」 */
  document.querySelector('.right').classList.toggle('reg', reg);
  document.getElementById('accPass').setAttribute('autocomplete', reg ? 'new-password' : 'current-password');
  accClearErr();
  accApplyText();
}
function accTogglePw() {
  var a = document.getElementById('accPass');
  if (!a) { return; }
  a.type = (a.type === 'password') ? 'text' : 'password';
}
/* 「记住我」：取消勾选立刻让宿主忘掉已保存的密码 */
function accRememberChanged(){
  var box = document.getElementById('accRemember');
  if (box && !box.checked) { wv('FORGET_PASS'); }
}
function accRememberOn(){
  var c = document.getElementById('accRemember');
  return !!(c && c.checked);
}
/* ── 必须以管理员身份运行：点登录时用主题化弹层提示 ── */
function isAdminRun(){
  return !(typeof UI_CFG !== 'undefined' && UI_CFG && UI_CFG.isAdmin === false);
}
/* 程序名以实际 exe 为准（宿主注入），不写死 */
function exeDisplayName(){
  var n = (typeof UI_CFG !== 'undefined' && UI_CFG && UI_CFG.exeName) ? String(UI_CFG.exeName) : '';
  return n || 'STARLINE';
}
function applyAdminGateText(){
  var exe = exeDisplayName();
  var t = document.getElementById('adminTitle');
  var b = document.getElementById('adminBody');
  var h = document.getElementById('gateExe');
  if (t) { t.textContent = uiT('adminTitle'); }
  /* 文案里的 {exe} 换成真实文件名 */
  if (b) { b.textContent = String(uiT('adminBody')).replace('{exe}', exe); }
  if (h) { h.textContent = exe; }
}
function adminGateShown(){
  var o = document.getElementById('adminOvl');
  return !!(o && o.classList.contains('show'));
}
function openAdminGate(){
  var o = document.getElementById('adminOvl');
  if (!o) { return; }
  var st = document.getElementById('status');
  st.className = 'status-msg'; st.textContent = '';
  applyAdminGateText();
  o.classList.add('show');
}
function closeAdminGate(){
  var o = document.getElementById('adminOvl');
  if (o) { o.classList.remove('show'); }
}
/* 点「确定」：通知宿主关闭程序 */
function adminGateExit(){
  wv('CLOSE');
}
document.addEventListener('keydown', function(e){
  if (e.key !== 'Escape' && e.key !== 'Esc') { return; }
  if (errOvlShown())     { closeErrOvl(); return; }
  if (adminGateShown())  { closeAdminGate(); }
});
function accSubmit() {
  if (accBusy) { return; }
  if (errOvlShown()) { closeErrOvl(); }   /* 又点了一次：把上次的提示窗口收起来再试 */
  /* 不是管理员：不发登录请求，直接弹主题提示（确定=退出，稍后=返回） */
  if (!isAdminRun()) { openAdminGate(); return; }
  var reg = accMode_ === 'reg';
  var u = document.getElementById('accUser').value.trim();
  var p = document.getElementById('accPass').value;
  if (!u || !p) {
    showErr(uiT('accErrEmpty'));
    if (!u) { accMark('accUser'); }
    if (!p) { accMark('accPass'); }
    return;
  }
  if (!ACC_USER_RE.test(u)) { showErr(uiT('accErrUserFmt')); accMark('accUser'); return; }
  var mail = '';
  if (reg) {
    mail = document.getElementById('accEmail').value.trim();
    if (p.length < 8) { showErr(uiT('accErrPassShort')); accMark('accPass'); return; }
    if (!ACC_MAIL_RE.test(mail)) { showErr(uiT('accErrEmail')); accMark('accEmail'); return; }
  }
  accSetBusy(true);
  showStatus('ld', uiT(reg ? 'accCreating' : 'accSigningIn'));
  /* 账号验证走网站（www.cata1y3t.boats/api/auth.php）：
     注册：用户名 邮箱 密码；登录：用户名 密码 + 是否记住密码 */
  wv((reg ? 'ACC_REG:' : 'ACC_LOGIN:') + u + String.fromCharCode(31) + p
     + String.fromCharCode(31) + (reg ? mail : (accRememberOn() ? '1' : '0')));
}
window.chrome.webview.addEventListener('message', function(e) {
  var msg = e.data;
  if (typeof msg !== 'string') { return; }
  if (msg.indexOf('SAVED_PASS:') === 0) {
    /* 宿主回传的已保存账号：用户名总是填，密码只在「记住我」保存过时填 */
    var sp = msg.slice(11).split(String.fromCharCode(31));
    var su = sp[0] || '', sw = sp[1] || '';
    var uEl = document.getElementById('accUser'), pEl = document.getElementById('accPass');
    if (uEl && su) { uEl.value = su; }
    var box = document.getElementById('accRemember');
    if (pEl && sw) { pEl.value = sw; if (box) { box.checked = true; } }
    else if (box && !su) { box.checked = false; }
  }
  else if (msg.indexOf('ACC_OK:') === 0) {
    var parts = msg.slice(7).split('|'), name = parts[0], kind = parts[1];
    ACC_FIELDS.forEach(function(id){ var f = document.getElementById(id); if (f) { f.classList.add('is-ok'); } });
    document.getElementById('sTitle').textContent = uiT(kind === 'new' ? 'accCreated' : 'accWelcome');
    var meta = document.getElementById('sMeta');
    meta.innerHTML = '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><circle cx="12" cy="8" r="4"/><path d="M4 21v-1a6 6 0 0 1 6-6h4a6 6 0 0 1 6 6v1"/></svg><span></span>';
    meta.lastChild.textContent = name;
    document.getElementById('sovl').classList.add('show');
    setTimeout(function(){ wv('LAUNCH'); }, 1600);
  } else if (msg.indexOf('ACC_ERRTEXT:') === 0) {
    /* 网站直接回的错误文案，原样显示在提示窗口里 */
    accSetBusy(false);
    showErr(msg.slice(12) || uiT('accErrFail'));
  } else if (msg.indexOf('ACC_ERR:') === 0) {
    accSetBusy(false);
    var code = msg.slice(8);
    var map = { EMPTY: 'accErrEmpty', USER_FMT: 'accErrUserFmt', PASS_SHORT: 'accErrPassShort',
                EMAIL_FMT: 'accErrEmail', EXISTS: 'accErrExists', BAD_CRED: 'accErrBad',
                BANNED: 'accErrBanned', SUSPENDED: 'accErrSuspended', HWID: 'accErrHwid',
                HWID_PENDING: 'accErrHwidPending', NET: 'errNet', AUTH: 'errAuth' };
    showErr(uiT(map[code] || 'accErrFail'));
    if (code === 'EXISTS' || code === 'USER_FMT') { accMark('accUser'); }
    if (code === 'BAD_CRED') { accMark('accUser'); accMark('accPass'); }
    if (code === 'EMAIL_FMT') { accMark('accEmail'); }
  }
});
uiInit();
/* 账号存在网站上，不存在「本机有没有账号」这回事：一律先显示登录，
   新用户点上面的 Register 切过去 */
accSetMode('login');
applyAdminGateText();   /* i18n 跑完后再套一次真实程序名 */
bgfxApply();
</script>

<script>
__UI_SCRIPT__


  var LICENSES = __LICENSES_JSON__;
  var ACTIVE_GAME = (LICENSES && LICENSES.active) || '';

  /* 游戏列表 & 顺序由宿主从 games.txt 注入 */
  var GAMES = __GAMES_JSON__;
  var ORDER = __GAMES_ORDER_JSON__;

  /* 精选 banner：只收录 games.txt 里 featured = true 的游戏，顺序沿用文件顺序；
     一个都没标（或标注的游戏不存在）时回退成全部游戏，避免 banner 空白。 */
  var HERO_ORDER = (function(){
    var picked = ORDER.filter(function(g){
      return !!(GAMES[g] && GAMES[g].featured);
    });
    return picked.length ? picked : ORDER.slice();
  })();
  var HERO_GAME = HERO_ORDER[0] || '';
  var HERO_POS = 0;
  var CURRENT_GAME = '';
  var FILTER = 'all';
  var SEARCH = '';
  var DETAIL_IDX = 0;
  var HERO_TIMER = null;

  function isVideoUrl(u){ return /\.(mp4|webm|ogv|m4v|mov)(\?.*)?$/i.test(u || ''); }

  /* 给每个游戏补一个 poster（不能是视频）：扫全部 cover，再退回 icon */
  function normalizeGames(){
    Object.keys(GAMES).forEach(function(k){
      var g = GAMES[k];
      if (!g.covers) g.covers = [];
      var list = g.covers.slice();
      list.push(g.img);
      g.poster = list.filter(function(u){ return u && !isVideoUrl(u); })[0] || g.img;
      if (!g.gen)  g.gen  = [];
      if (!g.feat) g.feat = [];
      if (!g.pub)  g.pub  = g.dev || '';
      if (!g.rel)  g.rel  = 'TBA';
    });
  }
  normalizeGames();

  /* 宿主在后台拉到新的 games.txt 时会推一份过来：就地换掉并重刷界面，不用重启 */
  function applyGamesUpdate(json, orderCsv){
    var next = null;
    try { next = (typeof json === 'string') ? JSON.parse(json) : json; } catch (e) { next = null; }
    if (!next || typeof next !== 'object') return false;
    var nextOrder = [];
    if (orderCsv) {
      String(orderCsv).split(',').forEach(function(x){ if (x) nextOrder.push(x); });
    }
    if (!nextOrder.length) nextOrder = Object.keys(next);

    GAMES = next;
    ORDER = nextOrder;
    normalizeGames();
    HERO_ORDER = (function(){
      var picked = ORDER.filter(function(g){ return !!(GAMES[g] && GAMES[g].featured); });
      return picked.length ? picked : ORDER.slice();
    })();
    HERO_POS = 0;
    HERO_GAME = HERO_ORDER[0] || '';
    /* 用户自己拖出来的顺序保留，新游戏排到后面 */
    var keep = [];
    GRID_ORDER.forEach(function(g){ if (GAMES[g] && keep.indexOf(g) < 0) keep.push(g); });
    ORDER.forEach(function(g){ if (keep.indexOf(g) < 0) keep.push(g); });
    GRID_ORDER = keep;

    if (typeof gpopHide === 'function') gpopHide();
    renderHero();
    renderGrid();
    renderQuickplay();
    renderGenreBtn();
    renderGenrePop();
    if (CURRENT_GAME && !GAMES[CURRENT_GAME]) closeDetail();
    if (typeof PANE !== 'undefined' && PANE.code && !GAMES[PANE.code]) paneHide();
    toast(uiT('gamesUpdated'), 'ok', 2600);
    return true;
  }


  var USER = (function(){
    try {
      var raw = localStorage.getItem('starline.user');
      if (raw) return JSON.parse(raw);
    } catch (e) {}
    return { name: (UI_CFG && UI_CFG.account) || 'STARLINE User', avatar: '', dpi: 100, lang: '' };
  })();

  function doLogout(){ wv('ACC_LOGOUT'); }

  function saveUser(){
    try { localStorage.setItem('starline.user', JSON.stringify(USER)); }
    catch (e) {}
  }

  var WISHLIST = (function(){
    try {
      if (typeof UI_CFG !== 'undefined' && UI_CFG && Array.isArray(UI_CFG.wishlist))
        return UI_CFG.wishlist.slice();
    } catch (e) {}
    return [];
  })();


  function saveWishlist(){ wv('UI_WISH:' + WISHLIST.join(',')); }
  function isWished(g){ return WISHLIST.indexOf(g) >= 0; }

  /* ── 媒体轮播（网格浮层 + 列表右侧详情块共用一套逻辑）──────────────────
     图片每 3 秒自动换一张；视频不受 3 秒管，播完（或出错）再换；不能手动切。 */
  function carMake(shotId, videoId, dotsId){
    var ctx = { shot:shotId, video:videoId, dots:dotsId, code:'', items:[], idx:0, timer:0, on:false };
    var vid = document.getElementById(videoId);
    if (vid) {
      vid.addEventListener('ended', function(){ if (ctx.on) carNext(ctx); });
      vid.addEventListener('error', function(){ if (ctx.on) carNext(ctx); });
    }
    return ctx;
  }
  function carStop(ctx){
    if (!ctx) return;
    clearTimeout(ctx.timer);
    ctx.timer = 0;
    ctx.on = false;
    var vid = document.getElementById(ctx.video);
    if (vid) {
      try { vid.pause(); } catch (e) {}
      vid.removeAttribute('src');
      vid.classList.remove('on');
      try { vid.load(); } catch (e) {}
    }
    var img = document.getElementById(ctx.shot);
    if (img) { img.classList.remove('on'); img.style.backgroundImage = ''; }
    var dots = document.getElementById(ctx.dots);
    if (dots) dots.innerHTML = '';
  }
  function carNext(ctx){
    if (!ctx || !ctx.on || ctx.items.length < 2) return;
    ctx.idx = (ctx.idx + 1) % ctx.items.length;
    carPaint(ctx);
  }
  function carPaint(ctx){
    if (!ctx || !ctx.on) return;
    clearTimeout(ctx.timer);
    ctx.timer = 0;                  /* 视频那一档不挂计时器，这里必须是 0 */
    var url = ctx.items[ctx.idx] || '';
    var img = document.getElementById(ctx.shot);
    var vid = document.getElementById(ctx.video);
    var dots = document.getElementById(ctx.dots);
    var isVid = url && typeof isVideoUrl === 'function' && isVideoUrl(url);
    var token = ctx.idx;
    function loadDone(box){ if (box) box.classList.remove('loading'); }
    if (dots) {
      var dh = '';
      for (var i = 0; i < ctx.items.length; i++)
        dh += '<i class="' + (i === ctx.idx ? 'on' : '') + '"></i>';
      dots.innerHTML = ctx.items.length > 1 ? dh : '';
    }
    if (isVid) {
      if (img) { img.classList.remove('on'); img.style.backgroundImage = ''; }
      if (vid) {
        var vbox = vid.parentNode;
        if (vbox) vbox.classList.add('loading');
        if (vid.getAttribute('src') !== url) vid.setAttribute('src', url);
        try { vid.currentTime = 0; } catch (e) {}
        var mark = function(){ if (token === ctx.idx) { vid.classList.add('on'); loadDone(vbox); } };
        vid.addEventListener('loadeddata', mark);
        vid.addEventListener('canplay', mark);
        vid.addEventListener('error', mark);
        var pr = vid.play();
        if (pr && pr.catch) pr.catch(function(){});
      }
    } else {
      if (vid) { try { vid.pause(); } catch (e) {} vid.classList.remove('on'); }
      if (img) {
        var ibox = img.parentNode;
        if (ibox) ibox.classList.add('loading');     /* 加载期间显示流光 */
        (function (el, box, u, tk) {
          var pre = new Image();
          var done = function(){
            if (tk !== ctx.idx) return;              /* 已经切到别的媒体了 */
            el.style.backgroundImage = "url('" + u + "')";
            el.classList.add('on');
            loadDone(box);
          };
          pre.onload = done;
          pre.onerror = done;
          pre.src = u;
        })(img, ibox, url, token);
      }
      if (ctx.items.length > 1) ctx.timer = setTimeout(function(){ carNext(ctx); }, 3000);
    }
  }
  function carStart(ctx, code, items){
    if (!ctx) return;
    ctx.code = code || '';
    ctx.items = items || [];
    ctx.idx = 0;
    ctx.on = ctx.items.length > 0;
    carPaint(ctx);
  }
  /* 某款游戏详情页那套媒体；一个都没有就退回海报/图标 */
  function mediaItems(g){
    var items = (typeof detailCovers === 'function') ? detailCovers(g) : [];
    if (!items.length) {
      var m = GAMES[g] || {};
      if (m.poster) items = [m.poster];
      else if (m.img) items = [m.img];
    }
    return items;
  }
  function metaBits(meta, code){
    var bits = [];
    if (meta.dev) bits.push(meta.dev);
    var gens = (meta.gen || []).slice(0, 3).map(function(x){ return tagT(x); }).join(' · ');
    if (gens) bits.push(gens);
    if (meta.rel) bits.push(relT(meta.rel));
    return bits.join('  ·  ') || code || '';
  }
  function statsInto(code, weekId, totalId){
    var m = GAMES[code] || {};
    var w = document.getElementById(weekId), t = document.getElementById(totalId);
    var wk = Math.max(0, Number(m.runsWeek) || 0);
    var tt = Math.max(0, Number(m.runs) || 0);
    if (w) { w.textContent = String(wk); w.classList.toggle('hot', wk > 0); }
    if (t) t.textContent = String(tt);
  }

  /* ── 网格模式：悬停卡片浮出的预览 ─────────────────────────────────────── */
  var GPOP = { code:'', showT:0, hideT:0, on:false, car:null };

  function gpopNode(id){ return document.getElementById(id); }

  function gpopHide(){
    clearTimeout(GPOP.showT); clearTimeout(GPOP.hideT);
    GPOP.on = false; GPOP.code = '';
    var p = gpopNode('gpop');
    if (p) p.classList.remove('show');
    if (GPOP.car) carStop(GPOP.car);
  }

  function gpopPlace(card){
    var p = gpopNode('gpop');
    if (!p || !card) return;
    var r = card.getBoundingClientRect();
    var pw = p.offsetWidth || 320;
    var ph = p.offsetHeight || Math.max(180, r.height);
    var vw = window.innerWidth, vh = window.innerHeight;
    var x = r.right + 14;
    if (x + pw > vw - 10) {
      x = r.left - 14 - pw;
      if (x < 10) x = Math.max(10, Math.min(vw - pw - 10, r.left + (r.width - pw) / 2));
    }
    /* 高度和卡片一样，所以顶部直接对齐卡片 */
    var y = Math.max(10, Math.min(vh - ph - 10, r.top));
    p.style.left = Math.round(x) + 'px';
    p.style.top = Math.round(y) + 'px';
  }

  function gpopShow(card){
    if (!card) return;
    if (LAYOUT === 'list') return;                      /* 列表模式用右侧那块，不弹浮层 */
    if (typeof DND !== 'undefined' && DND) return;      /* 卡片拖拽中不弹 */
    if (typeof SDND !== 'undefined' && SDND) return;
    var code = card.getAttribute('data-game');
    if (!code || !GAMES[code]) return;
    var items = mediaItems(code);
    if (!items.length) return;

    GPOP.code = code;
    GPOP.on = true;
    var meta = GAMES[code] || {};
    var tEl = gpopNode('gpopTitle'), sEl = gpopNode('gpopSub');
    if (tEl) tEl.textContent = meta.title || code;
    if (sEl) sEl.textContent = metaBits(meta, code);
    statsInto(code, 'gpopWeek', 'gpopTotal');
    carStart(GPOP.car, code, items);
    var p = gpopNode('gpop');
    p.classList.add('show');    /* 先上屏才能量到真实宽高；同一帧内完成不会闪 */
    /* 高度跟卡片一致；卡片矮（每行 3/4/5/6 张）时切到紧凑版，别让内容溢出 */
    var h = Math.max(180, Math.round(card.getBoundingClientRect().height));
    p.style.height = h + 'px';
    p.classList.toggle('compact', h < 300);
    p.classList.toggle('tiny', h < 236);
    gpopPlace(card);
  }
  /* 启动成功后宿主会回新的计数，这里就地刷新 */
  function gpopRefreshStats(code){
    if (GPOP.on && code && GPOP.code === code) statsInto(code, 'gpopWeek', 'gpopTotal');
    if (PANE.code && code && PANE.code === code) statsInto(code, 'paneWeek', 'paneTotal');
  }

  /* ── 列表模式：右侧常驻的详情块（照 Steam 商店列表那样）────────────────
     鼠标停在哪一行就显示哪一行；移开列表后保持“最后一次悬停”的内容
     （第一次进列表、还没悬停过时显示第一个游戏）。
     媒体不做轮换：一张图/视频一行，全部列出来，没加载完有流光动画。 */
  var PANE = { code:'' };

  function paneMedia(code){
    var box = gpopNode('paneMedia');
    if (!box) return;
    var items = mediaItems(code);
    box.innerHTML = '';
    for (var i = 0; i < items.length; i++) {
      var u = items[i];
      if (typeof isVideoUrl === 'function' && isVideoUrl(u)) {
        var v = document.createElement('video');
        v.className = 'm';
        v.muted = true; v.loop = true; v.defaultMuted = true;
        v.setAttribute('muted', '');
        v.setAttribute('playsinline', '');
        v.preload = 'metadata';
        v.addEventListener('loadeddata', function(){ this.classList.add('loaded'); });
        v.addEventListener('canplay',    function(){ this.classList.add('loaded'); });
        v.addEventListener('error',      function(){ this.classList.add('loaded'); });
        v.src = u;
        box.appendChild(v);
        var pr = v.play();
        if (pr && pr.catch) pr.catch(function(){});
      } else {
        var d = document.createElement('div');
        d.className = 'm';
        box.appendChild(d);
        (function (el, url) {
          var pre = new Image();
          var done = function(){ el.style.backgroundImage = "url('" + url + "')"; el.classList.add('loaded'); };
          pre.onload = done;
          pre.onerror = done;
          pre.src = url;
        })(d, u);
      }
    }
    box.scrollTop = 0;
  }
  function paneFill(code){
    var box = gpopNode('gamePane');
    if (!box || !code || !GAMES[code]) return;
    if (PANE.code === code && !box.hidden) return;      /* 同一款游戏不用重画 */
    PANE.code = code;
    var meta = GAMES[code] || {};
    var tEl = gpopNode('paneTitle'), sEl = gpopNode('paneSub');
    if (tEl) tEl.textContent = meta.title || code;
    if (sEl) sEl.textContent = metaBits(meta, code);
    statsInto(code, 'paneWeek', 'paneTotal');
    paneMedia(code);
  }
  /* 把右侧块撑到当前可用高度（滚动/改变窗口大小都重算）：媒体区吃掉多余空间 */
  function paneFit(){
    var box = gpopNode('gamePane');
    if (!box || box.hidden) return;
    var top = box.getBoundingClientRect().top;
    var avail = window.innerHeight - top - 12;
    var h = Math.max(380, Math.min(avail, window.innerHeight - 20));
    box.style.height = Math.round(h) + 'px';
  }
  var paneFitRaf = 0;
  function paneFitSoon(){
    if (paneFitRaf) return;
    paneFitRaf = requestAnimationFrame(function(){ paneFitRaf = 0; paneFit(); });
  }
  function paneShow(firstCode){
    var box = gpopNode('gamePane');
    if (!box) return;
    if (!firstCode || !GAMES[firstCode]) { paneHide(); return; }
    box.hidden = false;
    paneFill(firstCode);
    paneFit();
    paneFitSoon();
  }
  function paneHide(){
    var box = gpopNode('gamePane');
    if (box) box.hidden = true;
    var media = gpopNode('paneMedia');
    if (media) {
      var vids = media.querySelectorAll('video');
      for (var v = 0; v < vids.length; v++) { try { vids[v].pause(); } catch (e) {} }
      media.innerHTML = '';
    }
    PANE.code = '';
  }

  function gpopBind(){
    GPOP.car = carMake('gpopShot', 'gpopVideo', 'gpopDots');
    var grid = document.getElementById('grid');
    if (!grid || grid.getAttribute('data-gpop') === '1') return;
    grid.setAttribute('data-gpop', '1');
    grid.addEventListener('mouseover', function(e){
      var card = e.target && e.target.closest ? e.target.closest('.card[data-game]') : null;
      if (!card) return;
      var code = card.getAttribute('data-game');
      if (LAYOUT === 'list') {                   /* 列表：右侧那块换成这一行 */
        if (code && code !== PANE.code) paneFill(code);
        return;
      }
      if (GPOP.on && GPOP.code === code) { clearTimeout(GPOP.hideT); return; }
      clearTimeout(GPOP.hideT);
      clearTimeout(GPOP.showT);
      GPOP.showT = setTimeout(function(){ gpopShow(card); }, 220);   /* 路过不弹，停一下才弹 */
    });
    grid.addEventListener('mouseout', function(e){
      var card = e.target && e.target.closest ? e.target.closest('.card[data-game]') : null;
      if (!card) return;
      var to = e.relatedTarget;
      if (to && to.closest && to.closest('.card[data-game]') === card) return;
      if (LAYOUT === 'list') return;             /* 列表：移开后保持最后一次悬停的内容 */
      clearTimeout(GPOP.showT);
      GPOP.hideT = setTimeout(gpopHide, 120);
    });
    var page = document.getElementById('storePage');
    if (page) page.addEventListener('scroll', function(){
      if (GPOP.on) gpopHide();
      if (LAYOUT === 'list') paneFitSoon();
    }, { passive: true });
    window.addEventListener('resize', function(){ if (LAYOUT === 'list') paneFitSoon(); });
    document.addEventListener('pointerdown', function(){ if (GPOP.on) gpopHide(); }, true);
    window.addEventListener('blur', function(){ if (GPOP.on) gpopHide(); });
  }
  /* 列表里第一个（当前筛选后能看到的）游戏代号 */
  function gridFirstCode(){
    var first = document.querySelector('#grid .card[data-game]');
    return first ? first.getAttribute('data-game') : '';
  }

  function toggleWish(ev, g){
    if (ev) ev.stopPropagation();
    if (!g) return;
    var i = WISHLIST.indexOf(g);
    var added = false;
    if (i >= 0) {
      WISHLIST.splice(i, 1);
      toast(uiT('wishRemoved'), 'info', 2000);
    } else {
      WISHLIST.push(g);
      added = true;
      toast(uiT('wishAdded'), 'ok', 2000);
    }
    saveWishlist();
    renderHeroWish();
    renderGrid();
    if (CURRENT_GAME) renderDetailWish();
    if (added) {
      var btn = document.querySelector('.card-wish[data-game="' + g + '"]');
      if (btn) {
        btn.classList.add('pop');
        setTimeout(function(){ btn.classList.remove('pop'); }, 600);
      }
    }
  }



  function renderDetailWish(){
    var btn = document.getElementById('detailWish');
    var txt = document.getElementById('detailWishText');
    if (!btn || !txt) return;
    if (isWished(CURRENT_GAME)) {
      btn.classList.add('wished');
      txt.textContent = uiT('wishlisted');
    } else {
      btn.classList.remove('wished');
      txt.textContent = uiT('wishlist');
    }
  }

  function wv(m){
    try { window.chrome.webview.postMessage(m); }
    catch (e) {}
  }

  function licOf(g){
    return (LICENSES && LICENSES.games && LICENSES.games[g]) || null;
  }
  function hasLic(g){
    var l = licOf(g);
    return !!l && !!l.type;
  }

  /* 名下有任意一个还在有效期内的订阅（宿主给的 LICENSES 里只会有有效授权） */
  function hasActiveSub(){
    if (!LICENSES || !LICENSES.games) { return false; }
    for (var g in LICENSES.games) {
      if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(LICENSES.games, g) && hasLic(g)) { return true; }
    }
    return false;
  }
  /* 个人资料头像旁的 PRO 徽标：有订阅才显示 */
  function updateProfileBadges(){
    var box = document.getElementById('profileBadges');
    if (!box) { return; }
    box.hidden = !hasActiveSub();
  }

  /* 单游戏显示价（USD）：取 games.txt 里最便宜的那档（7 → 15 → 30 → 90 → 永久），
     都没写就回退默认价。**显式写 0 = 免费**（宿主会带上 free7/free15/free30/free90/freeLife
     标记，不能只看数值，否则 0 会被当成“没设定”而回退成默认价）。 */
  var PRICE_DEFAULT = 19.99;
  function priceNum(v){
    return (typeof v === 'number' && isFinite(v) && v > 0) ? v : 0;
  }
  function isFreePlan(g, plan){
    var m = GAMES[g] || {};
    return !!m['free' + plan];
  }
  function priceOf(g){
    var m = GAMES[g] || {};
    if (m.free7)  return 0;
    if (priceNum(m.price7))  return priceNum(m.price7);
    if (m.free15) return 0;
    if (priceNum(m.price15)) return priceNum(m.price15);
    if (m.free30) return 0;
    if (priceNum(m.price30)) return priceNum(m.price30);
    if (m.free90) return 0;
    if (priceNum(m.price90)) return priceNum(m.price90);
    if (m.freeLife) return 0;
    if (priceNum(m.priceLife)) return priceNum(m.priceLife);
    return PRICE_DEFAULT;
  }
  function priceFor(g){ return priceOf(g); }
  function isFreeGame(g){ return priceOf(g) <= 0; }
  function fmtPrice(g){ return isFreeGame(g) ? uiT('free') : '$' + priceOf(g).toFixed(2); }
  /* 折扣角标也不能给免费游戏显示 */
  function fmtOldPrice(g){
    if (isFreeGame(g)) return '';
    return '$' + (priceOf(g) * 1.1).toFixed(2);
  }


  var TOAST_ICONS = {
    ok:   '<path d="M20 6L9 17l-5-5"/>',
    err:  '<circle cx="12" cy="12" r="9"/><path d="M12 7.5v5.5"/><path d="M12 16.6v.01"/>',
    warn: '<path d="M12 3.5L2.5 20h19L12 3.5z"/><path d="M12 10v4.5"/><path d="M12 17.6v.01"/>',
    info: '<circle cx="12" cy="12" r="9"/><path d="M12 11.4v5.1"/><circle cx="12" cy="7.9" r="1.15" fill="currentColor" stroke="none"/>'
  };
  function toast(text, type, ms){
    type = TOAST_ICONS[type] ? type : 'info';
    ms = ms || 3200;
    var wrap = document.getElementById('toastWrap');
    while (wrap.children.length >= 3) wrap.removeChild(wrap.firstChild);
    var t = document.createElement('div');
    t.className = 'toast ' + type;
    t.style.setProperty('--toast-ms', ms + 'ms');
    t.innerHTML = '<span class="toast-ico"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24">' + TOAST_ICONS[type] + '</svg></span><span class="toast-txt"></span>';
    t.lastChild.textContent = text;
    wrap.appendChild(t);
    setTimeout(function(){
      t.classList.add('out');
      setTimeout(function(){ if (t.parentNode) t.parentNode.removeChild(t); }, 300);
    }, ms);
  }


  function fmtDuration(sec){
    if (sec < 0) return 'LIFETIME';
    var d = Math.floor(sec / 86400);
    var h = Math.floor((sec % 86400) / 3600);
    var m = Math.floor((sec % 3600) / 60);
    if (d > 0) return d + 'd ' + h + 'h';
    if (h > 0) return h + 'h ' + m + 'm';
    return m + 'm';
  }

  function applyDPI(dpi){
    var scale = (dpi || 100) / 100;
    if (scale < 0.5) scale = 0.5;
    if (scale > 2)   scale = 2;
    document.body.style.zoom = '';
    try {
      var sw = window.screen.availWidth;
      var sh = window.screen.availHeight;
      var baseW = 1100;
      var baseH = 700;
      var w = Math.round(baseW * scale);
      var h = Math.round(baseH * scale);
      if (w > sw - 8) w = sw - 8;
      if (h > sh - 8) h = sh - 8;
      if (w < 640) w = 640;
      if (h < 480) h = 480;
      wv('RESIZE:' + w + 'x' + h);
      setTimeout(function(){
        wv('SET_ZOOM:' + scale.toFixed(3));
      }, 60);
    } catch (e) {}
  }


  function applyUser(){
    var name = USER.name || 'STARLINE User';
    var initial = name.trim().charAt(0).toUpperCase() || 'S';
    var av = document.getElementById('profileAvatar');
    var tbAv = document.getElementById('tbAvatar');
    if (USER.avatar) {
      av.innerHTML = '<img src="' + USER.avatar + '" alt="">';
      tbAv.innerHTML = '<img src="' + USER.avatar + '" alt="">';
    } else {
      av.innerHTML = '<span id="profileAvatarText">' + initial + '</span>';
      tbAv.textContent = initial;
    }
    document.getElementById('profileName').textContent = name;
    updateProfileBadges();      /* 有订阅才显示 PRO */
  }

  function renderHero(){
    var n = HERO_ORDER.length;
    if (!n) return;
    HERO_PENDING = -1;
    if (HERO_POS >= n) HERO_POS = 0;
    HERO_GAME = HERO_ORDER[HERO_POS];
    heroSetPanel(HERO_PANEL_EL(), HERO_GAME);
    heroSetPanel(HERO_OTHER_EL(), HERO_GAME);   /* 备用面板先对齐，切换时再换成目标游戏 */
    heroPlace(HERO_PANEL_EL(), 0);
    heroPlace(HERO_OTHER_EL(), 0);
    heroRenderDots();
  }


  /* ── banner 轮播：整块面板左右滑动 ──────────────────────────────────────
     每个精选做成一张整块面板（背景图 + 文字 + 按钮 + 价格徽标），
     切换时两张面板一起平移：下一张 → 整块向左移出、新的从右移入；
     上一张 → 整块向右移出、新的从左移入。动画结束后把内容并回一张面板，
     于是可以一直朝同一个方向循环，不会出现反向跳帧。
     圆点在面板之外（#heroNav），固定显示在左下角不跟着滑动。 */
  var HERO_ANIM_MS = 480;          /* 与 .hero-panel.anim 的 transition 时长一致 */
  var HERO_SLIDE_TK = 0;
  var HERO_SLIDE_TIMER = 0;
  var HERO_ANIMATING = false;
  var HERO_PENDING = -1;           /* 动画中收到的切换请求，落位后补做 */

  function heroN(){ return HERO_ORDER.length; }

  function heroPanelEls(){
    return [
      document.getElementById('heroPanelA'),
      document.getElementById('heroPanelB')
    ];
  }
  /* 当前显示的面板保持“奇数/偶数”交替，两张轮流当进出面板 */
  function HERO_PANEL_EL(){
    var els = heroPanelEls();
    return els[HERO_PANEL_IDX % 2];
  }
  function HERO_OTHER_EL(){
    var els = heroPanelEls();
    return els[(HERO_PANEL_IDX + 1) % 2];
  }
  var HERO_PANEL_IDX = 0;



  /* 直接设置面板位移（不带动画）*/
  function heroPlace(el, x){
    if (!el) return;
    el.classList.remove('anim');
    el.style.transform = 'translateX(' + x + 'px)';
  }

  /* 按语言取标签文字（面板内容是在 JS 里拼的，所以要手动翻译）*/
  function heroLabel(key, fallback){
    var v = uiT(key);
    return (v && v !== key) ? v : fallback;
  }
  /* 价格徽标的折扣文案：uiT 里没有对应词条时用默认值 */
  function heroDiscLabel(){
    var v = uiT('discount');
    return (v && v !== 'discount') ? v : '-10%';
  }

  /* 把一张面板填成指定游戏：图片、文字、按钮、价格一起换 */
  function heroSetPanel(el, gid){
    if (!el) return;
    var g = GAMES[gid];
    if (!g) return;
    var wished = isWished(gid);
    var owned = hasLic(gid);
    var metaTxt = g.dev || '';
    for (var k = 0; k < (g.gen || []).length; k++){
      metaTxt += (metaTxt ? ' \u00B7 ' : '') + g.gen[k];
    }
    var p = priceFor(gid);
    el.setAttribute('data-game', gid);
    /* 先让 buildSlide 设好背景（它会清空 innerHTML），再铺面板内容。
       海报挂了就退回 icon，避免出现空白 banner。 */
    buildSlide(el, g.poster || g.img || '', g.img || '');
    el.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend',
      '<div class="hero-overlay" onclick="heroOpenDetail(event)"></div>'
      + '<div class="hero-body">'
      +   '<div class="hero-info">'
      +     '<div class="hero-tag">' + uiEsc(heroLabel('featured', 'Featured')) + '</div>'
      +     '<div class="hero-title"></div>'
      +     '<div class="hero-meta"></div>'
      +   '</div>'
      +   '<div class="hero-actions">'
      +     '<button class="btn btn-primary" data-hero-h="buy" onclick="onHeroBuy()">'
      +       '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M12 5v14M5 12h14"/></svg>'
      +       '<span></span>'
      +     '</button>'
      +     '<button class="btn btn-secondary' + (wished ? ' wished' : '') + '" data-hero-h="wish"'
      +       ' onclick="toggleWish(event,\'' + gid + '\')">'
      +       '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M20.84 4.61a5.5 5.5 0 0 0-7.78 0L12 5.67l-1.06-1.06a5.5 5.5 0 0 0-7.78 7.78l1.06 1.06L12 21.23l7.78-7.78 1.06-1.06a5.5 5.5 0 0 0 0-7.78z"/></svg>'
      +       '<span></span>'
      +     '</button>'
      +   '</div>'
      + '</div>'
      + '<div class="hero-badge">'
      +   '<span class="discount">' + uiEsc(isFreeGame(gid) ? uiT('free') : heroDiscLabel()) + '</span>'
      + (isFreeGame(gid) ? '' : '<span class="price-old">$' + (p * 1.1).toFixed(2) + '</span>')
      +   '<span class="price-new' + (isFreeGame(gid) ? ' free' : '') + '">' + uiEsc(fmtPrice(gid)) + '</span>'
      + '</div>');



    var q = function(sel){ return el.querySelector(sel); };
    q('.hero-title').textContent = g.title || g.short || gid;
    q('.hero-meta').textContent = metaTxt;
    var buy = q('[data-hero-h="buy"] span');
    if (buy) buy.textContent = owned ? uiT('launch') : uiT('buyNow');
    var wish = q('[data-hero-h="wish"] span');
    if (wish) wish.textContent = wished ? uiT('wishlisted') : uiT('wishlist');
    el.classList.toggle('on', el === HERO_PANEL_EL());
  }

  /* 圆点在面板外面，是固定的一份，翻页时不会跟着滑走 */
  function heroRenderDots(){
    var nav = document.getElementById('heroNav');
    if (!nav) return;
    var html = '';
    for (var j = 0; j < heroN(); j++){
      var meta = GAMES[HERO_ORDER[j]];
      if (!meta) continue;
      var cls = (j === HERO_POS) ? 'hero-dot active' : 'hero-dot';
      /* data-nodrag + stopPropagation：点圆点只切换 banner，不会跳进详情页 */
      html += '<button class="' + cls + '" data-nodrag'
            + ' onclick="event.stopPropagation();heroGo(' + j + ')"'
            + ' title="' + uiEsc(meta.title || HERO_ORDER[j]) + '"></button>';
    }
    nav.innerHTML = html;
  }



  function heroRefreshDots(){
    var nav = document.getElementById('heroNav');
    if (!nav) return;
    var dots = nav.children;
    for (var k = 0; k < dots.length; k++){
      dots[k].classList.toggle('active', k === HERO_POS);
    }
  }

  /* 当前面板里的愿望按钮状态（呼应 toggleWish 后的刷新）*/
  function renderHeroWish(){
    var el = HERO_PANEL_EL();
    if (!el) return;
    var btn = el.querySelector('[data-hero-h="wish"]');
    if (!btn) return;
    var span = btn.querySelector('span');
    var wished = isWished(HERO_GAME);
    btn.classList.toggle('wished', wished);
    if (span) span.textContent = wished ? uiT('wishlisted') : uiT('wishlist');
  }

  /* 切换完成后：把目标内容并回当前面板，两张面板归位 */
  function heroSlideSettle(target, nextIdx){
    var cur = HERO_PANEL_EL(), other = HERO_OTHER_EL();
    heroSetPanel(cur, HERO_ORDER[target]);
    HERO_PANEL_IDX = nextIdx;
    heroPlace(cur, 0);
    heroPlace(other, 0);
    HERO_ANIMATING = false;
    HERO_POS = target;
    HERO_GAME = HERO_ORDER[target];
    heroRefreshDots();
    /* 动画期间被挡下的那次切换（自动轮播/连点）现在补上 */
    if (HERO_PENDING >= 0){
      var p = HERO_PENDING;
      HERO_PENDING = -1;
      heroGo(p);
    }
  }



  /* 第 i 个精选；dir>0 下一张（向左滑），dir<0 上一张（向右滑）*/
  function heroGoDir(i, dir){
    var n = heroN();
    if (!n) return;
    var target = ((i % n) + n) % n;
    if (target === HERO_POS) return;

    var cur = HERO_PANEL_EL(), other = HERO_OTHER_EL();
    if (!cur || !other || n < 2){                     /* 只有一个精选：直接换内容 */
      heroSetPanel(cur, HERO_ORDER[target]);
      HERO_POS = target;
      HERO_GAME = HERO_ORDER[target];
      heroRefreshDots();
      return;
    }

    if (HERO_ANIMATING){                              /* 正在滑：记住这次请求，落位后补做 */
      HERO_PENDING = i;
      return;
    }

    var w = cur.offsetWidth || 1;
    var sgn = (dir < 0) ? -1 : 1;
    HERO_ANIMATING = true;

    /* 新的一块摆到入场位置，再让两块一起平移 */
    heroSetPanel(other, HERO_ORDER[target]);
    other.classList.remove('on');
    heroPlace(other, sgn * w);



    var tk = ++HERO_SLIDE_TK;
    requestAnimationFrame(function(){
      if (tk !== HERO_SLIDE_TK) return;
      cur.classList.add('anim');
      other.classList.add('anim');
      cur.style.transform   = 'translateX(' + (-sgn * w) + 'px)';
      other.style.transform = 'translateX(0px)';
    });

    clearTimeout(HERO_SLIDE_TIMER);
    HERO_SLIDE_TIMER = setTimeout(function(){
      if (tk !== HERO_SLIDE_TK) return;
      heroSlideSettle(target, (HERO_PANEL_IDX + 1) % 2);
    }, HERO_ANIM_MS + 90);
  }

  /* 自动轮播 / 圆点直达：按距离选方向，保证动画方向合理 */
  function heroGo(i){
    var n = heroN();
    if (!n) return;
    var target = ((i % n) + n) % n;
    if (target === HERO_POS) return;
    if (HERO_ANIMATING){ HERO_PENDING = i; return; }   /* 别丢掉这一拍 */
    var nf = (target - HERO_POS + n) % n;             /* 向前几步 */
    var nb = (HERO_POS - target + n) % n;             /* 向后几步 */
    heroGoDir(target, nf <= nb ? 1 : -1);
  }



  function heroPrev(ev){ if (ev) ev.stopPropagation(); heroGoDir(HERO_POS - 1, -1); }
  function heroNext(ev){ if (ev) ev.stopPropagation(); heroGoDir(HERO_POS + 1, 1); }

  /* 窗口尺寸变化时把两张面板归位（此时没有动画在进行）*/
  window.addEventListener('resize', function(){
    if (HERO_ANIMATING) return;
    heroPlace(HERO_PANEL_EL(), 0);
    heroPlace(HERO_OTHER_EL(), 0);
  });

  function startHeroTimer(){
    if (HERO_TIMER) clearInterval(HERO_TIMER);
    HERO_TIMER = setInterval(function(){
      if (document.getElementById('storePage').hidden) return;
      if (heroN() < 2) return;
      /* 页面被切到后台时浏览器不跑 transition，等回来再翻，免得"没动画" */
      if (document.hidden){ setTimeout(function(){ heroGo(HERO_POS + 1); }, 600); return; }
      heroGo(HERO_POS + 1);
    }, 7000);
  }

  var LAYOUT = (UI_CFG && UI_CFG.layout === 'list') ? 'list' : 'grid';



  var GRID_ORDER = (function(){
    var out = [];
    try {
      if (UI_CFG && Array.isArray(UI_CFG.gridOrder)) {
        UI_CFG.gridOrder.forEach(function(g){ if (GAMES[g] && out.indexOf(g) < 0) out.push(g); });
      }
    } catch (e) {}
    ORDER.forEach(function(g){ if (out.indexOf(g) < 0) out.push(g); });
    return out;
  })();
  function saveGridOrder(){ wv('UI_GORDER:' + GRID_ORDER.join(',')); }


  function setFilter(f){
    FILTER = f;
    document.querySelectorAll('#secFilter button').forEach(function(b){
      b.classList.toggle('active', b.getAttribute('data-filter') === f);
    });
    renderGrid();
  }

  /* ── 按 Genres 筛选 ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
     GENRE = '' 表示不筛；与 All/Owned/Available/Wishlist 是「与」关系 */
  var GENRE = '';

  /* 收集目录里出现过的所有类型（按名字排序，去重） */
  function allGenres(){
    var seen = {}, out = [];
    ORDER.forEach(function(g){
      var meta = GAMES[g];
      if (!meta || !meta.gen) return;
      meta.gen.forEach(function(x){
        var v = String(x || '').trim();
        if (!v || seen[v.toLowerCase()]) return;
        seen[v.toLowerCase()] = 1;
        out.push(v);
      });
    });
    out.sort(function(a, b){ return a.toLowerCase() < b.toLowerCase() ? -1 : 1; });
    return out;
  }
  function renderGenreBtn(){
    var btn = document.getElementById('genreBtn');
    var txt = document.getElementById('genreBtnTxt');
    if (!btn || !txt) return;
    if (GENRE) {
      /* 选了类型就把按钮文字换成类型名，并摘掉 data-ui，免得被 i18n 覆盖 */
      txt.removeAttribute('data-ui');
      txt.textContent = tagT(GENRE);
      btn.classList.add('active');
    } else {
      txt.setAttribute('data-ui', 'genres');
      txt.textContent = uiT('genres');
      btn.classList.remove('active');
    }
  }
  function renderGenrePop(){
    var pop = document.getElementById('genrePop');
    if (!pop) return;
    var list = allGenres();
    var html = '<button type="button" data-genre=""' + (GENRE ? '' : ' class="active"') + '>'
      + '<svg class="tick" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><polyline points="20 6 9 17 4 12"/></svg>'
      + '<span>' + uiEsc(uiT('genreAll')) + '</span></button>';
    if (list.length) { html += '<div class="genre-sep"></div>'; }
    list.forEach(function(x){
      html += '<button type="button" data-genre="' + uiEsc(x) + '"'
        + (x === GENRE ? ' class="active"' : '') + '>'
        + '<svg class="tick" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><polyline points="20 6 9 17 4 12"/></svg>'
        + '<span>' + uiEsc(tagT(x)) + '</span></button>';
    });
    if (!list.length) { html += '<div class="genre-none">' + uiEsc(uiT('genreNone')) + '</div>'; }
    pop.innerHTML = html;
  }
  function openGenrePop(){
    var pop = document.getElementById('genrePop');
    var btn = document.getElementById('genreBtn');
    if (!pop) return;
    renderGenrePop();
    pop.hidden = false;
    if (btn) { btn.classList.add('open'); btn.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'true'); }
  }
  function closeGenrePop(){
    var pop = document.getElementById('genrePop');
    var btn = document.getElementById('genreBtn');
    if (pop) pop.hidden = true;
    if (btn) { btn.classList.remove('open'); btn.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false'); }
  }
  function genreOpen(){
    var p = document.getElementById('genrePop');
    if (!p || p.hidden) return false;
    /* 弹层在商店页里，商店页被隐藏时它其实也看不见 */
    var sp = document.getElementById('storePage');
    if (sp && sp.hidden) return false;
    return true;
  }
  function toggleGenrePop(ev){
    if (ev) { ev.stopPropagation(); ev.preventDefault(); }
    if (genreOpen()) closeGenrePop(); else openGenrePop();
  }
  function setGenre(x){
    GENRE = x || '';
    renderGenreBtn();
    renderGenrePop();
    closeGenrePop();
    renderGrid();
  }
  (function initGenrePop(){
    var pop = document.getElementById('genrePop');
    if (!pop) return;
    /* 类型名来自 games.txt，可能有引号，所以走 data 属性 + 委托，不拼 onclick */
    pop.addEventListener('click', function(e){
      var b = e.target && e.target.closest ? e.target.closest('button[data-genre]') : null;
      if (!b) return;
      setGenre(b.getAttribute('data-genre') || '');
    });
    document.addEventListener('pointerdown', function(e){
      if (!genreOpen()) return;
      var wrap = document.getElementById('genreWrap');
      if (wrap && wrap.contains(e.target)) return;
      closeGenrePop();
    }, true);
  })();

  var COLS_OPTS = ['auto', '2', '3', '4', '5', '6'];
  var COLS = (UI_CFG && COLS_OPTS.indexOf(String(UI_CFG.cols)) >= 0) ? String(UI_CFG.cols) : 'auto';

  function applyGridCols(){
    var grid = document.getElementById('grid');
    if (!grid) return;
    var fixed = (LAYOUT === 'grid' && COLS !== 'auto');
    grid.classList.toggle('cols-n', fixed);
    if (fixed) {
      grid.style.setProperty('--cols', COLS);
      grid.style.setProperty('--gap', (COLS === '6' ? 12 : COLS === '5' ? 14 : 20) + 'px');
    } else {
      grid.style.removeProperty('--cols');
      grid.style.removeProperty('--gap');
    }
  }
  function renderColsOpts(){
    var box = document.getElementById('colsChips');
    var row = document.getElementById('colsRow');
    if (!box || !row) return;
    var html = '';
    COLS_OPTS.forEach(function(c){
      html += '<button type="button" class="cols-chip' + (c === COLS ? ' active' : '') + '" '
            + 'onclick="setCols(\'' + c + '\')">'
            + (c === 'auto' ? uiEsc(uiT('colsAuto')) : c) + '</button>';
    });
    box.innerHTML = html;
    row.classList.toggle('disabled', LAYOUT !== 'grid');
  }
  function setCols(c){
    if (COLS_OPTS.indexOf(c) < 0 || c === COLS) return;
    COLS = c;
    wv('UI_COLS:' + c);
    renderColsOpts();
    applyGridCols();
    var grid = document.getElementById('grid');
    grid.classList.remove('relayout');
    void grid.offsetWidth;
    grid.classList.add('relayout');
    setTimeout(function(){ grid.classList.remove('relayout'); }, 450);
  }
  function renderLayoutOpts(){
    document.querySelectorAll('#layoutOpts .layout-opt').forEach(function(b){
      b.classList.toggle('active', b.getAttribute('data-layout') === LAYOUT);
    });
    renderColsOpts();
  }
  function setLayout(mode){
    mode = (mode === 'list') ? 'list' : 'grid';
    if (mode === LAYOUT) return;
    LAYOUT = mode;
    wv('UI_LAYOUT:' + LAYOUT);
    renderLayoutOpts();
    renderGrid();
  }


  function licPausable(g){
    var l = licOf(g);
    return !!(l && l.pausable && l.type !== 'LIFETIME');
  }
  function pauseIcon(paused){
    return paused
      ? '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><polygon points="6 4 20 12 6 20 6 4"/></svg>'
      : '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><line x1="9" y1="5" x2="9" y2="19"/><line x1="15" y1="5" x2="15" y2="19"/></svg>';
  }
  function togglePause(ev, g){
    if (ev) ev.stopPropagation();
    var l = licOf(g);
    if (!l || !licPausable(g)) return;
    wv((l.paused ? 'RESUME_TIME:' : 'PAUSE_TIME:') + g);
  }

  var REQ_BUSY = false;
  function openGameReq(){
    var m = document.getElementById('reqModal');
    if (!m) return;
    document.getElementById('reqGame').value = (SEARCH || '').slice(0, 60);
    document.getElementById('reqGame').classList.remove('is-err');
    reqSetBusy(false);
    m.classList.add('open');
    setTimeout(function(){
      var e = document.getElementById('reqGame');
      if (e) { e.focus(); e.select(); }
    }, 120);
  }
  function closeGameReq(){
    var m = document.getElementById('reqModal');
    if (m) m.classList.remove('open');
  }
  function reqSetBusy(b){
    REQ_BUSY = b;
    var btn = document.getElementById('reqSendBtn');
    if (btn) {
      btn.disabled = b;
      btn.textContent = uiT(b ? 'reqSending' : 'reqSend');
    }
  }
  function submitGameReq(){
    if (REQ_BUSY) return;
    var gm = document.getElementById('reqGame');
    var game = gm.value.trim();
    if (game.length < 2) {
      gm.classList.add('is-err'); toast(uiT('reqErrGame'), 'err', 2500); gm.focus(); return;
    }
    reqSetBusy(true);
    wv('GAME_REQ:' + game);       /* 宿主 → 网站 api/game_requests.php（带登录 token） */
  }
  document.addEventListener('keydown', function(e){
    if (e.key !== 'Escape') return;
    closeGameReq();
    closeReviewModal();
  });

  /* ════════ What players say：网站后台已审核的评价 ════════ */
  var REVIEWS = null;        /* [{username, rating, content, date, avatar}] */
  var REV_BUSY = false;
  var REV_RATING = 5;
  var REV_FIRST = 3;         /* 首屏只显示 3 条 */
  var REV_STEP  = 6;         /* 每点一次「加载更多」再多 6 条（两行），要再点一次才继续 */
  var REV_LIMIT = 3;         /* 当前显示条数 */

  function renderReviews(){
    var box = document.getElementById('revList');
    var empty = document.getElementById('revEmpty');
    var more = document.getElementById('revMoreWrap');
    if (!box) return;
    var list = REVIEWS || [];
    if (!list.length) {
      box.innerHTML = '';
      if (more) more.hidden = true;
      if (empty) { empty.textContent = uiT('reviewEmpty'); empty.hidden = false; }
      return;
    }
    if (empty) empty.hidden = true;
    if (REV_LIMIT < REV_FIRST) REV_LIMIT = REV_FIRST;

    var n = Math.min(list.length, REV_LIMIT);
    var html = '';
    for (var i = 0; i < n; i++) {
      var r = list[i] || {};
      var rating = parseInt(r.rating, 10);
      if (!isFinite(rating) || rating < 1) rating = 5;
      if (rating > 5) rating = 5;
      var stars = '';
      for (var s = 1; s <= 5; s++) { stars += (s <= rating) ? '★' : '☆'; }
      var name = String(r.username || '');
      var initial = (name.replace(/^\s+/, '').charAt(0) || '?').toUpperCase();
      var color = String(r.avatar || '');
      if (!/^#[0-9a-fA-F]{3,8}$/.test(color)) color = '#e8375a';
      html += '<div class="rev-card">'
        + '<div class="rev-stars-line">' + stars + '</div>'
        + '<div class="rev-text">“' + uiEsc(String(r.content || '')) + '”</div>'
        + '<div class="rev-author">'
        +   '<div class="rev-avatar" style="background:' + color + '">' + uiEsc(initial) + '</div>'
        +   '<div><div class="rev-name">' + uiEsc(name) + '</div>'
        +   '<div class="rev-meta">' + uiEsc(String(r.date || '')) + '</div></div>'
        + '</div></div>';
    }
    box.innerHTML = html;
    if (more) more.hidden = (list.length <= n);      /* 全显示完了就收起按钮 */
  }

  /* 加载更多：一次多两行（6 条），加载完还想看就再点一次 */
  function loadMoreReviews(){
    REV_LIMIT += REV_STEP;
    renderReviews();
  }

  /* 详情页每次打开都刷一次；宿主那边有 120 秒缓存，不会每次都跑网络 */
  function loadReviews(){
    if (!(REVIEWS && REVIEWS.length)) { renderReviews(); }
    wv('REVIEWS');
  }

  function openReviewModal(){
    var m = document.getElementById('revModal');
    if (!m) return;
    REV_RATING = 5;
    paintReviewStars();
    var t = document.getElementById('revText');
    if (t) { t.value = ''; t.classList.remove('is-err'); }
    revSetBusy(false);
    m.classList.add('open');
    setTimeout(function(){ if (t) t.focus(); }, 120);
  }
  function closeReviewModal(){
    var m = document.getElementById('revModal');
    if (m) m.classList.remove('open');
  }
  function paintReviewStars(){
    var box = document.getElementById('revStars');
    if (!box) return;
    var btns = box.querySelectorAll('button');
    for (var i = 0; i < btns.length; i++) { btns[i].classList.toggle('on', (i + 1) <= REV_RATING); }
  }
  function pickReviewStar(n){
    REV_RATING = Math.max(1, Math.min(5, n || 5));
    paintReviewStars();
  }
  function revSetBusy(b){
    REV_BUSY = b;
    var btn = document.getElementById('revSendBtn');
    if (btn) { btn.disabled = b; btn.textContent = uiT(b ? 'reqSending' : 'reqSend'); }
  }
  function submitReview(){
    if (REV_BUSY) return;
    var t = document.getElementById('revText');
    var txt = String((t && t.value) || '')
      .replace(/[\u0000-\u001f]+/g, ' ').replace(/\s{2,}/g, ' ').trim();
    if (txt.length < 10) {
      if (t) { t.classList.add('is-err'); t.focus(); }
      toast(uiT('revErrShort'), 'err', 2500);
      return;
    }
    if (txt.length > 600) txt = txt.slice(0, 600);
    revSetBusy(true);
    wv('REVIEW_SUBMIT:' + REV_RATING + String.fromCharCode(31) + txt);
  }

  /* ════════ 网站实时组（个人资料里那行）+ 管理员入口 ════════ */
  var MY_GROUP = (UI_CFG && UI_CFG.group) || '';
  var MY_ROLE  = (UI_CFG && UI_CFG.role)  || '';

  function isAdminUser(){ return (MY_ROLE === 'admin' || MY_GROUP === 'admin'); }

  function updateProfileGroup(){
    var el = document.getElementById('profileGroup');
    if (el) {
      var grp = MY_GROUP || (MY_ROLE === 'admin' ? 'admin' : 'release');
      el.textContent = uiT(grp === 'admin' ? 'groupAdmin' : 'groupRelease');
      el.classList.toggle('admin', grp === 'admin');
      el.hidden = false;
    }
    var nav = document.getElementById('navAdmin');
    if (nav) nav.hidden = !isAdminUser();
  }

  /* ════════ 管理面板：用户 / 订阅 / HWID 工单 ════════ */
  var ADMIN_USERS = [], ADMIN_TICKETS = [], ADMIN_TAB = 'users', ADMIN_ERR = '';

  function showAdmin(){
    if (!isAdminUser()) return;
    closeDetail();
    document.getElementById('storePage').hidden = true;
    document.getElementById('profilePage').hidden = true;
    document.getElementById('adminPage').hidden = false;
    setActiveNav('navAdmin');
    adminShowTab(ADMIN_TAB);
    adminRefresh();
  }
  function adminRefresh(){
    if (!isAdminUser()) return;
    wv('ADMIN:users');
    wv('ADMIN:tickets');
  }
  function adminShowTab(tab){
    ADMIN_TAB = (tab === 'tickets') ? 'tickets' : 'users';
    var bu = document.getElementById('adminTabUsers'), bt = document.getElementById('adminTabTickets');
    if (bu) bu.classList.toggle('active', ADMIN_TAB === 'users');
    if (bt) bt.classList.toggle('active', ADMIN_TAB === 'tickets');
    var pu = document.getElementById('adminUsersPane'), pt = document.getElementById('adminTicketsPane');
    if (pu) pu.hidden = (ADMIN_TAB !== 'users');
    if (pt) pt.hidden = (ADMIN_TAB !== 'tickets');
  }
  function adminAct(cmd){ wv('ADMIN:' + cmd); }

  /* 删除订阅要再点一次确认（避免手滑） */
  var ADMIN_ARM = '';
  function adminSubDelete(id){
    if (ADMIN_ARM !== id) {
      ADMIN_ARM = id;
      toast(uiT('adminConfirm'), 'info', 3000);
      return;
    }
    ADMIN_ARM = '';
    adminAct('sub_delete:' + id);
  }

  function admDate(s){
    s = String(s || '');
    return s ? s.slice(0, 10) : '';
  }
  function admExpired(s){
    if (!s) return false;
    var t = Date.parse(String(s).replace(' ', 'T'));
    return isFinite(t) && t <= Date.now();
  }

  function renderAdminUsers(){
    var box = document.getElementById('adminUserList');
    if (!box) return;
    var si = document.getElementById('adminUserSearch');
    var q = String((si && si.value) || '').toLowerCase().trim();
    var cnt = document.getElementById('adminUserCount');
    if (cnt) cnt.textContent = String(ADMIN_USERS.length);

    var rows = '', shown = 0;
    for (var i = 0; i < ADMIN_USERS.length; i++) {
      var u = ADMIN_USERS[i] || {};
      var name = String(u.username || ''), email = String(u.email || '');
      if (q && (name + ' ' + email).toLowerCase().indexOf(q) < 0) continue;
      shown++;

      var banned = (u.status === 'banned');
      var isAdm  = (u.group === 'admin' || u.role === 'admin');
      var initial = ((name.charAt(0) || '?') + (name.charAt(1) || '')).toUpperCase();
      var hwid = String(u.hwid || '');
      var subs = u.subs || [];

      var subHtml;
      if (!subs.length) {
        subHtml = '<div class="adm-empty">' + uiEsc(uiT('adminNone')) + '</div>';
      } else {
        subHtml = '<div class="adm-subs">';
        for (var s = 0; s < subs.length; s++) {
          var sub = subs[s] || {};
          var frozen  = (sub.status === 'frozen');
          var expired = admExpired(sub.expires);
          var cls = 'adm-sub' + (frozen ? ' frozen' : (expired ? ' expired' : ''));
          var state = frozen ? (' · ' + uiT('adminFrozen')) : (expired ? (' · ' + uiT('adminExpired')) : '');
          subHtml += '<span class="' + cls + '">'
            + '<b>' + uiEsc(String(sub.game || '?')) + '</b> '
            + uiEsc(String(sub.plan || '')) + ' · ' + uiEsc(sub.expires ? admDate(sub.expires) : '∞')
            + uiEsc(state)
            + '<button class="adm-sub-btn" type="button" onclick="adminAct(\''
            + (frozen ? 'sub_unfreeze:' : 'sub_freeze:') + sub.id + '\')" title="'
            + uiEsc(frozen ? uiT('adminUnfreeze') : uiT('adminFreeze')) + '">'
            + (frozen ? '&#9654;' : '&#10074;&#10074;') + '</button>'
            + '<button class="adm-sub-btn danger" type="button" onclick="adminSubDelete(' + sub.id + ')" title="'
            + uiEsc(uiT('remove')) + '">&times;</button>'
            + '</span>';
        }
        subHtml += '</div>';
      }

      rows += '<div class="adm-user">'
        + '<div class="adm-user-top">'
        +   '<div class="adm-av" style="background:' + (isAdm ? 'linear-gradient(135deg,#ef4444,#f59e0b)' : 'var(--accent)') + '">'
        +     uiEsc(initial) + '</div>'
        +   '<div class="adm-user-info">'
        +     '<div class="adm-name">' + uiEsc(name)
        +       '<span class="adm-tag ' + (isAdm ? 'admin' : '') + '">' + uiEsc(uiT(isAdm ? 'groupAdmin' : 'groupRelease')) + '</span>'
        +       '<span class="adm-tag ' + (banned ? 'banned' : 'active') + '">' + uiEsc(banned ? 'BANNED' : 'OK') + '</span>'
        +       (u.tickets ? '<span class="adm-tag banned">HWID &times;' + u.tickets + '</span>' : '')
        +     '</div>'
        +     '<div class="adm-meta">' + uiEsc(email)
        +       (hwid ? ' · HWID: ' + uiEsc(hwid.slice(0, 16)) + '…' : '')
        +       (u.last_login ? ' · ' + uiEsc(uiT('adminLastLogin')) + ' ' + uiEsc(admDate(u.last_login)) : '')
        +     '</div>'
        +   '</div>'
        +   '<div class="adm-acts">'
        +     '<button class="btn ' + (banned ? 'btn-primary' : 'btn-secondary') + '" type="button"'
        +       (isAdm ? ' disabled' : '')
        +       ' onclick="adminAct(\'' + (banned ? 'unban:' : 'ban:') + u.id + '\')">'
        +       uiEsc(uiT(banned ? 'adminUnban' : 'adminBan')) + '</button>'
        +   '</div>'
        + '</div>'
        + subHtml
        + '</div>';
    }
    box.innerHTML = shown ? rows : ('<div class="adm-empty">' + uiEsc(ADMIN_ERR || uiT('adminNoUsers')) + '</div>');
  }

  function renderAdminTickets(){
    var box = document.getElementById('adminTicketList');
    if (!box) return;
    var n = ADMIN_TICKETS.length;
    var cnt = document.getElementById('adminTicketCount');
    if (cnt) { cnt.textContent = String(n); cnt.hidden = !n; }
    var sb = document.getElementById('sbAdminBadge');
    if (sb) { sb.textContent = String(n); sb.hidden = !n; }

    if (!n) {
      box.innerHTML = '<div class="adm-empty">' + uiEsc(ADMIN_ERR || uiT('adminNoTickets')) + '</div>';
      return;
    }
    var html = '';
    for (var i = 0; i < n; i++) {
      var t = ADMIN_TICKETS[i] || {};
      html += '<div class="adm-ticket">'
        + '<div class="adm-ticket-info">'
        +   '<div class="adm-ticket-name">' + uiEsc(String(t.username || '')) + '</div>'
        +   '<div class="adm-ticket-reason">' + uiEsc(uiT('adminReason')) + ': ' + uiEsc(String(t.reason || '')) + '</div>'
        +   '<div class="adm-ticket-meta">' + uiEsc(admDate(t.created_at))
        +     (t.hwid ? ' · HWID: ' + uiEsc(String(t.hwid).slice(0, 20)) + '…' : '') + '</div>'
        + '</div>'
        + '<div class="adm-acts">'
        +   '<button class="btn btn-primary" type="button" onclick="adminAct(\'ticket_approve:' + t.id + '\')">'
        +     uiEsc(uiT('adminApprove')) + '</button>'
        +   '<button class="btn btn-secondary" type="button" onclick="adminAct(\'ticket_reject:' + t.id + '\')">'
        +     uiEsc(uiT('adminReject')) + '</button>'
        + '</div></div>';
    }
    box.innerHTML = html;
  }


  /* ════════ 拖动卡片 ════════ */
  var DND = null;
  var DND_SUPPRESS = 0;
  function dndLerp(a, b, k){ return a + (b - a) * k; }
  function dndIn(x, y, el, pad){
    if (!el) return false;
    var r = el.getBoundingClientRect();
    pad = pad || 0;
    return x >= r.left - pad && x <= r.right + pad && y >= r.top - pad && y <= r.bottom + pad;
  }
  function dndEsc(e){ if (e.key === 'Escape' && DND && !DND.pending) dndEnd(null, true); }

  function dndMetrics(){
    var k = DND.k;
    var w = dndLerp(DND.fw, DND.mw, k), h = dndLerp(DND.fh, DND.mh, k);
    var ax = DND.gx / DND.fw * w, ay = DND.gy / DND.fh * h;
    return { x: DND.px - ax, y: DND.py - ay, w: w, h: h, ax: ax, ay: ay };
  }
  function dndPaint(m, op){
    var g = DND.ghost;
    g.style.width = m.w + 'px';
    g.style.height = m.h + 'px';
    g.style.translate = m.x + 'px ' + m.y + 'px';
    g.style.transformOrigin = m.ax + 'px ' + m.ay + 'px';
    g.style.opacity = (op === undefined) ? ((DND.hotW || DND.hotV) ? 0.82 : 1) : op;
    var k = DND.k;
    DND.cardEl.style.opacity = Math.max(0, 1 - k * 1.9);
    DND.miniEl.style.opacity = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (k - 0.3) / 0.6));
    g.style.borderRadius = dndLerp(DND.radius, 14, k) + 'px';
  }


  function dndStart(e){
    var origin = DND.el;
    var r = origin.getBoundingClientRect();
    DND.pending = false;
    DND.fw = r.width; DND.fh = r.height;
    DND.gx = DND.sx - r.left; DND.gy = DND.sy - r.top;
    var listMode = (LAYOUT === 'list');
    DND.mw = listMode ? 232 : 150; DND.mh = listMode ? 60 : 94;
    DND.k = 0; DND.kt = 0;
    DND.px = e.clientX; DND.py = e.clientY;
    DND.radius = parseFloat(getComputedStyle(origin).borderTopLeftRadius) || 16;
    DND.mode = 'grid';
    DND.swapAt = 0;

    var meta = GAMES[DND.id];
    var ghost = document.createElement('div');
    ghost.className = 'dg-ghost';
    var clone = origin.cloneNode(true);
    clone.removeAttribute('onclick');
    clone.classList.remove('drag-origin');
    clone.classList.add('dg-card');
    clone.style.width = r.width + 'px';
    clone.style.height = r.height + 'px';
    var holder = document.createElement('div');
    holder.className = 'grid' + (listMode ? ' list' : '');
    holder.style.cssText = 'display:block;position:absolute;left:0;top:0;width:' + r.width + 'px;height:' + r.height + 'px';
    holder.appendChild(clone);
    var mini = document.createElement('div');
    if (listMode) {
      mini.className = 'dg-mini row';
      var th = document.createElement('div');
      th.className = 'thumb';
      th.style.backgroundImage = "url('" + (meta.poster || meta.img) + "')";
      var tx = document.createElement('div');
      tx.className = 'txt';
      var tb = document.createElement('b');
      tb.textContent = meta.title;
      var te = document.createElement('em');
      te.textContent = (meta.dev || '') + ' \u00B7 ' + tagT((meta.gen || [])[0] || '');
      tx.appendChild(tb); tx.appendChild(te);
      mini.appendChild(th); mini.appendChild(tx);
    } else {
      mini.className = 'dg-mini';
      mini.style.backgroundImage = "url('" + (meta.poster || meta.img) + "')";
      var nm = document.createElement('span');
      nm.textContent = meta.title;
      mini.appendChild(nm);
    }
    ghost.appendChild(holder);
    ghost.appendChild(mini);
    document.body.appendChild(ghost);
    DND.ghost = ghost; DND.cardEl = holder; DND.miniEl = mini;


    origin.classList.add('drag-origin');
    document.body.classList.add('dragging');
    if (typeof gpopHide === 'function') gpopHide();     /* 拖卡片时把悬停预览收掉 */
    try { document.getElementById('grid').setPointerCapture(DND.pid); } catch (err) {}
    var page = document.getElementById('storePage');
    DND.scrollBehavior = page.style.scrollBehavior;
    page.style.scrollBehavior = 'auto';

    var wished = isWished(DND.id);
    document.getElementById('dzWishTxt').textContent =
      uiT(wished ? 'dzRemove' : 'dzAdd').replace('{g}', meta.title);
    document.getElementById('dzStrip').classList.add('show');

    dndPaint(dndMetrics());
    requestAnimationFrame(function(){ if (DND && DND.ghost) DND.ghost.classList.add('lift'); });
    DND.raf = requestAnimationFrame(dndTick);
  }

  function dndReorder(target){
    var grid = document.getElementById('grid');
    var origin = DND.el;
    if (!origin || !origin.isConnected || !target || target === origin) return;
    var tid = target.getAttribute('data-game');
    if (isWished(tid) !== isWished(DND.id)) return;
    var now = performance.now();
    if (now - DND.swapAt < 140) return;
    DND.swapAt = now;
    var cards = [].slice.call(grid.querySelectorAll('.card[data-game]'));
    var before = new Map();
    cards.forEach(function(c){ before.set(c, c.getBoundingClientRect()); });
    var ci = cards.indexOf(origin), ti = cards.indexOf(target);
    if (ci < ti) target.after(origin); else target.before(origin);
    cards.forEach(function(c){
      var a = before.get(c), b = c.getBoundingClientRect();
      var dx = a.left - b.left, dy = a.top - b.top;
      if (!dx && !dy) return;
      c.animate(
        [{ transform: 'translate(' + dx + 'px,' + dy + 'px)' }, { transform: 'translate(0,0)' }],
        { duration: 280, easing: 'cubic-bezier(.2,.8,.2,1)' }
      );
    });
  }


  function dndViewEnter(){
    var z = document.getElementById('dzView');
    z.classList.remove('done');
    void z.offsetWidth;
    z.classList.add('hot');
    DND.viewT = setTimeout(function(){
      if (!DND) return;
      z.classList.add('done');
      DND.viewDone = true;
      setFilter('wish');
      var el = document.querySelector('#grid .card[data-game="' + DND.id + '"]');
      DND.el = el || null;
      if (el) el.classList.add('drag-origin');
    }, 1500);
  }
  function dndViewLeave(){
    var z = document.getElementById('dzView');
    clearTimeout(DND.viewT);
    z.classList.remove('hot');
    if (!DND.viewDone) z.classList.remove('done');
  }

  function dndHover(){
    var px = DND.px, py = DND.py;
    var side = document.querySelector('.sidebar');
    /* 判断指针是不是在侧栏上，必须用矩形包含：以前写的 `px < 侧栏.right`
       那是假定侧栏在左边——右侧边栏布局下这个条件恒为真，mode 永远是 'side'。
       于是卡片永远进不了下面的重排分支，拖动改顺序就失效了。 */
    var inSide = dndIn(px, py, side, 0);
    DND.kt = inSide ? 1 : 0;
    DND.mode = inSide ? 'side' : 'grid';


    var qp = document.getElementById('qpZone');
    var hotQ = inSide && dndIn(px, py, qp, 6);
    qp.classList.toggle('hot', hotQ);
    DND.hotQ = hotQ;

    var zw = document.getElementById('dzWish'), zv = document.getElementById('dzView');
    var hotW = dndIn(px, py, zw, 0);
    var hotV = dndIn(px, py, zv, 0);
    zw.classList.toggle('hot', hotW);
    DND.hotW = hotW;
    if (hotV && !DND.inView) { DND.inView = true; dndViewEnter(); }
    else if (!hotV && DND.inView) { DND.inView = false; dndViewLeave(); }

    if (!inSide) {
      var under = document.elementFromPoint(px, py);
      var card = under && under.closest ? under.closest('#grid .card[data-game]') : null;
      if (card) dndReorder(card);
    }
  }

  function dndTick(){
    if (!DND || DND.pending) return;
    DND.raf = requestAnimationFrame(dndTick);
    if (Math.abs(DND.kt - DND.k) > 0.004) DND.k += (DND.kt - DND.k) * 0.3;
    else DND.k = DND.kt;
    var page = document.getElementById('storePage');
    var pr = page.getBoundingClientRect();
    if (DND.px > pr.left && DND.mode === 'grid') {
      if (DND.py < pr.top + 56) page.scrollTop -= Math.ceil((pr.top + 56 - DND.py) / 5);
      else if (DND.py > pr.bottom - 56) page.scrollTop += Math.ceil((DND.py - (pr.bottom - 56)) / 5);
    }
    dndHover();
    dndPaint(dndMetrics());
  }


  function dndTween(to, done){
    var from = dndMetrics();
    var t0 = performance.now(), dur = 240;
    DND.finishing = true;
    function step(now){
      var t = Math.min(1, (now - t0) / dur);
      var e = 1 - Math.pow(1 - t, 3);
      var m = {
        x: dndLerp(from.x, to.x, e), y: dndLerp(from.y, to.y, e),
        w: dndLerp(from.w, to.w, e), h: dndLerp(from.h, to.h, e),
        ax: dndLerp(from.ax, to.w / 2, e), ay: dndLerp(from.ay, to.h / 2, e)
      };
      dndPaint(m, dndLerp(1, to.op, e));
      if (t < 1) requestAnimationFrame(step); else done();
    }
    requestAnimationFrame(step);
  }

  function dndCleanup(){
    var D = DND;
    if (!D) return;
    DND = null;
    clearTimeout(D.viewT);
    cancelAnimationFrame(D.raf);
    if (D.ghost && D.ghost.parentNode) D.ghost.parentNode.removeChild(D.ghost);
    if (D.el) D.el.classList.remove('drag-origin');
    document.querySelectorAll('#grid .drag-origin').forEach(function(c){ c.classList.remove('drag-origin'); });
    document.body.classList.remove('dragging');
    document.getElementById('dzStrip').classList.remove('show');
    ['dzWish', 'dzView'].forEach(function(id){
      var z = document.getElementById(id);
      z.classList.remove('hot'); z.classList.remove('done');
    });
    document.getElementById('qpZone').classList.remove('hot');
    document.getElementById('storePage').style.scrollBehavior = D.scrollBehavior || '';
    try { document.getElementById('grid').releasePointerCapture(D.pid); } catch (err) {}
  }


  function dndCommitOrder(){
    var grid = document.getElementById('grid');
    var ids = [].slice.call(grid.querySelectorAll('.card[data-game]')).map(function(c){
      return c.getAttribute('data-game');
    });
    if (ids.indexOf(DND.id) < 0) return;
    var next = GRID_ORDER.filter(function(g){ return g !== DND.id; });
    var i = ids.indexOf(DND.id);
    if (i + 1 < ids.length) {
      var at = next.indexOf(ids[i + 1]);
      next.splice(at < 0 ? next.length : at, 0, DND.id);
    } else if (i > 0) {
      var ap = next.indexOf(ids[i - 1]);
      next.splice(ap < 0 ? next.length : ap + 1, 0, DND.id);
    } else {
      next.unshift(DND.id);
    }
    if (next.join(',') !== GRID_ORDER.join(',')) {
      GRID_ORDER = next;
      saveGridOrder();
    }
  }

  function dndEnd(e, cancel){
    if (!DND) return;
    if (DND.pending) { DND = null; return; }
    if (DND.finishing) return;
    DND_SUPPRESS = Date.now() + 400;
    var D = DND;
    var id = D.id;
    var back = function(){
      var r = D.el && D.el.isConnected ? D.el.getBoundingClientRect() : null;
      D.kt = 0; D.k = 0;
      if (!r) { dndTween({ x: D.px - 20, y: D.py - 14, w: 40, h: 28, op: 0 }, dndCleanup); return; }
      dndTween({ x: r.left, y: r.top, w: r.width, h: r.height, op: 1 }, dndCleanup);
    };
    if (cancel) { renderGrid(); D.el = document.querySelector('#grid .card[data-game="' + id + '"]'); back(); return; }


    if (D.hotW) {
      var zr = document.getElementById('dzWish').getBoundingClientRect();
      dndTween({ x: zr.left + zr.width / 2 - 20, y: zr.top + zr.height / 2 - 14, w: 40, h: 28, op: 0 }, function(){
        dndCleanup();
        toggleWish(null, id);
      });
      return;
    }
    if (D.hotQ) {
      if (QUICK.indexOf(id) >= 0) {
        toast(uiT('qpExists'), 'info', 2200);
        back();
        return;
      }
      var hr = document.getElementById('qpDropHint').getBoundingClientRect();
      dndTween({ x: hr.left + hr.width / 2 - 24, y: hr.top + hr.height / 2 - 15, w: 48, h: 30, op: 0 }, function(){
        dndCleanup();
        addQuick(null, id);
        var items = document.querySelectorAll('#sbQuick .sb-mini');
        var last = items[items.length - 1];
        if (last) { last.classList.add('pop'); setTimeout(function(){ last.classList.remove('pop'); }, 600); }
      });
      return;
    }
    if (D.mode === 'side' || D.inView) { back(); return; }
    dndCommitOrder();
    back();
  }


  (function initDnd(){
    var grid = document.getElementById('grid');
    grid.addEventListener('dragstart', function(e){ e.preventDefault(); });
    grid.addEventListener('click', function(e){
      if (Date.now() < DND_SUPPRESS) { e.stopPropagation(); e.preventDefault(); }
    }, true);
    grid.addEventListener('pointerdown', function(e){
      if (e.button !== 0 || DND) return;
      var card = e.target.closest ? e.target.closest('.card[data-game]') : null;
      if (!card || e.target.closest('.card-wish')) return;
      DND = { pending: true, id: card.getAttribute('data-game'), el: card,
              sx: e.clientX, sy: e.clientY, pid: e.pointerId, px: e.clientX, py: e.clientY };
    });
    document.addEventListener('pointermove', function(e){
      if (!DND) return;
      DND.px = e.clientX; DND.py = e.clientY;
      if (DND.pending) {
        var dx = e.clientX - DND.sx, dy = e.clientY - DND.sy;
        if (dx * dx + dy * dy < 36) return;
        dndStart(e);
        return;
      }
      if (!DND.finishing) dndPaint(dndMetrics());
    }, true);
    document.addEventListener('pointerup', function(e){ if (DND) dndEnd(e, false); }, true);
    document.addEventListener('pointercancel', function(e){ if (DND) dndEnd(e, true); }, true);
    window.addEventListener('blur', function(){ if (DND && !DND.pending) dndEnd(null, true); });
    document.addEventListener('keydown', dndEsc);
  })();


  function renderGrid(){
    var grid = document.getElementById('grid');
    grid.className = 'grid' + (LAYOUT === 'list' ? ' list' : '');
    applyGridCols();
    document.getElementById('gameCount').textContent = ORDER.length;

    var list = GRID_ORDER.filter(function(g){
      if (FILTER === 'wish' && !isWished(g)) return false;
      if (FILTER === 'owned' && !hasLic(g)) return false;
