      if (FILTER === 'available' && hasLic(g)) return false;
      var meta = GAMES[g];
      if (!meta) return false;
      if (GENRE) {
        var gens = meta.gen || [], hit = false;
        for (var gi = 0; gi < gens.length; gi++) {
          if (String(gens[gi]).toLowerCase() === GENRE.toLowerCase()) { hit = true; break; }
        }
        if (!hit) return false;
      }
      if (SEARCH) {
        var s = SEARCH.toLowerCase();
        var t1 = String(meta.title || '').toLowerCase();
        var t2 = String(meta.dev || '').toLowerCase();
        if (t1.indexOf(s) < 0 && t2.indexOf(s) < 0) return false;
      }
      return true;
    });

    list.sort(function(a, b){
      var wa = isWished(a) ? 0 : 1;
      var wb = isWished(b) ? 0 : 1;
      return wa - wb;
    });


    if (!list.length) {
      var anyOwned = ORDER.some(function(g){ return hasLic(g); });
      var sub = (FILTER === 'owned' && !anyOwned) ? uiT('noOwnedSub')
              : (FILTER === 'wish' && !WISHLIST.length) ? uiT('noWishSub')
              : uiT('noGamesSub');
      var reqBtn = SEARCH
        ? '<button class="btn btn-primary req-btn" type="button" onclick="openGameReq()">'
          + '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M22 2L11 13"/><path d="M22 2l-7 20-4-9-9-4 20-7z"/></svg>'
          + '<span>' + uiEsc(uiT('reqBtn')) + '</span></button>'
        : '';
      grid.innerHTML = '<div class="empty">'
        + '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><circle cx="11" cy="11" r="7"/>'
        + '<line x1="21" y1="21" x2="16.65" y2="16.65"/></svg>'
        + '<b>' + uiEsc(uiT('noGames')) + '</b>'
        + '<span>' + uiEsc(sub) + '</span>'
        + reqBtn
        + '</div>';
      return;
    }


    var html = '';
    list.forEach(function(g){
      var meta = GAMES[g];
      if (!meta) return;
      var p = priceFor(g);
      var freeNow = isFreeGame(g);
      var owned = hasLic(g);
      var lic = licOf(g);
      var wished = isWished(g);

      var subBadge = '';
      if (owned && lic) {
        var sec = lic.seconds;
        var cls = '';
        if (sec >= 0 && sec < 3 * 86400) cls = ' expiring';
        if (sec === 0) cls = ' expired';
        var pausedTag = '';
        if (lic.paused) pausedTag = '<span class="paused-badge">' + uiEsc(uiT('paused')) + '</span>';
        subBadge = '<div class="card-sublen' + cls + '">'
          + '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24">'
          + '<circle cx="12" cy="12" r="9"/>'
          + '<path d="M12 7v5l3 2"/>'
          + '</svg>'
          + '<span>' + fmtDuration(sec) + '</span>'
          + pausedTag
          + '</div>';
      }


      html += '<div class="card" data-game="' + g + '" onclick="openDetail(\'' + g + '\')">';
      html += '<div class="card-img loading">';
      html += '<img src="' + meta.img + '" alt="" ';
      html += 'onload="this.classList.add(\'loaded\');this.parentNode.classList.remove(\'loading\')" ';
      html += 'onerror="var p=this.parentNode;this.remove();if(p)p.classList.remove(\'loading\')">';
      if (owned) html += '<span class="card-badge owned">' + uiEsc(uiT('badgeOwned')) + '</span>';
      else       html += '<span class="card-badge">' + uiEsc(uiT('badgeNew')) + '</span>';
      html += '<button class="card-wish' + (wished ? ' active' : '') + '" '
            + 'data-game="' + g + '" '
            + 'onclick="toggleWish(event,\'' + g + '\')" '
            + 'title="' + uiEsc(wished ? uiT('wishRemoveTitle') : uiT('wishAddTitle')) + '">'
            + '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M20.84 4.61a5.5 5.5 0 0 0-7.78 0L12 5.67l-1.06-1.06a5.5 5.5 0 0 0-7.78 7.78l1.06 1.06L12 21.23l7.78-7.78 1.06-1.06a5.5 5.5 0 0 0 0-7.78z"/></svg>'
            + '</button>';
      html += subBadge;
      html += '</div>';


      html += '<div class="card-body">';
      html += '<div class="card-title">' + uiEsc(meta.title || g) + '</div>';
      html += '<div class="card-sub">' + uiEsc(meta.dev || '');
      html += '<span class="dot"></span>' + uiEsc(tagT((meta.gen || [])[0] || ''));
      html += '</div>';
      html += '<div class="card-foot">';
      html += '<div class="card-price">';
      if (owned) {
        html += '<span class="new" style="background:linear-gradient(135deg,var(--ok),#34d399);';
        html += '-webkit-background-clip:text;background-clip:text">' + uiEsc(uiT('ownNow')) + '</span>';
      } else if (freeNow) {
        /* 售价写 0 = 免费：绿色 FREE，不显示划线原价 */
        html += '<span class="new free" data-ui="free">' + uiEsc(uiT('free')) + '</span>';
      } else {
        html += '<span class="old">$' + (p * 1.1).toFixed(2) + '</span>';
        html += '<span class="new">' + fmtPrice(g) + '</span>';
      }
      html += '</div>';
      if (owned) {
        html += '<div class="card-cta owned">';
        html += '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><polygon points="5 3 19 12 5 21 5 3"/></svg>';
        html += '<span>' + uiEsc(uiT('launch')) + '</span></div>';
      } else {
        html += '<div class="card-cta">';
        html += '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M12 5v14M5 12h14"/></svg>';
        html += '<span>' + uiEsc(uiT('buy')) + '</span></div>';
      }
      html += '</div></div></div>';
    });
    grid.innerHTML = html;
    /* 列表模式：左边列表 + 右边常驻详情块
       还没悬停过 → 显示列表第一个；已经悬停过 → 保持最后一次那款（还在列表里的话） */
    var wrap = document.getElementById('gridWrap');
    if (wrap) wrap.classList.toggle('as-list', LAYOUT === 'list');
    if (LAYOUT === 'list') {
      var still = PANE.code && grid.querySelector('.card[data-game="' + PANE.code + '"]');
      paneShow(still ? PANE.code : gridFirstCode());
    } else {
      paneHide();
    }
    if (typeof gpopHide === 'function' && GPOP.on) gpopHide();   /* 重排后位置就变了 */
  }


  /* Quickplay */
  var QUICK = (function(){
    try {
      if (UI_CFG && UI_CFG.quickSet && Array.isArray(UI_CFG.quick))
        return UI_CFG.quick.filter(function(g){ return !!GAMES[g]; });
    } catch (e) {}
    return ORDER.slice(0, 3);
  })();
  var QUICK_PICKER_OPEN = false;

  function saveQuick(){ wv('UI_QUICK:' + QUICK.join(',')); }

  function addQuick(ev, g){
    if (ev) ev.stopPropagation();
    if (!GAMES[g] || QUICK.indexOf(g) >= 0) return;
    QUICK.push(g);
    saveQuick();
    QUICK_PICKER_OPEN = false;
    renderQuickplay();
  }
  function removeQuick(ev, g){
    if (ev) ev.stopPropagation();
    var i = QUICK.indexOf(g);
    if (i < 0) return;
    QUICK.splice(i, 1);
    saveQuick();
    renderQuickplay();
  }
  function toggleQuickPicker(ev){
    if (ev) ev.stopPropagation();
    QUICK_PICKER_OPEN = !QUICK_PICKER_OPEN;
    renderQuickplay();
  }
  document.addEventListener('click', function(e){
    if (!QUICK_PICKER_OPEN) return;
    var t = e.target;
    if (t && t.closest && (t.closest('#qpPicker') || t.closest('#sbQuickAdd'))) return;
    QUICK_PICKER_OPEN = false;
    renderQuickplay();
  });


  function watchMiniIcons(root){
    root.querySelectorAll('.sb-mini-ico').forEach(function(c){
      var im = c.querySelector('img');
      if (im) uiImgWatch(c, im.getAttribute('src'));
    });
  }

  /* 侧栏拖动排序 */
  var SDND = null;
  var SDND_SUPPRESS = 0;

  function sdndPaint(){
    var g = SDND.ghost;
    g.style.translate = (SDND.px - SDND.gx) + 'px ' + (SDND.py - SDND.gy) + 'px';
    g.style.transformOrigin = SDND.gx + 'px ' + SDND.gy + 'px';
  }

  function sdndStart(e){
    var origin = SDND.el;
    var r = origin.getBoundingClientRect();
    SDND.pending = false;
    SDND.gx = SDND.sx - r.left; SDND.gy = SDND.sy - r.top;
    SDND.px = e.clientX; SDND.py = e.clientY;
    SDND.swapAt = 0;
    var ghost = origin.cloneNode(true);
    ghost.removeAttribute('onclick');
    ghost.classList.remove('s-origin');
    ghost.classList.add('sb-ghost');
    ghost.style.width = r.width + 'px';
    ghost.style.height = r.height + 'px';
    ghost.style.margin = '0';
    var x = ghost.querySelector('.sb-mini-x'); if (x) x.style.opacity = '0';
    document.body.appendChild(ghost);
    SDND.ghost = ghost;
    origin.classList.add('s-origin');
    document.body.classList.add('sdragging');
    try { SDND.list.setPointerCapture(SDND.pid); } catch (err) {}
    sdndPaint();
    requestAnimationFrame(function(){ if (SDND && SDND.ghost) SDND.ghost.classList.add('lift'); });
    SDND.raf = requestAnimationFrame(sdndTick);
  }


  function sdndTick(){
    if (!SDND || SDND.pending || SDND.finishing) return;
    SDND.raf = requestAnimationFrame(sdndTick);
    var list = SDND.list, origin = SDND.el;
    var lr = list.getBoundingClientRect();
    if (SDND.px < lr.left - 40 || SDND.px > lr.right + 40) return;
    var items = [].slice.call(list.querySelectorAll('.sb-mini[data-game]'));
    var now = performance.now();
    if (now - SDND.swapAt < 120) return;
    for (var i = 0; i < items.length; i++) {
      var it = items[i];
      if (it === origin) continue;
      var r = it.getBoundingClientRect();
      if (SDND.py >= r.top && SDND.py <= r.bottom) {
        SDND.swapAt = now;
        var before = new Map();
        items.forEach(function(c){ before.set(c, c.getBoundingClientRect()); });
        var ci = items.indexOf(origin);
        if (ci < i) it.after(origin); else it.before(origin);
        items.forEach(function(c){
          var a = before.get(c), b = c.getBoundingClientRect();
          var dy = a.top - b.top;
          if (!dy) return;
          c.animate([{ transform: 'translateY(' + dy + 'px)' }, { transform: 'translateY(0)' }],
                    { duration: 220, easing: 'cubic-bezier(.2,.8,.2,1)' });
        });
        break;
      }
    }
  }


  function sdndEnd(cancel){
    if (!SDND) return;
    if (SDND.pending) { SDND = null; return; }
    if (SDND.finishing) return;
    SDND_SUPPRESS = Date.now() + 400;
    SDND.finishing = true;
    var D = SDND;
    cancelAnimationFrame(D.raf);
    var items = [].slice.call(D.list.querySelectorAll('.sb-mini[data-game]'));
    var ids = items.map(function(c){ return c.getAttribute('data-game'); });
    if (!cancel) {
      if (D.kind === 'quick') {
        var nq = ids.filter(function(g){ return QUICK.indexOf(g) >= 0; });
        QUICK.forEach(function(g){ if (nq.indexOf(g) < 0) nq.push(g); });
        if (nq.join(',') !== QUICK.join(',')) { QUICK = nq; saveQuick(); }
      } else {
        var rest = DOWN_ORDER.filter(function(g){ return ids.indexOf(g) < 0; });
        var nd = ids.concat(rest);
        if (nd.join(',') !== DOWN_ORDER.join(',')) { DOWN_ORDER = nd; saveDownOrder(); }
      }
    }
    var tr = D.el.getBoundingClientRect();
    var g = D.ghost;
    var fx = D.px - D.gx, fy = D.py - D.gy;
    var t0 = performance.now(), dur = 200;
    g.classList.remove('lift');
    function step(now){
      var t = Math.min(1, (now - t0) / dur), e = 1 - Math.pow(1 - t, 3);
      g.style.translate = (fx + (tr.left - fx) * e) + 'px ' + (fy + (tr.top - fy) * e) + 'px';
      if (t < 1) { requestAnimationFrame(step); return; }
      if (g.parentNode) g.parentNode.removeChild(g);
      D.el.classList.remove('s-origin');
      document.body.classList.remove('sdragging');
      try { D.list.releasePointerCapture(D.pid); } catch (err) {}
      SDND = null;
    }
    requestAnimationFrame(step);
  }


  (function initSidebarDnd(){
    [['sbQuick', 'quick'], ['sbDown', 'down']].forEach(function(pair){
      var list = document.getElementById(pair[0]);
      if (!list) return;
      list.addEventListener('dragstart', function(e){ e.preventDefault(); });
      list.addEventListener('click', function(e){
        if (Date.now() < SDND_SUPPRESS) { e.stopPropagation(); e.preventDefault(); }
      }, true);
      list.addEventListener('pointerdown', function(e){
        if (e.button !== 0 || SDND || DND) return;
        if (e.target.closest && e.target.closest('.sb-mini-x')) return;
        var it = e.target.closest ? e.target.closest('.sb-mini[data-game]') : null;
        if (!it) return;
        if (list.querySelectorAll('.sb-mini[data-game]').length < 2) return;
        SDND = { pending: true, kind: pair[1], list: list, el: it, pid: e.pointerId,
                 sx: e.clientX, sy: e.clientY, px: e.clientX, py: e.clientY };
      });
    });
    document.addEventListener('pointermove', function(e){
      if (!SDND) return;
      SDND.px = e.clientX; SDND.py = e.clientY;
      if (SDND.pending) {
        var dx = e.clientX - SDND.sx, dy = e.clientY - SDND.sy;
        if (dx * dx + dy * dy < 25) return;
        sdndStart(e);
        return;
      }
      if (!SDND.finishing) sdndPaint();
    }, true);
    document.addEventListener('pointerup', function(){ if (SDND) sdndEnd(false); }, true);
    document.addEventListener('pointercancel', function(){ if (SDND) sdndEnd(true); }, true);
    document.addEventListener('keydown', function(e){
      if (e.key === 'Escape' && SDND && !SDND.pending) {
        if (SDND.kind === 'quick') renderQuickplay(); else renderDownloads();
        sdndEnd(true);
      }
    });
  })();


  function renderQuickplay(){
    var box = document.getElementById('sbQuick');
    var picker = document.getElementById('qpPicker');
    var addBtn = document.getElementById('sbQuickAdd');
    var html = '';
    QUICK.forEach(function(g){
      if (!GAMES[g]) return;
      html += '<div class="sb-mini" data-game="' + g + '" onclick="openDetail(\'' + g + '\')">';
      html += '<div class="sb-mini-ico"><img src="' + GAMES[g].img + '" alt=""></div>';
      html += '<span class="sb-mini-txt">' + uiEsc(GAMES[g].short || g) + '</span>';
      html += '<button class="sb-mini-x" type="button" title="' + uiEsc(uiT('quickRemove')) + '" '
            + 'onclick="removeQuick(event,\'' + g + '\')">'
            + '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M6 6l12 12M18 6L6 18"/></svg></button>';
      html += '</div>';
    });
    if (!QUICK.length) html += '<div class="qp-empty">' + uiEsc(uiT('quickEmpty')) + '</div>';
    box.innerHTML = html;
    watchMiniIcons(box);


    if (addBtn) {
      if (QUICK_PICKER_OPEN) addBtn.classList.add('open');
      else addBtn.classList.remove('open');
    }
    if (!picker) return;
    if (!QUICK_PICKER_OPEN) { picker.hidden = true; picker.innerHTML = ''; return; }
    var avail = ORDER.filter(function(g){ return QUICK.indexOf(g) < 0; });
    var ph = '<div class="qp-head">' + uiEsc(uiT('quickPick')) + '</div>';
    if (!avail.length) ph += '<div class="qp-empty">' + uiEsc(uiT('quickAllAdded')) + '</div>';
    avail.forEach(function(g){
      ph += '<div class="sb-mini" onclick="addQuick(event,\'' + g + '\')">';
      ph += '<div class="sb-mini-ico"><img src="' + GAMES[g].img + '" alt=""></div>';
      ph += '<span class="sb-mini-txt">' + uiEsc(GAMES[g].short || g) + '</span>';
      ph += '<svg class="sb-mini-plus" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M12 5v14M5 12h14"/></svg>';
      ph += '</div>';
    });
    picker.innerHTML = ph;
    picker.hidden = false;
    watchMiniIcons(picker);
  }


  var DOWN_ORDER = (function(){
    var out = [];
    try {
      if (UI_CFG && Array.isArray(UI_CFG.downOrder)) {
        UI_CFG.downOrder.forEach(function(g){ if (GAMES[g] && out.indexOf(g) < 0) out.push(g); });
      }
    } catch (e) {}
    ORDER.forEach(function(g){ if (out.indexOf(g) < 0) out.push(g); });
    return out;
  })();
  function saveDownOrder(){ wv('UI_DORDER:' + DOWN_ORDER.join(',')); }

  function renderDownloads(){
    var box = document.getElementById('sbDown');
    var list = DOWN_ORDER.filter(hasLic);
    if (!list.length) {
      box.innerHTML = '<div style="font-size:11.5px;'
        + 'color:var(--text-3);padding:8px 10px">'
        + uiEsc(uiT('noLicenses')) + '</div>';
      return;
    }
    var html = '';
    list.forEach(function(g){
      var lic = licOf(g);
      var sec = lic ? lic.seconds : 0;
      html += '<div class="sb-mini" data-game="' + g + '" onclick="openDetail(\'' + g + '\')">';
      html += '<div class="sb-mini-ico">';
      html += '<img src="' + GAMES[g].img + '" alt="">';
      html += '</div>';
      html += '<span class="sb-mini-txt">' + uiEsc(GAMES[g].short || g) + '</span>';
      html += '<span class="sb-mini-badge ok">' + fmtDuration(sec) + '</span>';
      html += '</div>';
    });
    box.innerHTML = html;
    watchMiniIcons(box);
  }


  function renderDetailHeroButtons(g){
    var box = document.getElementById('detailHeroBtns');
    if (!box) return;
    var owned = hasLic(g);
    var wished = isWished(g);

    var wishBtn = '<button class="btn btn-secondary' + (wished ? ' wished' : '') + '" '
                + 'id="detailWish" onclick="toggleWish(event,CURRENT_GAME)">'
                + '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M20.84 4.61a5.5 5.5 0 0 0-7.78 0L12 5.67l-1.06-1.06a5.5 5.5 0 0 0-7.78 7.78l1.06 1.06L12 21.23l7.78-7.78 1.06-1.06a5.5 5.5 0 0 0 0-7.78z"/></svg>'
                + '<span id="detailWishText">' + (wished ? uiT('wishlisted') : uiT('wishlist')) + '</span>'
                + '</button>';

    var mainBtn;
    if (owned) {
      mainBtn = '<button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="jumpToInject()">'
              + '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><polygon points="5 3 19 12 5 21 5 3"/></svg>'
              + '<span>' + uiEsc(uiT('launch')) + '</span></button>';
    } else {
      mainBtn = '<button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="renewGame(event,CURRENT_GAME)">'
              + '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M12 5v14M5 12h14"/></svg>'
              + '<span>' + uiEsc(uiT('buyNow')) + '</span></button>';
    }
    var pauseBtn = '';
    if (owned && licPausable(g)) {
      var pz = !!licOf(g).paused;
      pauseBtn = '<button class="btn btn-secondary" onclick="togglePause(event,CURRENT_GAME)">'
               + pauseIcon(pz)
               + '<span>' + uiEsc(uiT(pz ? 'resumeTime' : 'pauseTime')) + '</span></button>';
    }
    box.innerHTML = wishBtn + pauseBtn + mainBtn;
  }


  function jumpToInject(){
    var sec = document.getElementById('injectSection');
    if (sec && !sec.hidden) {
      sec.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'center' });
    }
  }

  function tagT(x){
    var k = 't' + String(x).replace(/[^A-Za-z]/g, '');
    var v = uiT(k);
    return v === k ? x : v;
  }
  function relT(r){
    if (r === 'TBA') return uiT('tba');
    if (/[A-Za-z]/.test(r)) {
      var t = Date.parse(r);
      if (!isNaN(t)) {
        try {
          return new Date(t).toLocaleDateString(UI_LOCALES[uiState.lang] || 'en-US',
            { year: 'numeric', month: 'short', day: 'numeric' });
        } catch (e) {}
      }
    }
    return r;
  }
  function renderDetailTexts(g){
    var meta = GAMES[g];
    if (!meta) return;
    var rel = relT(meta.rel || 'TBA');
    document.getElementById('detailRel').textContent = rel;
    document.getElementById('dRel').textContent = rel;
    var genHtml = '';
    (meta.gen || []).forEach(function(x){ genHtml += '<span class="tag">' + uiEsc(tagT(x)) + '</span>'; });
    document.getElementById('dGen').innerHTML = genHtml;
    var featHtml = '';
    (meta.feat || []).forEach(function(x){ featHtml += '<span class="tag">' + uiEsc(tagT(x)) + '</span>'; });
    document.getElementById('dFeat').innerHTML = featHtml;
  }


  function buildSlide(el, url, fallback){
    el.innerHTML = '';
    el.style.backgroundImage = '';
    el.removeAttribute('data-sl-url');
    if (!url) return;
    if (!isVideoUrl(url)) {
      el.style.backgroundImage = "url('" + url + "')";
      uiImgWatch(el, url);
      return;
    }
    var sk = document.createElement('div');
    sk.className = 'sl-skel';
    var v = document.createElement('video');
    v.className = 'detail-video';
    /* 音量/静音状态跨图集槽位共用：切到别的视频不会突然恢复默认 */
    v.muted = !!DV.muted; v.defaultMuted = !!DV.muted;
    v.volume = DV.vol;
    v.loop = true; v.playsInline = true;
    if (DV.muted) { v.setAttribute('muted', ''); }
    v.setAttribute('playsinline', '');
    v.setAttribute('webkit-playsinline', '');
    v.preload = 'auto';
    v.disablePictureInPicture = true;
    var ready = function(){ sk.classList.add('done'); };
    v.addEventListener('loadeddata', ready);
    v.addEventListener('canplay', ready);
    v.addEventListener('playing', ready);
    v.addEventListener('error', function(){
      sk.classList.add('done');
      if (v.parentNode) v.parentNode.removeChild(v);
      if (fallback) { el.style.backgroundImage = "url('" + fallback + "')"; }
      dvSync();          /* 视频挂了就退回图片，控制条跟着收起来 */
    });
    v.src = url;
    el.appendChild(v);
    el.appendChild(sk);
  }


  /* 图集槽位：按需要生成 / 回收，数量完全跟 games.txt 的 cover 张数走 */
  function detailSlideEls(){
    var box = document.getElementById('detailSlides');
    return box ? [].slice.call(box.children) : [];
  }
  function detailEnsureSlides(n){
    var box = document.getElementById('detailSlides');
    if (!box) return [];
    while (box.children.length < n) {
      var d = document.createElement('div');
      d.className = 'detail-slide';
      box.appendChild(d);
    }
    while (box.children.length > n) {
      box.removeChild(box.lastElementChild);
    }
    return [].slice.call(box.children);
  }

  function syncDetailVideos(){
    detailSlideEls().forEach(function(el, k){
      var v = el.querySelector('video');
      if (!v) return;
      if (k === DETAIL_IDX) {
        dvPlaySafe(v);
      } else {
        v.pause();
      }
    });
    dvSync();
  }

  function stopDetailVideos(){
    detailSlideEls().forEach(function(el){
      var v = el.querySelector('video');
      if (!v) return;
      v.pause();
      v.removeAttribute('src');
      try { v.load(); } catch (e) {}
    });
    var hero = dvHeroEl();
    if (hero) hero.classList.remove('has-video');
    dvUnbind();
    DV.video = null;
    dvPin(false);
  }

  /* ── 视频悬浮控件 ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
     当前展示的 cover 是视频时（hero 带 has-video），鼠标移入画面浮出控制条：
     播放/暂停 · 音量（点图标静音，悬停展开音量条）· 进度条 · 时间 · 全屏按钮
     控件只绑在“当前这张”视频上，切图时自动改绑；音量/静音跨槽位共用。 */
  var DV = { video: null, hEv: null, vol: 1, muted: true, pins: 0,
             hintOff: false,   /* 用户自己按过静音 → 别再弹“点喇叭开声音” */
             noAudio: false };  /* 这段视频确实没有音轨（解码字节数一直为 0） */
  var DV_ICO = {
    play:  '<svg class="fill" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M7.5 4.6v14.8L19.8 12z"/></svg>',
    pause: '<svg class="fill" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><rect x="6" y="4.5" width="4.2" height="15" rx="1.4"/><rect x="13.8" y="4.5" width="4.2" height="15" rx="1.4"/></svg>',
    volHigh: '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M11 5 6.5 9H3v6h3.5L11 19z"/><path d="M15.6 8.4a4.9 4.9 0 0 1 0 7.2"/><path d="M18.4 5.6a8.9 8.9 0 0 1 0 12.8"/></svg>',
    volLow:  '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M11 5 6.5 9H3v6h3.5L11 19z"/><path d="M15.6 8.4a4.9 4.9 0 0 1 0 7.2"/></svg>',
    volMute: '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M11 5 6.5 9H3v6h3.5L11 19z"/><path d="M16.4 9.6l5 4.8m0-4.8l-5 4.8"/></svg>',
    fsOn:  '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M4 9V4h5"/><path d="M20 9V4h-5"/><path d="M4 15v5h5"/><path d="M20 15v5h-5"/></svg>',
    fsOff: '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M9 4v5H4"/><path d="M15 4v5h5"/><path d="M9 20v-5H4"/><path d="M15 20v-5h5"/></svg>',
    pillMute: '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M11 5 6.5 9H3v6h3.5L11 19z"/><path d="M16.4 9.6l5 4.8m0-4.8l-5 4.8"/></svg>',
    pillWarn: '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M12 8v5"/><circle cx="12" cy="16.6" r=".6" fill="currentColor"/><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="9"/></svg>'
  };
  function dvById(id){ return document.getElementById(id); }
  function dvHeroEl(){ return document.querySelector('.detail-hero'); }
  function dvClock(sec){
    if (!isFinite(sec) || sec < 0) sec = 0;
    sec = Math.floor(sec);
    var h = Math.floor(sec / 3600), m = Math.floor((sec % 3600) / 60), s = sec % 60;
    return (h ? h + ':' + (m < 10 ? '0' : '') + m : '' + m) + ':' + (s < 10 ? '0' : '') + s;
  }
  function dvVideo(){
    var el = detailSlideEls()[DETAIL_IDX];
    return el ? el.querySelector('video') : null;
  }
  function dvPin(on){
    var bar = dvById('detailVui');
    if (bar) bar.classList.toggle('pinned', !!on);
  }
  /* 带声音自动播放可能被浏览器拦（尤其切图后）：退回静音起播，再用提示请用户点喇叭 */
  function dvPlaySafe(v){
    if (!v) return;
    var pr = v.play();
    if (pr && pr.catch) {
      pr.catch(function(){
        if (!v.paused) return;
        v.muted = true; v.defaultMuted = true; v.setAttribute('muted', '');
        DV.muted = true; DV.hintOff = false;
        var again = v.play();
        if (again && again.catch) again.catch(function(){});
        dvPaint();
      });
    }
  }

  /* ── 全屏 ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
     先加 .dv-fs 让画面铺满窗口，再顺手请求系统全屏：
     宿主（WebView2）没接管 ContainsFullScreenElementChanged 也不影响使用。 */
  function dvFsOn(){
    var hero = dvHeroEl();
    return !!hero && (document.fullscreenElement === hero || hero.classList.contains('dv-fs'));
  }
  function dvExitFull(){
    if (!dvFsOn()) return false;
    if (document.fullscreenElement) { try { document.exitFullscreen(); } catch (e) {} }
    var hero = dvHeroEl();
    if (hero) hero.classList.remove('dv-fs');
    dvPaint();
    return true;
  }
  function dvToggleFull(ev){
    if (ev) ev.stopPropagation();
    var hero = dvHeroEl();
    if (!hero) return;
    if (dvFsOn()) { dvExitFull(); return; }
    hero.classList.add('dv-fs');
    if (hero.requestFullscreen) {
      var pr = hero.requestFullscreen();
      if (pr && pr.catch) pr.catch(function(){});
    }
    dvPaint();
  }
  document.addEventListener('fullscreenchange', function(){
    var hero = dvHeroEl();
    if (hero && document.fullscreenElement !== hero) hero.classList.remove('dv-fs');
    dvPaint();
  });

  /* 改绑：切图 / 关详情页 / 视频加载失败都会走到这里 */
  function dvSync(){
    var v = dvVideo();
    if (DV.video !== v) { dvUnbind(); DV.video = v; if (v) dvBind(v); }
    var hero = dvHeroEl();
    if (hero) hero.classList.toggle('has-video', !!v);
    if (v) dvPaint();
  }
  function dvBind(v){
    var h = {
      upd:  function(){ dvPaint(); },
      play: function(){ dvPaint(); },
      pause:function(){ dvPaint(); },
      vol:  function(){ dvPaint(); }
    };
    v.addEventListener('timeupdate', h.upd);
    v.addEventListener('durationchange', h.upd);
    v.addEventListener('loadedmetadata', h.upd);
    v.addEventListener('play', h.play);
    v.addEventListener('pause', h.pause);
    v.addEventListener('ended', h.pause);
    v.addEventListener('volumechange', h.vol);
    DV.hEv = h;
    DV.noAudio = false;
    dvApplyVol(DV.vol > 0 ? DV.vol : 1, DV.muted);   /* 顺序在这里要紧：先音量、再静音 */
    dvWire();
    dvPaint();
  }
  function dvUnbind(){
    var v = DV.video, h = DV.hEv;
    if (v && h) {
      v.removeEventListener('timeupdate', h.upd);
      v.removeEventListener('durationchange', h.upd);
      v.removeEventListener('loadedmetadata', h.upd);
      v.removeEventListener('play', h.play);
      v.removeEventListener('pause', h.pause);
      v.removeEventListener('ended', h.pause);
      v.removeEventListener('volumechange', h.vol);
    }
    DV.hEv = null;
  }

  /* 把当前状态刷到控件上：图标 / 标题 / 进度 / 时间 / 音量 / 提示 */
  function dvPaint(){
    var v = DV.video;
    var play = dvById('dvPlay'), mute = dvById('dvMute');
    if (!v || !play || !mute) return;
    var playing = !v.paused && !v.ended;
    var dur = isFinite(v.duration) ? v.duration : 0;
    var cur = isFinite(v.currentTime) ? v.currentTime : 0;
    var pct = dur > 0 ? Math.min(100, Math.max(0, cur / dur * 100)) : 0;
    play.innerHTML = playing ? DV_ICO.pause : DV_ICO.play;
    play.title = uiT(playing ? 'videoPause' : 'videoPlay');
    var vol = v.muted ? 0 : v.volume;
    mute.innerHTML = vol <= 0 ? DV_ICO.volMute : (vol < .5 ? DV_ICO.volLow : DV_ICO.volHigh);
    mute.title = uiT(vol <= 0 ? 'videoUnmute' : 'videoMute');
    var fill = dvById('dvFill'), knob = dvById('dvKnob');
    if (fill) fill.style.width = pct + '%';
    if (knob) knob.style.left = pct + '%';
    var t = dvById('dvTime');
    if (t) t.textContent = dvClock(cur) + ' / ' + dvClock(dur);
    var vp = Math.round(vol * 100);
    var vFill = dvById('dvVolFill'), vKnob = dvById('dvVolKnob');
    if (vFill) vFill.style.height = vp + '%';
    if (vKnob) vKnob.style.bottom = vp + '%';
    /* 全屏按钮：进/出两套图标 */
    var full = dvById('dvFull');
    if (full) {
      var on = dvFsOn();
      full.innerHTML = on ? DV_ICO.fsOff : DV_ICO.fsOn;
      full.title = uiT(on ? 'videoExitFull' : 'videoFull');
    }
    /* 「点喇叭开声音」/「这段视频没有音轨」——两种情况互斥，共用一个胶囊 */
    if (cur > 1 && !v.muted && !v.paused && !DV.noAudio &&
        'webkitAudioDecodedByteCount' in v && v.webkitAudioDecodedByteCount === 0) {
      DV.noAudio = true;      /* 播放过 1 秒仍是 0 字节音频 → 这段素材确实没音轨 */
    }
    var pill = dvById('dvPill');
    if (pill) {
      if (vol <= 0 && !DV.hintOff) {
        pill.innerHTML = DV_ICO.pillMute + '<span>' + uiEsc(uiT('videoUnmuteHint')) + '</span>';
        pill.className = 'detail-vui-pill';
        pill.hidden = false;
      } else if (DV.noAudio && !v.muted) {
        pill.innerHTML = DV_ICO.pillWarn + '<span>' + uiEsc(uiT('videoNoAudio')) + '</span>';
        pill.className = 'detail-vui-pill static';
        pill.hidden = false;
      } else {
        pill.hidden = true;
      }
    }
  }

  function dvPillTap(ev){
    if (ev) ev.stopPropagation();
    var v = DV.video;
    if (!v) return;
    if (v.muted || v.volume === 0) dvToggleMute(ev);
  }

  function dvTogglePlay(ev){
    if (ev) ev.stopPropagation();
    var v = DV.video;
    if (!v) return;
    if (v.paused) {
      dvPlaySafe(v);
    } else {
      v.pause();
    }
    dvPaint();
  }

  /* 音量：>0 记成“上次的音量”，点图标静音后可一键还原 */
  function dvApplyVol(vol, muted){
    vol = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, isFinite(vol) ? vol : 1));
    if (vol > 0) DV.vol = vol;
    DV.muted = !!muted;
    var v = DV.video;
    if (v) {
      v.volume = vol;
      v.muted = !!muted;
      if (muted) {
        v.defaultMuted = true;
        v.setAttribute('muted', '');
      } else {
        /* 关键：把 muted 属性也一起摘掉，避免之后 load()/重新取流时又被静音 */
        v.defaultMuted = false;
        v.removeAttribute('muted');
        if (v.paused) dvPlaySafe(v);     /* 静音起播后再开声音：顺手把播放接上 */
      }
    }
    dvPaint();
  }
  function dvToggleMute(ev){
    if (ev) ev.stopPropagation();
    var v = DV.video;
    if (!v) return;
    var off = v.muted || v.volume === 0;    /* 当前静音 → 这一下是要开声音 */
    DV.hintOff = !off;                      /* 主动按静音就别再提示；主动开声音则重新武装提示 */
    dvApplyVol(off ? (DV.vol > 0 ? DV.vol : 1) : v.volume, !off);
  }
  function dvVolFromEvent(ev){
    var rail = dvById('dvVolRail');
    if (!rail) return;
    var r = rail.getBoundingClientRect();
    var p = r.height > 0 ? 1 - (ev.clientY - r.top) / r.height : 0;
    p = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, p));
    DV.hintOff = p <= 0;            /* 自己拖到 0：算主动静音，别提示 */
    dvApplyVol(p, p <= 0);
  }
  function dvSeekFromEvent(ev){
    var v = DV.video, box = dvById('dvSeek');
    if (!v || !box || !(v.duration > 0)) return;
    var r = box.getBoundingClientRect();
    var p = r.width > 0 ? (ev.clientX - r.left) / r.width : 0;
    p = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, p));
    v.currentTime = p * v.duration;
    dvPaint();
  }
  /* 进度条 / 音量条共用一套拖动逻辑（pointer capture，拖出画面也不丢） */
  function dvDrag(node, apply, ev){
    if (ev.button) return;
    ev.preventDefault(); ev.stopPropagation();
    node.classList.add('dragging');
    DV.pins++; dvPin(true);
    if (node.setPointerCapture) { try { node.setPointerCapture(ev.pointerId); } catch (e) {} }
    apply(ev);
    var move = function(e){ apply(e); };
    var up = function(){
      node.removeEventListener('pointermove', move);
      node.removeEventListener('pointerup', up);
      node.removeEventListener('pointercancel', up);
      node.classList.remove('dragging');
      DV.pins = Math.max(0, DV.pins - 1);
      dvPin(DV.pins > 0);
    };
    node.addEventListener('pointermove', move);
    node.addEventListener('pointerup', up);
    node.addEventListener('pointercancel', up);
  }
  var DV_WIRED = false;
  function dvWire(){
    if (DV_WIRED) return;
    var seek = dvById('dvSeek'), rail = dvById('dvVolRail'), hero = dvHeroEl();
    if (!seek || !rail || !hero) return;
    DV_WIRED = true;
    seek.addEventListener('pointerdown', function(e){ dvDrag(seek, dvSeekFromEvent, e); });
    rail.addEventListener('pointerdown', function(e){ dvDrag(rail, dvVolFromEvent, e); });
    /* 点画面 = 播放/暂停（点控件、标题、按钮、圆点不算） */
    hero.addEventListener('click', function(e){
      var t = e.target;
      if (!t || !DV.video) return;
      if (t.closest && t.closest('.detail-vui, .detail-ctrl, .detail-dot, .detail-hero-body')) return;
      dvTogglePlay(e);
    });
  }

  /* ── 详情页图集：完全由 games.txt 的 cover1/2/3 决定 ───────────────────
     写了几张就显示几张（1~3），空的不占位、重复的只算一次；
     一张都没写才退回 poster/icon。 */
  var DETAIL_COVERS = [];
  function detailCovers(g){
    var meta = GAMES[g] || {};
    var list = meta.covers || [];
    var out = [];
    for (var i = 0; i < list.length; i++) {
      var u = String(list[i] || '').trim();
      if (!u || out.indexOf(u) >= 0) continue;
      out.push(u);
    }
    if (!out.length) {
      var fb = String(meta.poster || meta.img || '').trim();
      if (fb) out.push(fb);
    }
    return out;
  }

  function openDetail(g){
    CURRENT_GAME = g;
    DETAIL_IDX = 0;
    var meta = GAMES[g];
    if (!meta) return;

    /* 图集来自 games.txt 的 cover，有几张就生成几个槽位 */
    DETAIL_COVERS = detailCovers(g);
    var fallback = meta.poster || meta.img || '';
    var slides = detailEnsureSlides(DETAIL_COVERS.length);
    for (var i = 0; i < slides.length; i++) {
      buildSlide(slides[i], DETAIL_COVERS[i] || '', fallback);
      slides[i].classList.toggle('active', i === 0);
    }


    document.getElementById('detailTitle').textContent = meta.title || g;
    document.getElementById('detailDev').textContent = meta.dev || '';
    document.getElementById('dDev').textContent = meta.dev || '';
    document.getElementById('dPub').textContent = meta.pub || '';
    document.getElementById('dPlat').textContent = 'Windows';
    renderDetailTexts(g);

    var owned = hasLic(g);
    document.getElementById('subSection').hidden = !owned;
    document.getElementById('injectSection').hidden = !owned;

    if (owned) {
      var lic = licOf(g);
      updateSubDisplay(lic);
      document.getElementById('injectThumb').src = meta.img;
      uiImgWatch(document.querySelector('.inject-thumb'), meta.img);
      document.getElementById('injectTitle').textContent = meta.title || g;
      document.getElementById('injectStatus').textContent = uiT('readyInject');
      document.getElementById('injectProg').style.width = '0%';
      document.getElementById('injectStart').hidden = false;
      document.getElementById('injectCancel').hidden = true;
      document.getElementById('injectPanel').classList.remove('active');
    }


    renderDetailHeroButtons(g);
    loadReviews();          /* What players say：每次打开详情页都刷一次 */

    DETAIL_TK++;
    document.getElementById('detail').classList.remove('closing');
    document.getElementById('detail').classList.add('open');
    /* 清掉可能残留的退场动画，避免下一次返回时页面错过这拍 */
    document.getElementById('storePage').classList.remove('page-back');
    document.getElementById('profilePage').classList.remove('page-back');
    document.querySelector('.detail-scroll').scrollTop = 0;
    syncDetailVideos();

    var nav = document.getElementById('detailNav');
    var navHtml = '';
    for (var j = 0; j < DETAIL_COVERS.length; j++) {
      navHtml += '<button class="detail-dot' + (j === 0 ? ' active' : '') + '"'
              + ' onclick="detailGo(' + j + ')"></button>';
    }
    nav.innerHTML = navHtml;
    nav.hidden = DETAIL_COVERS.length < 2;        /* 只有一张图就不显示圆点 */
    var hero = document.querySelector('.detail-hero');
    if (hero) hero.classList.toggle('single', DETAIL_COVERS.length < 2);  /* 也藏掉左右箭头 */
  }

  function detailGo(i){
    var n = DETAIL_COVERS.length || 1;
    DETAIL_IDX = ((i % n) + n) % n;
    detailSlideEls().forEach(function(el, k){
      el.classList.toggle('active', k === DETAIL_IDX);
    });
    var dots = document.querySelectorAll('.detail-dot');
    for (var m = 0; m < dots.length; m++) {
      dots[m].classList.toggle('active', m === DETAIL_IDX);
    }
    syncDetailVideos();
  }


  function detailPrev(ev){ if (ev) ev.stopPropagation(); detailGo(DETAIL_IDX - 1); }
  function detailNext(ev){ if (ev) ev.stopPropagation(); detailGo(DETAIL_IDX + 1); }

  var DETAIL_TK = 0;
  function closeDetail(){
    var d = document.getElementById('detail');
    CURRENT_GAME = '';
    if (!d.classList.contains('open')) return;
    dvExitFull();                    /* 全屏里直接返回：先把全屏退掉，别把画面缩在固定层里 */
    var tk = ++DETAIL_TK;
    d.classList.add('closing');
    /* 下面的页面同时轻轻“落回”原位，返回时才不会显得生硬 */
    var page = document.getElementById(currentPage() === 'profile' ? 'profilePage' : 'storePage');
    if (page) {
      page.classList.remove('page-back');
      void page.offsetWidth;
      page.classList.add('page-back');
    }
    setTimeout(function(){
      if (tk !== DETAIL_TK) return;
      d.classList.remove('open');
      d.classList.remove('closing');
      if (page) { page.classList.remove('page-back'); }
      stopDetailVideos();
    }, 320);
  }

  function updateSubDisplay(lic){
    if (!lic) return;
    var timeEl = document.getElementById('subTime');
    var metaEl = document.getElementById('subMeta');
    if (!timeEl || !metaEl) return;
    var sec = lic.seconds;
    timeEl.className = 'sub-time';
    var pb = document.getElementById('subPauseBtn');
    if (pb) {
      var canPause = !!(lic.pausable && lic.type !== 'LIFETIME');
      pb.hidden = !canPause;
      if (canPause) {
        document.getElementById('subPauseIco').innerHTML = pauseIcon(!!lic.paused);
        document.getElementById('subPauseTxt').textContent = uiT(lic.paused ? 'resumeTime' : 'pauseTime');
      }
    }
    if (sec < 0) {
      timeEl.textContent = 'LIFETIME';
    } else if (lic.paused) {
      timeEl.classList.add('paused');
      timeEl.textContent = fmtDuration(sec) + ' (' + uiT('paused') + ')';
    } else {
      if (sec < 3 * 86400) timeEl.classList.add('expired');
      timeEl.textContent = fmtDuration(sec);
    }
    var parts = [];
    if (lic.type) parts.push(lic.type);
    if (lic.expiry && lic.type !== 'LIFETIME') {
      var dateOnly = String(lic.expiry).split('T')[0];
      parts.push('Until ' + dateOnly);
    }
    metaEl.textContent = parts.join(' \u00B7 ');
  }


  var injectTimer = null;
  var injectPct = 0;         /* 详情页进度条当前值 */
  var injectRealPct = -1;    /* 宿主上报的真实下载百分比；-1 = 还没收到 */
  var injectGame = '';
  var sbTaskTimer = 0;

  /* ── 侧栏任务卡：Settings 上方显示「正在启动的游戏 + 下载进度」 ── */
  function sbTaskEls(){
    return {
      box:  document.getElementById('sbTask'),
      ico:  document.getElementById('sbTaskIco'),
      name: document.getElementById('sbTaskName'),
      stat: document.getElementById('sbTaskStat'),
      pct:  document.getElementById('sbTaskPct'),
      fill: document.getElementById('sbTaskFill')
    };
  }
  function sbTaskProgress(pct){
    var e = sbTaskEls();
    if (!e.box || e.box.hidden) return;
    var p = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, Math.round(pct)));
    if (e.fill) e.fill.style.width = p + '%';
    if (e.pct)  e.pct.textContent = p + '%';
  }
  function sbTaskShow(g, text){
    var e = sbTaskEls();
    if (!e.box) return;
    clearTimeout(sbTaskTimer);
    var meta = GAMES[g] || {};
    e.box.hidden = false;
    e.box.classList.remove('done', 'err', 'closing');   /* 打断正在播的退场动画 */
    if (e.ico)  e.ico.src = meta.img || '';
    if (e.name) e.name.textContent = meta.title || meta.short || g || '';
    if (e.stat) e.stat.textContent = text || uiT('injecting');
    sbTaskProgress(0);
  }
  function sbTaskStage(text){
    var e = sbTaskEls();
    if (!e.box || e.box.hidden || !e.stat) return;
    e.stat.textContent = text;
  }
  /* 消失：先播顺着侧栏那一侧的滑出动画，动画播完再真正隐藏 */
  var SB_TASK_OUT_MS = 300;
  function sbTaskHide(){
    clearTimeout(sbTaskTimer);
    var e = sbTaskEls();
    if (!e.box || e.box.hidden) return;
    if (e.box.classList.contains('closing')) return;    /* 已经在退场了 */
    e.box.classList.remove('done', 'err');
    e.box.classList.add('closing');
    sbTaskTimer = setTimeout(function(){
      e.box.classList.remove('closing');
      e.box.hidden = true;
    }, SB_TASK_OUT_MS + 40);
  }
  function sbTaskFinish(kind, text, delay){
    var e = sbTaskEls();
    if (!e.box) return;
    clearTimeout(sbTaskTimer);
    e.box.hidden = false;
    e.box.classList.remove('closing');
    e.box.classList.toggle('done', kind === 'done');
    e.box.classList.toggle('err',  kind === 'err');
    if (e.stat && text) e.stat.textContent = text;
    if (kind === 'done') { sbTaskProgress(100); }
    sbTaskTimer = setTimeout(sbTaskHide, delay || 5000);
  }

  function doInject(){
    if (!CURRENT_GAME) return;
    var start = document.getElementById('injectStart');
    var cancel = document.getElementById('injectCancel');
    var fill = document.getElementById('injectProg');
    var stat = document.getElementById('injectStatus');
    var panel = document.getElementById('injectPanel');

    start.hidden = true;
    cancel.hidden = false;
    panel.classList.add('active');
    stat.textContent = uiT('injecting');
    fill.style.width = '0%';

    injectGame = CURRENT_GAME;
    injectPct = 0;
    injectRealPct = -1;
    sbTaskShow(CURRENT_GAME, uiT('injecting'));

    injectTimer = setInterval(function(){
      if (injectRealPct >= 0) {
        /* 有真实进度：只做平滑跟随，剩下的交给宿主的完成消息 */
        if (injectPct < injectRealPct) {
          injectPct += Math.max(0.4, (injectRealPct - injectPct) * 0.25);
          if (injectPct > injectRealPct) { injectPct = injectRealPct; }
        } else if (injectPct < 99) {
          injectPct += 0.05;
        }
      } else {
        /* 还没收到真实进度：先走估算，最多到 96%，不假装已经完成 */
        injectPct += 3 + Math.random() * 7;
        if (injectPct > 96) { injectPct = 96; }
      }
      var p = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, injectPct));
      fill.style.width = p + '%';
      sbTaskProgress(p);
    }, 110);


    /* 发送 LAUNCH 消息给宿主：宿主负责下载 / 解压 / 运行或注入 */
    wv('LAUNCH:' + CURRENT_GAME);
  }

  /* 宿主上报真实进度：详情页面板与侧栏任务卡一起更新 */
  function injectOnProgress(code, pct){
    injectRealPct = pct;
    if (code && code === injectGame) {
      var panel = document.getElementById('injectPanel');
      if (panel && panel.classList.contains('active') && pct > injectPct) {
        injectPct = pct;
        document.getElementById('injectProg').style.width = Math.min(99, pct) + '%';
      }
    }
    if (code && code !== injectGame) { return; }
    sbTaskProgress(pct);
  }

  /* 启动成功：面板收尾成「已启动」，侧栏任务卡短暂停留后自动消失 */
  function injectOnOk(code){
    if (injectTimer) { clearInterval(injectTimer); injectTimer = null; }
    var fill = document.getElementById('injectProg');
    var stat = document.getElementById('injectStatus');
    var cancel = document.getElementById('injectCancel');
    var start = document.getElementById('injectStart');
    var panel = document.getElementById('injectPanel');
    if (fill) fill.style.width = '100%';
    if (stat) stat.textContent = uiT('injected');
    sbTaskFinish('done', uiT('injected'), 4500);
    setTimeout(function(){
      if (cancel) cancel.hidden = true;
      if (start) start.hidden = false;
      if (fill) fill.style.width = '0%';
      if (stat) stat.textContent = uiT('readyInject');
      if (panel) panel.classList.remove('active');
    }, 1600);
  }

  function cancelInject(){
    if (injectTimer) { clearInterval(injectTimer); injectTimer = null; }
    injectRealPct = -1;
    document.getElementById('injectStart').hidden = false;
    document.getElementById('injectCancel').hidden = true;
    document.getElementById('injectProg').style.width = '0%';
    document.getElementById('injectStatus').textContent = uiT('readyInject');
    document.getElementById('injectPanel').classList.remove('active');
    sbTaskHide();
  }

    'https://api.dicebear.com/7.x/bottts-neutral/svg?seed=1',

  function renderAvatarGrid(){
    var box = document.getElementById('avatarGrid');
    var html = '';
    AVATARS.forEach(function(url){
      var cls = 'avatar-option';
      if (USER.avatar === url) cls += ' active';
      html += '<div class="' + cls + '" onclick="setAvatar(\'' + url + '\')">';
      html += '<img src="' + url + '" alt="">';
      html += '</div>';
    });
    box.innerHTML = html;
  }

  function setAvatar(url){
    USER.avatar = url;
    saveUser();
    applyUser();
    renderAvatarGrid();
    toast(uiT('avatarUpdated'), 'ok', 2000);
  }

  function pickAvatar(){
    document.getElementById('avatarGrid').scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'center' });
  }


  /* 个人资料 → 偏好：导航侧栏放左边还是右边 */
  function renderProfileRail(){
    var box = document.getElementById('profileRailOpts');
    if (!box) return;
    var cur = (typeof uiLayoutNormal === 'function')
      ? uiLayoutNormal(typeof uiState !== 'undefined' ? uiState.editorLayout : 'rail-l') : 'rail-l';
    var locked = (typeof uiLayoutLocked === 'function') && uiLayoutLocked();
    var defs = [
      { id:'rail-l', key:'layoutRailL', cls:'rl' },
      { id:'rail-r', key:'layoutRailR', cls:'rr' }
    ];
    var html = '';
    defs.forEach(function(d){
      var on = (d.id === cur) ? ' active' : '';
      html += '<button class="layout-opt' + on + '" type="button" data-rail="' + d.id + '"' +
        (locked ? ' disabled' : ' onclick="setProfileRail(\'' + d.id + '\')"') + '>' +
        '<div class="layout-prev rail ' + d.cls + '"><div class="rp-rail"></div><div class="rp-body"></div></div>' +
        '<span>' + uiEsc(uiT(d.key)) + '</span></button>';
    });
    box.innerHTML = html;
  }
  function setProfileRail(id){
    if (typeof uiOnLayoutPick !== 'function') return;
    uiOnLayoutPick(id);
    renderProfileRail();
  }

  function renderProfileLangs(){
    var box = document.getElementById('profileLangGrid');
    if (typeof UI_LANGS === 'undefined') return;
    var html = '';
    UI_LANGS.forEach(function(lang){
      var isActive = (typeof uiState !== 'undefined' && uiState.lang === lang.code);
      var cls = 'lang-option';
      if (isActive) cls += ' active';
      html += '<button class="' + cls + '" onclick="uiSetLang(\'' + lang.code + '\')">';
      html += '<span class="lang-flag">' + uiFlagSvg(lang.flag) + '</span>';
      html += '<span class="lang-name">';
      html += '<span class="lang-native">' + lang.native + '</span>';
      html += '<span class="lang-latin">' + lang.latin + '</span>';
      html += '</span></button>';
    });
    box.innerHTML = html;
  }

  var dpiSlider = document.getElementById('dpiSlider');
  var dpiValue = document.getElementById('dpiValue');


  function setDPI(v){
    v = Math.max(80, Math.min(150, v));
    USER.dpi = v;
    dpiSlider.value = v;
    dpiValue.textContent = v + '%';
    applyDPI(v);
    document.querySelectorAll('.dpi-preset').forEach(function(b){
      var bv = parseInt(b.getAttribute('data-dpi'), 10);
      if (bv === v) b.classList.add('active');
      else b.classList.remove('active');
    });
    saveUser();
  }

  dpiSlider.addEventListener('input', function(){
    setDPI(parseInt(this.value, 10));
  });

  document.getElementById('dpiPresets').addEventListener('click', function(e){
    var b = e.target.closest('.dpi-preset');
    if (!b) return;
    setDPI(parseInt(b.getAttribute('data-dpi'), 10));
  });

  function setActiveNav(id){
    document.querySelectorAll('.sb-item').forEach(function(b){
      if (b.id === id) b.classList.add('active');
      else b.classList.remove('active');
    });
  }


  function currentPage(){
    var p = document.getElementById('profilePage');
    return (p && !p.hidden) ? 'profile' : 'store';
  }

  function showPage(page){
    if (page === 'profile') { showProfile(); } else { showStore(); }
  }

  function showStore(){
    closeDetail();
    document.getElementById('profilePage').hidden = true;
    document.getElementById('adminPage').hidden = true;
    document.getElementById('storePage').hidden = false;
    setActiveNav('navGames');
  }

  function showProfile(){
    closeDetail();
    document.getElementById('storePage').hidden = true;
    document.getElementById('adminPage').hidden = true;
    document.getElementById('profilePage').hidden = false;
    setActiveNav('navProfile');
    renderAvatarGrid();
    renderProfileLangs();
    renderProfileRail();
    renderLayoutOpts();
    renderBgOpts();
    applyUser();
    updateProfileBadges();
    wv('MYINFO');        /* 每次打开个人资料都向网站要一次实时组 */
  }

  /* 打开设置/续费/更新日志前，记住当前是哪一页、正在看哪个游戏
     关掉之后原路退回，而不是一律回到商店页 */
  var VIEW_FROM_PAGE = 'store';
  var VIEW_FROM_GAME = '';

  function onUiViewOpened(view){
    if (view === 'settings' || view === 'renew' || view === 'changelog') {
      VIEW_FROM_PAGE = currentPage();
      VIEW_FROM_GAME = CURRENT_GAME || '';
      closeDetail();
    }
  }

  /* ESC：详情页 → 返回上一页；个人资料 → 返回商店页 */
  function onUiEscBack(){
    /* Genres 弹层最先生效，ESC 先把它收掉 */
    if (typeof genreOpen === 'function' && genreOpen()) { closeGenrePop(); return true; }
    /* 视频全屏时 ESC 只退全屏，第二次 ESC 才关详情页 */
    if (dvExitFull()) { return true; }
    /* 弹窗 / 拖拽这类浮层自己会处理 ESC，别顺手把详情页也关掉 */
    var rm = document.getElementById('reqModal');
    if (rm && rm.classList.contains('open')) { return true; }
    if (typeof DND !== 'undefined' && DND) { return true; }
    if (typeof SDND !== 'undefined' && SDND) { return true; }
    var d = document.getElementById('detail');
    if (d && d.classList.contains('open')) { closeDetail(); return true; }
    if (currentPage() === 'profile') { showStore(); return true; }
    return false;
  }

  var RETURN_GAME = '';
  function onUiViewClosed(){
    var page = VIEW_FROM_PAGE, game = VIEW_FROM_GAME;
    VIEW_FROM_PAGE = 'store';
    VIEW_FROM_GAME = '';
    showPage(page);
    var back = RETURN_GAME || game;
    RETURN_GAME = '';
    if (back && GAMES[back]) { openDetail(back); }
  }


  function onUiLangChanged(){
    if (CURRENT_GAME && GAMES[CURRENT_GAME]) {
      renderDetailTexts(CURRENT_GAME);
      renderDetailHeroButtons(CURRENT_GAME);
      if (hasLic(CURRENT_GAME)) updateSubDisplay(licOf(CURRENT_GAME));
      var st = document.getElementById('injectStatus');
      var go = document.getElementById('injectStart');
      if (st && go && !go.hidden) st.textContent = uiT('readyInject');
    }
    if (DV.video) dvPaint();     /* 视频控件的 title 跟着语言走 */
    renderHero();
    renderGrid();
    renderQuickplay();
    renderDownloads();
    renderGenreBtn();      /* 类型名/“Genres” 文案跟着语言走 */
    renderGenrePop();
    renderProfileLangs();
    renderLayoutOpts();
    renderBgOpts();
  }

  function renderBgOpts(){
    var tg = document.getElementById('bgFxToggle');
    var box = document.getElementById('bgStyles');
    if (!tg || !box) return;
    tg.checked = BGFX_ON;
    box.classList.toggle('disabled', !BGFX_ON);
    var html = '';
    BGFX_STYLES.forEach(function(k){
      var prev = '<span class="bg-prev p-' + k + '"'
        + (k === 'image' && BG_IMAGE ? ' style="background-image:url(\'' + BG_IMAGE + '\')"' : '') + '>'
        + (k === 'image' && !BG_IMAGE ? '+' : '') + '</span>';
      html += '<button type="button" class="bg-tile' + (k === BGFX_STYLE ? ' active' : '') + '" '
            + 'onclick="setBgStyle(\'' + k + '\')">' + prev
            + '<span class="bg-name">' + uiEsc(uiT(BGFX_NAME_KEY[k])) + '</span></button>';
    });
    box.innerHTML = html;
    var row = document.getElementById('bgImageRow');
    if (row) row.hidden = !BGFX_ON;
    var clr = document.getElementById('bgClearBtn');
    if (clr) clr.hidden = !BG_IMAGE;
  }


  function setBgFx(on){
    BGFX_ON = !!on;
    wv('UI_BGFX:' + (BGFX_ON ? '1' : '0'));
    bgfxApply(); renderBgOpts();
  }
  function setBgStyle(st){
    if (BGFX_STYLES.indexOf(st) < 0) return;
    if (st === 'image' && !BG_IMAGE) { pickBgImage(); return; }
    BGFX_STYLE = st;
    wv('UI_BGSTYLE:' + st);
    bgfxApply(); renderBgOpts();
  }
  function pickBgImage(){
    var f = document.getElementById('bgFile');
    if (f) f.click();
  }
  function clearBgImage(){
    BG_IMAGE = '';
    wv('UI_BGIMG_CLEAR');
    if (BGFX_STYLE === 'image') { BGFX_STYLE = 'aurora'; wv('UI_BGSTYLE:aurora'); }
    bgfxApply(); renderBgOpts();
  }

  function bgfxCompress(src, cb){
    var im = new Image();
    im.onload = function(){
      var w0 = im.naturalWidth, h0 = im.naturalHeight;
      if (!w0 || !h0) { cb(null); return; }
      var base = Math.min(1, 1920 / w0, 1200 / h0);
      var tries = [[1, .82], [1, .7], [.8, .7], [.65, .6], [.5, .55]];
      for (var i = 0; i < tries.length; i++) {
        var c = document.createElement('canvas');
        c.width = Math.max(2, Math.round(w0 * base * tries[i][0]));
        c.height = Math.max(2, Math.round(h0 * base * tries[i][0]));
        var x = c.getContext('2d');
        if (!x) { cb(null); return; }
        x.fillStyle = '#0b0b0f'; x.fillRect(0, 0, c.width, c.height);
        x.drawImage(im, 0, 0, c.width, c.height);
        var out = c.toDataURL('image/jpeg', tries[i][1]);
        if (out.length <= BGFX_MAX_CHARS - 2000) { cb(out); return; }
      }
      cb(null);
    };
    im.onerror = function(){ cb(null); };
    im.src = src;
  }
  (function(){
    var f = document.getElementById('bgFile');
    if (!f) return;
    f.addEventListener('change', function(){
      var file = f.files && f.files[0];
      f.value = '';
      if (!file) return;
      if (!/^image\//.test(file.type)) { toast(uiT('bgImageBad'), 'err', 2500); return; }
      var rd = new FileReader();
      rd.onerror = function(){ toast(uiT('bgImageBad'), 'err', 2500); };
      rd.onload = function(){
        bgfxCompress(String(rd.result), function(data){
          if (!data) { toast(uiT('bgImageBad'), 'err', 2500); return; }
          BG_IMAGE = data; BGFX_STYLE = 'image'; BGFX_ON = true;
          wv('UI_BGIMG:' + data);
          wv('UI_BGSTYLE:image');
          wv('UI_BGFX:1');
          bgfxApply(); renderBgOpts();
          toast(uiT('bgImageSet'), 'ok', 2000);
        });
      };
      rd.readAsDataURL(file);
    });
  })();


  bgfxApply();

  var searchInput = document.getElementById('searchInput');
  /* 性能：每敲一个字就重建整片卡片（含重新加载封面图）会明显卡手
     这里做个防抖，停手 140ms 后再渲染一次。 */
  var searchTimer = 0;
  searchInput.addEventListener('input', function(){
    var v = this.value.trim();
    if (searchTimer) { clearTimeout(searchTimer); }
    searchTimer = setTimeout(function(){
      searchTimer = 0;
      if (v === SEARCH) { return; }
      SEARCH = v;
      renderGrid();
    }, 140);
  });

  document.getElementById('secFilter').addEventListener('click', function(e){
    var btn = e.target.closest('button');
    if (!btn) return;
    setFilter(btn.getAttribute('data-filter'));
  });

  function onHeroBuy(){
    if (hasLic(HERO_GAME)) {
      openDetail(HERO_GAME);
      setTimeout(function(){ jumpToInject(); }, 300);
    } else {
      renewGame(null, HERO_GAME);
    }
  }

  function renewGame(ev, g){
    if (ev) ev.stopPropagation();
    /* 把“当前点的那个游戏”带进 Renew 页：价格、输入框前缀、封面/订阅时间都靠它 */
    var code = g || CURRENT_GAME || HERO_GAME || '';
    RETURN_GAME = CURRENT_GAME || '';
    if (typeof uiOpenRenew === 'function') uiOpenRenew(code);
  }

  function uiToggleTheme(){
    var cur = document.documentElement.getAttribute('data-theme') || 'dark';
    var next = 'dark';
    if (cur === 'dark') next = 'light';
    if (typeof uiSetTheme === 'function') uiSetTheme(next);
  }


  window.chrome.webview.addEventListener('message', function(e){
    var msg = e.data;
    if (typeof msg !== 'string') return;

    if (msg.indexOf('LICENSES:') === 0) {
      try {
        LICENSES = JSON.parse(msg.slice(9));
        ACTIVE_GAME = LICENSES.active || ACTIVE_GAME;
        renderHero();
        renderGrid();
        renderQuickplay();
        renderDownloads();
        updateProfileBadges();          /* 订阅变了，PRO 徽标跟着变 */
        if (CURRENT_GAME) {
          var owned = hasLic(CURRENT_GAME);
          document.getElementById('subSection').hidden = !owned;
          document.getElementById('injectSection').hidden = !owned;
          if (owned) updateSubDisplay(licOf(CURRENT_GAME));
          renderDetailHeroButtons(CURRENT_GAME);
        }
      } catch (err) {}
    }
    else if (msg === 'PAUSE_DENIED') {
      toast(uiT('pauseBlocked'), 'err', 3500);
    }
    else if (msg === 'PAUSE_ERR') {
      toast(uiT('errNet'), 'err', 3000);
    }
    else if (msg.indexOf('PAUSE_ERR:') === 0) {
      toast(msg.slice(10) || uiT('errNet'), 'err', 3500);
    }
    else if (msg === 'REQ_OK') {
      reqSetBusy(false); closeGameReq();
      toast(uiT('reqOk'), 'ok', 3000);
    }
    else if (msg.indexOf('REQ_ERR:') === 0) {
      reqSetBusy(false);
      var rc = msg.slice(8);
      if (rc === 'GAME') { document.getElementById('reqGame').classList.add('is-err'); toast(uiT('reqErrGame'), 'err', 2500); }
      else if (rc === 'RATE') { toast(uiT('reqErrRate'), 'err', 3000); }
      else if (rc === 'AUTH') { toast(uiT('errAuth'), 'err', 3000); }
      else if (rc.indexOf('TEXT:') === 0) { toast(rc.slice(5) || uiT('accErrFail'), 'err', 3500); }
      else { toast(uiT('errNet'), 'err', 3000); }
    }
    else if (msg.indexOf('TASK_BEGIN:') === 0) {
      var bg = msg.slice(11);
      if (bg && GAMES[bg]) { sbTaskShow(bg, uiT('injecting')); }
    }
    else if (msg.indexOf('TASK_PROG:') === 0) {
      var pp = msg.slice(10).split(':');
      var pv = parseInt(pp[1], 10);
      if (isFinite(pv)) { injectOnProgress(pp[0], pv); }
    }
    else if (msg.indexOf('TASK_STAGE:') === 0) {
      var ss2 = msg.slice(11).split(':');
      if (ss2[1] === 'extract') { sbTaskStage(uiT('extracting')); }
    }
    else if (msg.indexOf('INJECT_OK:') === 0) {
      toast(uiT('injectOk'), 'ok');
      injectOnOk(msg.slice(10));
    }
    else if (msg.indexOf('RUNS:') === 0) {
      /* 启动成功后宿主把新的运行次数发回来，就地更新，不用重开程序 */
      var rParts = msg.slice(5).split('|');
      var rCode = rParts[0];
      if (GAMES[rCode]) {
        GAMES[rCode].runs = parseInt(rParts[1], 10) || 0;
        GAMES[rCode].runsWeek = parseInt(rParts[2], 10) || 0;
      }
      if (typeof gpopRefreshStats === 'function') gpopRefreshStats(rCode);
    }
    else if (msg.indexOf('GAMESUPD:') === 0) {
      /* 宿主后台拉到新的 games.txt：GAMESUPD:<games json>\n<顺序 csv>，就地换上 */
      var gBody = msg.slice(9);
      var nl = gBody.indexOf('\n');
      var gJson = (nl >= 0) ? gBody.slice(0, nl) : gBody;
      var gOrder = (nl >= 0) ? gBody.slice(nl + 1) : '';
      if (typeof applyGamesUpdate === 'function') applyGamesUpdate(gJson, gOrder);
    }
    else if (msg.indexOf('INJECT_ERR:') === 0) {
      var parts = msg.split(':');
      var reason = '';
      if (parts.length > 2) reason = parts.slice(2).join(':');
      toast(uiT('injectFail') + uiErrText(reason), 'err');
      cancelInject();
      sbTaskFinish('err', uiT('injectFail') + uiErrText(reason), 7000);
    }
    else if (msg.indexOf('RENEW_OK:') === 0) {
      if (typeof uiRenewResult === 'function') {
        uiRenewResult(true, msg.slice(9));
      }
    }
    else if (msg.indexOf('RENEW_ERR:') === 0) {
      if (typeof uiRenewResult === 'function') {
        uiRenewResult(false, msg.slice(10));
      }
    }
    else if (msg.indexOf('REVIEWS_RAW:') === 0) {
      /* 宿主把网站返回的原文 JSON 直接转过来，这里自己 parse */
      try {
        var rv = JSON.parse(msg.slice(12));
        REVIEWS = (rv && rv.reviews) || [];
      } catch (err) { REVIEWS = []; }
      REV_LIMIT = REV_FIRST;        /* 新数据回来：回到首屏 3 条 */
      renderReviews();
    }
    else if (msg.indexOf('REVIEWS_ERR:') === 0) {
      REVIEWS = [];
      var revBox = document.getElementById('revList');
      var revEmpty = document.getElementById('revEmpty');
      if (revBox) revBox.innerHTML = '';
      if (revEmpty) { revEmpty.textContent = uiT('revErrLoad'); revEmpty.hidden = false; }
    }
    else if (msg === 'REVIEW_OK') {
      revSetBusy(false);
      closeReviewModal();
      toast(uiT('revOk'), 'ok', 3500);
      REVIEWS = null;
      wv('REVIEWS');          /* 立刻再拉一次（新评价要审核过后才会出现） */
    }
    else if (msg.indexOf('REVIEW_ERR:') === 0) {
      revSetBusy(false);
      var rc = msg.slice(11);
      if (rc === 'SHORT')        { toast(uiT('revErrShort'), 'err', 2500); }
      else if (rc === 'PENDING') { toast(uiT('revPending'), 'err', 3500); }
      else if (rc === 'AUTH')    { toast(uiT('errAuth'), 'err', 3000); }
      else if (rc.indexOf('TEXT:') === 0) { toast(rc.slice(5) || uiT('accErrFail'), 'err', 3500); }
      else                       { toast(uiT('errNet'), 'err', 3000); }
    }
    else if (msg.indexOf('MYGROUP:') === 0) {
      /* 网站回传的实时组：正常用户 = release，管理员 = admin */
      var gp = msg.slice(8).split('|');
      if (gp[0]) MY_GROUP = gp[0];
      if (gp[1]) MY_ROLE  = gp[1];
      updateProfileGroup();
    }
    else if (msg.indexOf('ADMIN_RAW:') === 0) {
      var arest  = msg.slice(10);
      var acolon = arest.indexOf(':');
      var aact   = (acolon >= 0) ? arest.slice(0, acolon) : arest;
      var abody  = (acolon >= 0) ? arest.slice(acolon + 1) : '';
      var ajson = null, aok = false, amsg = '';
      try {
        ajson = JSON.parse(abody);
        aok   = !!(ajson && ajson.ok);
        amsg  = (ajson && (ajson.msg || ajson.error)) || '';
      } catch (e) {}

      /* 列表类请求：成功就落数据重绘；失败把错误显示在列表区域里，
         **绝不再自动重试**（否则失败 → 重试 → 又失败，会把界面打成死循环） */
      if (aact === 'users') {
        ADMIN_ERR = aok ? '' : (amsg || uiT('adminFailed'));
        if (aok) { ADMIN_USERS = (ajson && ajson.users) || []; }
        renderAdminUsers();
      } else if (aact === 'tickets') {
        ADMIN_ERR = aok ? '' : (amsg || uiT('adminFailed'));
        if (aok) { ADMIN_TICKETS = (ajson && ajson.tickets) || []; }
        renderAdminTickets();
      } else if (!aok) {
        toast(amsg || uiT('adminFailed'), 'err', 3500);
      } else {
        if (amsg) { toast(amsg, 'ok', 2500); }
        /* 只有「操作成功」才回头刷新列表 */
        if (aact.indexOf('ticket') === 0) { wv('ADMIN:tickets'); wv('ADMIN:users'); }
        else { wv('ADMIN:users'); }
      }
    }
    else if (msg === 'SUBS_ERR') {
      /* 网站那边订阅拉不到（比如新版 api/auth.php 还没上传）——只提醒一次 */
      if (!window.__subsWarned) {
        window.__subsWarned = 1;
        toast(uiT('subsErr'), 'err', 5000);
      }
    }
  });

  /* ════════ 信息图标 / 收件箱：我提交的游戏 + 管理员的回复（网站 api/game_requests.php）════════ */
  (function(){

  var INBOX = null;          /* null = 还没拉到；[] = 没有任何请求 */
  var INBOX_LAST = -1;       /* 上一次看到的未读数（-1 = 还没拉过） */
  function inboxEl(id){ return document.getElementById(id); }
  function inboxIsOpen(){
    var m = inboxEl('inboxModal');
    return !!m && m.classList.contains('open');
  }
  function inboxSetBadge(n){
    var b = inboxEl('tbInboxBadge');
    if (!b) return;
    if (n > 0) { b.textContent = n > 9 ? '9+' : String(n); b.hidden = false; }
    else { b.hidden = true; }
  }
  function inboxRender(){
    var list = inboxEl('inboxList'), empty = inboxEl('inboxEmpty');
    if (!list || !empty) return;
    if (INBOX === null || !INBOX.length) {
      list.innerHTML = '';
      empty.textContent = uiT(INBOX === null ? 'inboxLoading' : 'inboxEmpty');
      empty.hidden = false;
      return;
    }
    empty.hidden = true;
    var h = '';
    INBOX.forEach(function(r){
      var st = /^(pending|replied|added|declined)$/.test(r.status) ? r.status : 'pending';
      h += '<div class="ib-item">'
        + '<div class="ib-head"><span class="ib-game">' + uiEsc(r.game) + '</span>'
        + '<span class="ib-st ib-st-' + st + '">'
        + uiEsc(uiT('inboxSt' + st.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + st.slice(1))) + '</span></div>'
        + '<div class="ib-meta">' + uiEsc(uiT('inboxYouAsked')) + ' · ' + uiEsc(r.date) + '</div>';
      var rp = r.replies || [];
      for (var i = 0; i < rp.length; i++) {
        h += '<div class="ib-reply' + (rp[i].unread ? ' is-new' : '') + '">'
          + '<div class="ib-reply-meta">' + uiEsc(rp[i].admin) + ' · ' + uiEsc(rp[i].date)
          + (rp[i].unread ? '<b>' + uiEsc(uiT('inboxNew')) + '</b>' : '') + '</div>'
          + '<div class="ib-reply-body">' + uiEsc(rp[i].body) + '</div></div>';
      }
      if (!rp.length) { h += '<div class="ib-wait">' + uiEsc(uiT('inboxWaiting')) + '</div>'; }
      h += '</div>';
    });
    list.innerHTML = h;
  }
  function openInbox(){
    var m = inboxEl('inboxModal');
    if (!m) return;
    m.classList.add('open');
    inboxRender();
    wv('INBOX');
  }
  function closeInbox(){
    var m = inboxEl('inboxModal');
    if (m) m.classList.remove('open');
  }
  function inboxNewRequest(){
    closeInbox();
    openGameReq();
  }
  document.addEventListener('keydown', function(e){
    if (e.key === 'Escape') closeInbox();
  });
  window.chrome.webview.addEventListener('message', function(e){
    var msg = e.data;
    if (typeof msg !== 'string') return;
    if (msg.indexOf('INBOX_RAW:') === 0) {
      var d = null;
      try { d = JSON.parse(msg.slice(10)); } catch (err) {}
      if (!d || !d.ok) return;
      INBOX = d.requests || [];
      var un = d.unread | 0;
      if (inboxIsOpen()) {
        inboxRender();                         /* 这一次还带着「新」标记，让你看清哪些是新回复 */
        if (un > 0) { wv('INBOX_READ'); }      /* 同时告诉网站已读 */
        INBOX_LAST = 0; inboxSetBadge(0);
      } else {
        if (un > Math.max(INBOX_LAST, 0)) { toast(uiT('inboxNewReply'), 'info', 4500); }
        INBOX_LAST = un; inboxSetBadge(un);
      }
    }
    else if (msg === 'REQ_OK') {
      setTimeout(function(){ wv('INBOX'); }, 400);     /* 刚提交的请求马上出现在信息里 */
    }
  });
  setInterval(function(){ if (!document.hidden) { wv('INBOX'); } }, 60000);
  wv('INBOX');


  window.onRenewSuccess = function(){
    wv('GET_LICENSES');
  };

  applyUser();
  updateProfileGroup();       /* 个人资料里的实时组 + 管理员左边 tab */
  applyDPI(USER.dpi || 100);
  renderHero();
  if (typeof gpopBind === 'function') gpopBind();      /* 卡片悬停预览 / 列表右侧详情块 */
  renderGrid();
  renderQuickplay();
  renderDownloads();
  renderGenreBtn();
  renderGenrePop();
  startHeroTimer();
  if (typeof uiInit === 'function') uiInit();
  renderGenreBtn();     /* uiInit 里的 i18n 会重刷按钮文字，这里再校正一次 */
  wv('GET_LICENSES');
  wv('SYNC_SUBS');      /* 登录后拉一次服务端订阅，管理员冻结/删除会立刻反映到本地 */
  wv('MYINFO');         /* 顺手刷新一次「实时组」 */
</script>
